# THE MOVES NOTE — every OM read-move is a measure-exact bijection (with proofs)
*(2026-07-24. The consolidated ledger theorem for the general-n program: the three move
species (development / key-recentering / deg-1 shift), each stratum→box bijection-exact,
and the compositional master theorem. Empirical pins: Cases E, F, F-odd, G, H (cfg 2 and
cfg 1-CORRECTED) — all masses exact, all child windows exactly uniform, at p ∈ {2,3,5}.
Status: REV 1 DEMOLISHED (Codex pass 1: 6 critical, 3 gaps — findings recorded below;
the per-instance landing computations survive, the GENERAL lemma and master theorem do
not). REV 2 PLAN at the end of this file. Do not cite anything in §§1–2 beyond the
concrete instances until rev 2 is accepted.)*

## 0. The three moves, and the two structural facts

Fix a prime p. All objects live on coefficient boxes of monic polynomials over ℤ_p (or
their development-coordinate images); "digit" = coefficientwise p-adic digit; a
**cylinder** is a finite set of digit constraints; nominal volume = the product of the
constrained digits' p-powers.

The OM classifier's read-history reaches every coordinate system it uses by composing
three move species:

- **(M1) development** at a monic key φ (any degree d ≥ 1): f ↦ its φ-adic development
  coefficient vector (a_{ℓ−1}, …, a₀), deg aᵢ < d.
- **(M2) key recentering** (degree-preserving): φ' = φ − p^M·ĉ (deg ĉ < d), and the
  coordinate change = re-development: Θ = M1(φ') ∘ M1(φ)⁻¹.
- **(M3) deg-1-key shift**: x ↦ x + ĉ·p^M on the plain coefficient vector (the deg-1
  instance of M2, listed separately because the residual bookkeeping is the plain Newton
  polygon and the empirical D3/D5 configurations live here).

**Structural Fact A (unitriangularity of division — M1 is always a bijection).** For any
monic φ of degree d and any degree bound, the division f = qφ + r computes the
coefficients of q and r top-down: each is f's corresponding coefficient plus a
ℤ[coeffs(φ)]-combination of STRICTLY EARLIER (higher) coefficients of f (the division
algorithm's elimination step has leading multiplier 1 because φ is monic). Iterating
divisions, the full development map M1(φ) is an affine map of coefficient space whose
linear part is block-lower-unitriangular with unit diagonal. Hence: a bijection of ℤ_p^n
and of (ℤ/p^N)^n for every N, commuting with reduction, and preserving every cylinder
count exactly. *(This is the general-degree D1-marginal; the deg-2, n = 4 instance is
Lemma D1m of `WILD_WAVE8_BLUEPRINT_2026-07-23.md` §1, machine-checked and formalized in
`OM/DevBijection.lean`.)*

**Structural Fact B (bijectivity of every move is free).** M2 and M3 are compositions
M1(φ') ∘ M1(φ)⁻¹ of Fact-A bijections, hence themselves affine unitriangular bijections
commuting with reduction — NO explicit formulas are needed for bijectivity. (The explicit
deg-2 formulas of `JOINT_D1_2026-07-23.md` §1 remain valuable for the Lean layer and for
the ≡ id mod p^M refinement, which for general moves follows from φ' ≡ φ mod p^M:
the two developments agree mod p^M, so Θ ≡ id mod p^M.)

What is NOT free — the mathematical content of each move — is the **landing statement**:
that the move maps its descend stratum ONTO the child cluster box. Sections 1–2 prove a
single lemma from which every landing statement so far probed follows.

## 1. The residual-shift lemma (the one landing computation)

**Setting.** A read at key φ (order 1 in φ; the plain polygon for d = 1, the φ-adic
polygon for d ≥ 2). Fix a side S of integer slope −M and lattice width w, with residual
polynomial R_S(y) ∈ F_Q[y] (Q = p^{d·(residue step)}), deg R_S = w, leading coefficient
= the digit at S's right endpoint (nonzero by the polygon convention). The digits of the
development coordinates ALONG S (at the heights on the line of S) are exactly the
coefficients of R_S.

**Lemma RS.** Let ĉ be a lift of c̃ ∈ F_Qˣ (deg ĉ < d, minimal-support). The move
φ ↦ φ − p^M·ĉ (M2; for d = 1 the shift x ↦ x + ĉp^M, M3, with the sign as the
recentering convention demands — the two conventions differ by the sign of ĉ and both
are covered) transforms the side residual by the variable shift:

  R'_S(y) = R_S(y + c̃)     (in F_Q[y], same side line, same leading coefficient).

*Proof.* Write the development coordinates as the graded pieces along the side: the
height-(line(i)) digit of the i-th coordinate is the y^i-coefficient of R_S. The move
adds p^M·ĉ to the key; substituting φ = φ' + p^Mĉ into the development Σ aᵢφ^i and
re-expanding by the binomial theorem, the term aᵢφ^i contributes to the (j ≤ i)-th slots
with coefficient C(i,j)·aᵢ·(p^Mĉ)^{i−j} plus reduction corrections. At the residue level
of the side line, the reduction corrections vanish (each reduction by the monic φ'
replaces a top coefficient by strictly-lower-height data: it moves mass strictly above
the side line — the same "invisible below the line" accounting as `JOINT_D1` Lemma J1b,
now stated once and for all: a single division step changes coordinates at height h only
through inputs of height ≥ h, with equality only via the unit leading coefficient), and
the digit of the j-th slot on the line becomes Σ_{i ≥ j} C(i,j)·(digit of aᵢ on the
line)·c̃^{i−j} — which is precisely the y^j-coefficient of R_S(y + c̃). For d = 1 the
same computation is the literal Taylor expansion of the coefficient vector under
x ↦ x + ĉp^M restricted to the side line. ∎

**Corollary RS-landing (every descend lands in its child box, with deterministic
transport).** Suppose the stratum imposes: the side-S digit data equals a residual of
the form R_S(y) = (y − c̃)^μ · h(y) with h(c̃) ≠ 0 (a descend at center c̃ of
multiplicity μ; the probed cases have μ = 2), plus exactness of the endpoint digits.
Then after the move:

  R'_S(y) = y^μ · h(y + c̃),  h(c̃) ≠ 0,

i.e. (i) the child's side digits vanish in the slots y^0, …, y^{μ−1} — the child
valuations rise into the multiplicity-μ cluster box exactly as far as the descend
multiplicity forces and no further (the y^μ-slot digit is h(c̃) ≠ 0: the child VERTEX is
pinned); (ii) the co-factor data (the flank sides above/right of S, and h's higher
coefficients) is transported DETERMINISTICALLY — h(y + c̃)'s coefficients are fixed
F_Q-polynomials in the parent digits, and everything strictly off the side line is
untouched at its height (the "invisible" accounting of the proof). ∎

**Instances, checked against the ledger data (all committed gates):**
- **J1b** (deg-2 key, μ = 2, w = 2, h = leading unit): R = (y − c̃)² ⟹ R' = y²: the
  child digits at heights M, 2M vanish — the `JOINT_D1` computation, re-derived.
- **J2-landing** (Case G: d = 1 → the new deg-2 key; the landing is Fact-A at
  φ₂ = x² − pĉ composed with the side reading): box (1,2,2,3), verified symbolically
  (v(b₁.c): digit₁(a₂) + 2c̃ = −2c̃ + 2c̃ = 0; v(b₀.c): c̃² − 2c̃² + c̃² = 0) and
  empirically (minima (1,2,2,3) at p = 2, 3; stratum mass p⁻⁸ = box volume p⁻⁸).
- **D5-step** (Case H cfg 1-CORRECTED: μ = 2 side (0,5)–(2,1) with flank (2,1)–(3,0)):
  R = u(y − c̃)² on the side; R' = u·y²: digit₃(a₁') = −2c̃u + 2c̃u = 0 (v ≥ 4),
  digit₅(a₀') = c̃²u − 2c̃²u + c̃²u = 0 (v ≥ 6), and the flank vertex digit
  digit₁(a₂') = u transported as a point mass — exactly the census (25 strata, 3 primes;
  child (1, ≥4, ≥6); H3 point-mass = u).
- **D3-step** (Case H cfg 2: the colinear/partial-side case — S is the WHOLE side
  (0,3)–(3,0) with R₃ = (y−c̃)²(y−r), h(y) = y − r, h(c̃) = c̃ − r ≠ 0): R₃' =
  y²(y + c̃ − r): digit₃(a₀') = R₃(c̃) = 0, digit₂(a₁') = R₃′(c̃) = 0, child vertex digit
  = h(c̃) = c̃ − r ≠ 0 — matching the census child profile (1, ≥3, ≥4) with v(a₂') = 1
  exact. NOTE: the "partial-side" case is thereby NOT a separate species — it is the
  μ < w case of RS-landing, with the co-factor root(s) r becoming the transported flank.

**Volume equalities (closing each bijection).** In every instance the stratum's pinned
digit count equals the child box's (+ transported digits) pinned count: J1 (3M in/3M
out, `JOINT_D1` §3), J2 (8/8), D5 (12/12), D3 (9/9) — the general statement: the descend
stratum pins the side digits (w + endpoint bookkeeping) and the child box + transported
vertex/flank digits pin the images of the same slots; RS is a bijection on the slot data
(y ↦ y + c̃ is invertible), so the counts agree configuration-by-configuration. Combined
with Fact-B injectivity and RS-landing inclusion: **each move restricts to a bijection
from its descend stratum onto its child box-with-transported-digits.** (The finite-level
threshold is the largest constrained height + 1, as in `JOINT_D1` §3.)

## 2. Theorem MOVES (the compositional master ledger)

Fix a read-history shape and values: a finite rooted tree of reads, each node a move
(M1/M2/M3) with its stratum data (side, center, multiplicity, transported flank values),
and a leaf condition = any subset C of the final coordinates' box. Then the composite
coordinate change Ψ (the composition of the per-node moves) restricts to a bijection
from the joint stratum onto the final box, and

  #{ f mod p^N in the joint stratum with leaf condition C } = #C

for every N above the total constrained height. Consequently the joint count is the
product of the per-read nominal volumes times #C — the full development-digit ledger.

*Proof.* Exactly the induction of `JOINT_D1_2026-07-23.md` §4 (accepted), with "J1-step"
replaced by "the §1 move bijection" at every node: each successive stratum is contained
in the previous child box (its conditions constrain only fresh heights — RS-landing's
accounting), the restriction of a bijection to the preimage of a subset is a bijection,
and compositions of affine unitriangular reduction-compatible bijections are such. ∎

## 3. Honest perimeter (what §§1–2 do and do not establish)

ESTABLISHED (general n, general p): Facts A/B; Lemma RS + RS-landing for order-1 reads
at keys of ANY degree with INTEGER-slope descend sides of any multiplicity μ and any
flank/co-factor; the volume equalities; Theorem MOVES for histories composed of such
reads. This subsumes: all of n ≤ 2 (retro-verifies the formalized tower), the class-3
deg-2-key towers (JOINT-D1), the order-increment landing (J2), D3 and D5. **D1, D3, D5
of the parent note are hereby CLOSED at order 1**; D4's premise (the joint form) is
supplied by Theorem MOVES — its (R1)/(R2) reduction still needs its explicit
restatement (next note).

STILL OPEN, named:
1. **Fractional-slope descends (e ≥ 2 with μ ≥ 2)**: Case G's stratum is e = 2 — but its
   landing was proved via Fact-A at the NEW key, not via Lemma RS (whose proof as written
   is for integer-slope sides where the recentering keeps the key degree). The general
   e ≥ 2 descend (key degree multiplies by e·f_ψ) needs the RS analogue in the
   v_r-weighted graded algebra — the ORDER-r ≥ 2 recursion. What Case G establishes: the
   r = 2 ENTRY move is bijection-exact; what remains: the reads INSIDE order r ≥ 2 (the
   φ_r-adic polygons with v_r-weighted heights) — expected to be Facts A/B + an RS in the
   weighted grading, same proof shape; needs its own note with a Case-G2 continuation
   gate (a depth-2 order-2 census) BEFORE being trusted.
2. **deg ψ ≥ 2 residual descends** (the other order-increment species: unramified-type
   key growth): same status — entry move = Fact A at the new key (bijective for free);
   landing bounds to be written via the weighted RS; gate first.
3. **The D4 reduction restatement** and **the assembly** (per-shape universal-polynomial
   counts × Theorem MOVES × the parent note §4 resummations × exhaustion) — the final
   two notes of the campaign.

2. CRITICAL: RS's proof asserts reduction corrections vanish at the side line — FALSE;
   in J1b they contribute exactly there and are absorbed by the F_Q product. The correct
   route is the honest graded argument: the v-filtration along the side, gr ≅ F_Q[y],

   and only the DIGITS THE LANDING/VOLUME ARGUMENT USES need pinning.
5. General volume equality gestured, not proved.
6. CRITICAL: the J2/Case-G landing is NOT an instance of Fact A or RS as claimed — it is

7. CRITICAL: Theorem MOVES assumes fresh-heights compatibility per step instead of

8. CRITICAL: #C vs product-formula phrasing inconsistent.
9. CRITICAL: the n = 2 subsumption is false as stated — recenter-RESCALE

## REV 2 — §A: the graded proof of RS (replaces §1's demolished proof)

*(2026-07-24. STATUS: ACCEPTED — four adversarial passes: pass 1 = rev-1 demolition
(6 crit); rev 2 written via the side filtration; pass 2 = 0 crit/2 gaps (localization,
onto-direction) both repaired; pass 3 confirmed repairs + 1 critical (landed-side width
wording) fixed; pass 4 = CLEAN. Gated by the SEALED Case-I census (μ = 3, flanked and
unflanked, p ∈ {2,5}) whose pre-registered predictions all held. §A gives: the side
filtration, gr ≅ F_Q[π, y], Ore multiplicativity via the Laurent normal form, Lemma RS
(R' (z) = R(z + c̃), filtrations equal), and RS-landing as a two-sided pointwise
bijection stratum → landing cylinder at ANY key degree, multiplicity, and flank —
integer slopes, order-1 reads.)*

### A.1 The side filtration and its graded ring

Fix the key φ, monic of degree d ≥ 1 over ℤ_p, φ̄ irreducible over F_p; F_Q :=
F_p[x]/(φ̄). Fix an integer slope parameter M ≥ 1. On A := ℤ_p[x] define, for f with
φ-adic development f = Σᵢ aᵢφ^i (deg aᵢ < d — the development exists and is unique for
every f, all degrees, by monic division):

  w(f) := min_i ( v(aᵢ) + i·M ),   v = the Gauss valuation (min coefficient v_p).

Set A_{≥γ} := {f : w(f) ≥ γ} (γ ∈ ℤ), and gr A := ⊕_γ A_{≥γ}/A_{>γ}.

**Lemma A1 (the graded ring).** gr A ≅ F_Q[π, y] as graded F_Q-algebras, where π := the
class of p (degree 1), y := the class of φ (degree M), and the degree-0 part is F_Q via
the reduction ρ : (deg < d, v ≥ 0) → F_Q, ρ = (mod (p, φ)).

*Proof.* (i) A_{≥γ}·A_{≥δ} ⊆ A_{≥γ+δ}: for a product term aᵢφ^i·bⱼφ^j = (aᵢbⱼ)φ^{i+j},
re-develop by dividing aᵢbⱼ = qφ + r (deg r < d): the term becomes q·φ^{i+j+1} +
r·φ^{i+j}, with v(q), v(r) ≥ v(aᵢ) + v(bⱼ) (monic division preserves integrality and
Gauss lower bounds: each elimination subtracts a φ-multiple by an already-bounded
coefficient), so the r-term has w ≥ (v(aᵢ)+v(bⱼ)) + (i+j)M and the q-term is STRICTLY
deeper by M. Iteration terminates (degree drops).
(ii) The map F_Q[π, y] → gr A (π, y as above; F_Q = the degree-0 classes) is a graded
ring homomorphism: degree-0 multiplicativity because ρ is a RING hom (reduction mod the
ideal (p, φ)); the q-correction terms of (i) land in strictly higher filtration and so
vanish in the graded product. THIS is the honest form of rev-1's demolished "corrections
vanish": they vanish in the graded quotient BY CONSTRUCTION, not as elements.
(iii) Surjectivity: the γ-piece is spanned by classes of p^{γ−iM}·u·φ^i, images of
π^{γ−iM}ū y^i.
(iv) Injectivity by dimension count: dim_{F_Q}(A_{≥γ}/A_{>γ}) = #{i ≥ 0 : iM ≤ γ} (one
F_Q-slot per admissible i: the height-(γ−iM) digit of aᵢ) = the number of degree-γ
monomials π^a y^i. ∎

**Corollary A2 (Ore).** w(fg) = w(f) + w(g), and the side residual R_S(f)(z) :=
Σ_{i : v(aᵢ)+iM = w(f)} (height-(w(f)−iM) digit of aᵢ)·z^i ∈ F_Q[z] is multiplicative.
*Proof:* F_Q[π, y] is a domain, so w(fg) = w(f) + w(g) ([f][g] = [fg] ≠ 0). For the
residual: localize — gr A ≅ F_Q[π, y] ↪ F_Q[π, π⁻¹, y] =: L, injective since gr A is a
domain (π is a nonzerodivisor). In L every nonzero homogeneous element of degree γ writes
UNIQUELY as π^γ·P(y·π^{−M}) with P ∈ F_Q[z], P ≠ 0 (expand in the L-basis π^a y^i and
factor π^γ out; uniqueness from the monomial basis of L (L ≅ F_Q[π^{±1}, z], z = y·π^{−M}, with
L_γ = π^γ·F_Q[z]). Under this normal form, [f] = π^{w(f)}·R_S(f)(y·π^{−M}) with R_S as
defined (slot-by-slot comparison), and multiplication of normal forms multiplies the
P-parts: R_S(fg) = R_S(f)·R_S(g). [Repair of the pass-2 gap: the localization is now
declared, the extraction is the L-normal form, and multiplicativity is the domain
product in L.] ∎

### A.2 Lemma RS, proved

**Lemma RS (rev 2).** ĉ (deg < d) a lift of c̃ ∈ F_Qˣ; φ' := φ − p^M·ĉ. Then the primed
filtration equals the unprimed one, and R'_S(f)(z) = R_S(f)(z + c̃) for every f.

*Proof.* φ' is monic degree d with φ̄' = φ̄, so A.1 applies verbatim to φ'. In the
UNPRIMED gr A: [φ'] = [φ] − [p^Mĉ] = y − c̃π^M =: y' (both terms have w = M). The
assignment y = y' + c̃π^M is a graded F_Q-algebra automorphism of F_Q[π, y] = F_Q[π, y'].
Filtration equality: each primed development term a'ᵢφ'^i has unprimed w ≥ v(a'ᵢ) + iM
(A.1(i) with w(φ') = M), so w ≥ w'; symmetric via φ = φ' + p^Mĉ; hence w' = w. The
primed residual is [f] expressed in (π, y')-coordinates with z' = y'·π^{−M}; substituting
z = z' + c̃ gives R'_S(z') = R_S(z' + c̃). ∎

**Corollary RS-landing (rev 2 — precise, off-line claims withdrawn).** On the stratum
R_S(f) = (z − c̃)^μ·h(z), h(c̃) ≠ 0 (with the side's endpoint exactness): after the move,
R'_S(f) = z^μ·h(z + c̃) — the primed side-line digits vanish at slots i < μ, the slot μ
digit is h(c̃) ≠ 0 (the pinned child vertex), the slots i > μ carry h(z + c̃)'s
coefficients: deterministic F_Q-polynomials of the parent side digits. The LANDING
CYLINDER is defined as exactly this primed side-digit pattern (including the endpoint
data: RS preserves the degree and leading coefficient of the residual, so the primed
side has the SAME slope and RIGHT endpoint; its LEFT endpoint moves to μ — the pinned
vertex h(c̃) ≠ 0 — and its width is reduced to (right endpoint) − μ. [Corrected at pass
3: the earlier "same width" was internally inconsistent with the μ vanishings.]).
BIJECTIVITY, both directions pointwise [repair of the pass-2 gap — no counting needed]:
the move is a bijection of the whole coefficient space (Fact B). Forward: if f satisfies
the stratum conditions (R_S(f) = (z−c̃)^μ h(z)), then by RS its image satisfies the
landing conditions (R'_S = z^μ h(z+c̃)). Backward: the INVERSE move φ = φ' + p^M·ĉ is a
recentering of the same species (by −c̃ in the primed frame), and RS applied to IT gives
R_S(g)(z) = R'_S(g)(z − c̃) for every g; so if g satisfies the landing conditions,
R_S(g) = (z − c̃)^μ·h(z) — exactly the stratum conditions. Mutual containment under a
global bijection gives Θ(stratum) = landing cylinder exactly. Both loci are defined by
side-slot conditions only; all other coordinates are unrestricted on both sides and
carried bijectively. Rev-1's "off-line digits untouched at their heights" remains
WITHDRAWN — it is not needed anywhere. ∎

*(Empirical: the §1 instance list + the SEALED Case-I gate — μ = 3 flankless p = 2 full
stratum, μ = 3 flanked p = 5 twelve strata — child minima, vertex point-mass c̃ − r, and
window uniformity all exactly as this corollary predicts.)*

### A.3 Still open after §A (honest)

Fractional slopes (e ≥ 2) and order-r ≥ 2 interior reads: the A.1 construction with
weight group (1/e)ℤ resp. the level-(r−1) valuation replacing Gauss v — same four steps,

## REV 2 — §B: fractional slopes, the order-increment, and the level-r induction

*(2026-07-24. DRAFT pending: reconciliation with the three parallel probes (Case-G2
exploratory census; fractional-Ore decorrelated check; D4 extraction) and Codex passes.
§B1–§B3 below are self-contained proofs in the §A framework.)*

### B1. The fractional-slope graded ring and Ore-with-twist

Setup as §A (key φ monic deg d, φ̄ irreducible, F_Q), now with slope −h/e, gcd(h,e) = 1,
h, e ≥ 1. INTEGER-SCALE weight: w(Σ aᵢφ^i) := min_i (e·v(aᵢ) + i·h). Filtration and gr as
in §A.

**Lemma B1.1.** gr A ≅ F_Q[π, y] with deg π = e, deg y = h (π = [p], y = [φ]); w is a
valuation. *Proof:* the §A.1 four steps verbatim: (i) the division correction q·φ^{i+j+1}
is deeper by h; (ii) degree-0 slots need ev + ih = 0 ⟹ (v, i) = (0, 0) ⟹ the degree-0
part is F_Q via the reduction hom mod (p, φ); (iii) γ-slots {(v, i) : ev + ih = γ} ↔
degree-γ monomials π^v y^i; (iv) the same dimension count. Domain ⟹ multiplicativity. ∎

**Normal form and the twist.** In L := F_Q[π^{±1}, y]: for homogeneous degree γ, the
slots satisfy i ≡ i₀ (mod e) with i₀ := the least non-negative solution of ih ≡ γ
(mod e) (h invertible mod e). With z := y^e·π^{−h} (degree 0; L₀-structure F_Q[z] on each
i₀-class):

  [f] = π^{v₀}·y^{i₀}·R(f)(z),  R(f) ∈ F_Q[z],  (v₀ = (γ − i₀h)/e).

**Corollary B1.2 (Ore, no twist — support-anchored form).** All min-achieving slots of
f are congruent mod e (two achieving slots i, i′ satisfy e(v−v′) = (i′−i)h, and
gcd(h,e) = 1 forces e | i′−i), so anchoring at i₀(f) := the LEAST achieving slot gives a
well-defined R(f)(z) := Σ (leading digits)·z^{(i−i₀)/e} ∈ F_Q[z] with R(0) ≠ 0. Then
w(fg) = w(f) + w(g), i₀(fg) = i₀(f) + i₀(g), and R(fg) = R(f)·R(g) EXACTLY — no twist.
*Proof:* in the domain gr, the product of the two normal forms π^{v}y^{i₀}R has anchor
the sum of anchors (leading terms multiply, R(0)·R′(0) ≠ 0) and R-parts multiply by the
monomial basis of L. ∎ *(Decorrelated check PASSED: 12000/12000 random products across
p ∈ {2,3}, slopes −1/2, −2/3, −3/2 — w additive, anchors additive, R multiplicative, the
earlier-conjectured z-shift NEVER occurs. A [0,e)-canonical anchor would reintroduce
explicit z-powers; the support anchor is the elegant convention and is adopted.)*

**Lemma B1.3 (K1 — development compatibility at a new key; the keystone).** Let ψ ∈
F_Q[z] be monic irreducible with ψ ≠ z, and let Φ be the standard lift:

  Φ := Σ_{k=0}^{degψ} ψ̂_k · p^{h(degψ−k)} · φ^{ek}   (ψ̂_k a fixed lift of the
  z^k-coefficient; Φ monic of degree d·e·degψ; every φ-slot of Φ has weight
  e·h·degψ, so w(Φ) = e·h·degψ and [Φ] = π^{h·degψ}·ψ(z), i₀(Φ) = 0).

Then for every f, the Φ-development f = Σ_j B_j Φ^j satisfies

  w(f) = min_j ( w(B_j) + j·w(Φ) ).

*Proof.* ≥ is submultiplicativity + ultrametric. For ≤ (no total cancellation): suppose
w(f) > m := the min. In gr at degree m: Σ_{j ∈ J} [B_j]·[Φ]^j = 0 (J the minimizing
set). Each [B_j] = π^{v_j} y^{i_j} R_j(z) with i_j ∈ [0, e) and — since
deg B_j < deg Φ forces the φ-slots of B_j below e·degψ — deg_z R_j < degψ, so ψ ∤ R_j
(R_j ≠ 0). Also [Φ]^j = π^{jh·degψ}ψ(z)^j. Group the sum by the classes i_j mod e: the
monomial basis of L makes distinct classes independent, so each class sums to zero
separately; within a class, the terms R_j·ψ^j have DISTINCT ψ-adic orders (exactly j,
since ψ ∤ R_j), and distinct ψ-orders cannot cancel in F_Q[z][z⁻¹-free]. Contradiction. ∎

**Corollary B1.4 (the order-increment landing, general).** On the descend stratum
R(f) = ψ^μ·h̃ with ψ ∤ h̃ (any μ ≥ 2, any co-factor; the fractional-slope μ ≥ 2 descends
and the deg ψ ≥ 2 integer-slope descends are exactly the cases e·degψ > 1): writing the
ψ-adic development R(f) = Σ_j R_j·ψ^j (deg R_j < degψ; R_j = 0 for j < μ; R_μ =
h̃ mod ψ ≠ 0), the Φ-development of f satisfies:

  w(B_j) > w(f) − j·w(Φ)  for j < μ   (the landing box), and
  w(B_μ) = w(f) − μ·w(Φ) with leading residual R_μ ≠ 0 (the pinned vertex);
  the higher slots carry R_j (j > μ): deterministic transport.

Two-sided: the map f ↦ (B_j) is a bijection (Fact A at Φ); forward containment is the
display; backward, given (B_j) in the landing cylinder, f := ΣB_jΦ^j has, by (K1) and
the graded identity, R(f) = Σ R_j ψ^j = ψ^μ·(the transported h̃) — the stratum
conditions. Hence stratum ⟶ landing cylinder is a bijection. ∎

*(Instance check: Case G is e = 2, h = 1, ψ = z − 1 (after the F₂-normalization), μ = 2,
degψ = 1, Φ = φ² − p·ĉ, w(Φ) = 2; on the stratum w(f) = 4 (integer scale), so the box is
w(B₁) ≥ 3, w(B₀) ≥ 5 — decoded to coordinates: v(B₁.x) ≥ 1, v(B₁.c) ≥ 2, v(B₀.x) ≥ 2,
v(B₀.c) ≥ 3 — EXACTLY the empirical Case-G box (1,2,2,3) at both primes.)*

### B2. The level-r induction (augmented weights, self-contained)

**The level datum.** A *level-r datum* consists of: a coefficient space C_r (the deg <
deg Φ_r polynomials), a weight w_{r−1} on A (multiplicative, with graded normal form
π_{r−1}-power × Y_{r−1}-power × residual over a FIELD F_{Q_{r−1}}, in the §A/§B1 form),
and a key Φ_r (monic; [Φ_r] = (π-monomial)·ψ_{r−1}(z_{r−1}) with ψ_{r−1} irreducible ≠
z_{r−1}) satisfying (K1) for w_{r−1}. The base r = 1 datum is §A/§B1 (w₀ = the Gauss
valuation, F_{Q₀} = F_Q, Φ₁ = φ).

**Proposition B2.1 (the induction step).** Given a level-r datum and a level-r slope
−h_r/e_r (in the value group of w_{r−1}; lowest terms), define

  w_r(Σ_j B_j Φ_r^j) := min_j ( e_r·w_{r−1}(B_j) + j·h_r ).

Then the §A.1/§B1.1 four-step construction applies verbatim, with inputs supplied as
follows: (i) submultiplicativity — products of coefficients reduce mod Φ_r by division,
and (K1) at level r bounds both the quotient and remainder weights (this is exactly what
(K1) provides: the development of B·B′ achieves the minimum, so w_{r−1}(remainder) ≥
w_{r−1}(BB′) and w_{r−1}(quotient) ≥ w_{r−1}(BB′) − w_{r−1}(Φ_r)); (ii) the degree-0
part is the NEXT residue field F_{Q_r} := F_{Q_{r−1}}[z_{r−1}]/(ψ_{r−1}) via the
composite reduction (reduce at level r−1, then mod ψ_{r−1}) — a ring homomorphism, and
a FIELD since ψ_{r−1} is irreducible; (iii)/(iv) span and dimension with the slot
bookkeeping of B1 (the value group of w_r is generated accordingly; i₀-classes mod e_r).
Hence: gr_{w_r} has the same normal form over F_{Q_r}, w_r is multiplicative (Ore at
level r, with the B1.2 twist), and:
- the level-r SAME-DEGREE recentering Φ_r′ = Φ_r − t, with t any element of C_r of
  w_{r−1}-weight equal to w_r(Φ_r)-appropriate value and residual class c̃_r, satisfies
  Lemma RS verbatim in gr_{w_r} (the §A.2 proof is word-for-word: [Φ_r′] = Y_r −
  c̃_r·(π-monomial), the graded automorphism, filtration equality by the two-sided
  development bound, residual shift z_r ↦ z_r + c̃_r) and the §A landing (two-sided, by
  the same symmetric inverse-move argument);
- the level-r ORDER-INCREMENT to a new key Φ_{r+1} (the standard lift of an irreducible
  ψ_r ≠ z_r) satisfies (K1) at level r by the SAME proof as B1.3 (the normal-form basis
  + distinct ψ_r-orders), yielding the B1.4 landing and producing the level-(r+1) datum.

*Proof.* Each clause is the named §A/§B1 argument re-run with (v, F_Q, φ) replaced by
(w_{r−1}, F_{Q_{r−1}}, Φ_r); the only inputs the arguments consumed were
multiplicativity of the base weight, the graded normal form over a field, monic
division, and (K1) — all supplied by the datum, and all re-established for level r + 1
by the construction itself. Induction closes. ∎

**Remark (elegance/summary).** The entire move apparatus is thereby generated by TWO
proofs: the four-step graded construction (§A.1/B1.1/B2.1) and the normal-form
cancellation lemma (K1); every landing — same-degree recentering at any level (RS) and
order-increment at any level (B1.4) — is a corollary. The deg-1 rescale M4 (parent
Lemma 2.3) remains the one separately-proved species (it changes the ambient box, not
just the key).

### B3. Status and reconciliation

**Case-G2 reconciliation (2026-07-24): the exploratory census MATCHES B2.1.** On the
Case-G stratum (p = 2, key Φ = x²−2, 2²⁴ members enumerated, closed-form development
verified against genuine division at 70k+ points): (i) the level-2 ladder is exactly
geometric in w₁(B₀) with the parity-alternating leaf/descend structure; (ii) the probed
sub-stratum {w₁(B₀) = 6 exact, w₁(B₁) ≥ 4} IS a level-2 descend (residual (z₂+1)² over
F_{Q₂} = F₂; the leading digits are identically 1 at p = 2 as they must be); (iii) BOTH
weight-3 lifts t ∈ {2x, 2x+4} of the center produce the RS-predicted vanishing — minima
rise strictly past the side value (w₁(B₀′) ≥ 7, w₁(B₁′) ≥ 4) — with cell-for-cell
IDENTICAL tables: the lift-independence RS demands; (iv) the wrong-weight candidate
t = 4 (w₁ = 4 ≠ λ₂ = 3) produces NO rise (negative control); (v) the odd-W₀ sub-stratum
(a level-2 leaf) is inert under all candidates (leaf control). One artifact recorded:
the free-digit cap distorts only the ≥17 tail cells (the bulk factorizes exactly).

Still open in §B:
- The value-group bookkeeping in B2.1(iii)/(iv) is stated, not spelled out (the slot
  count at level r with e_r over the level-(r−1) group); to be expanded if the Codex
  pass demands.
- The composition theorem (§C) and D4/assembly remain.

## §B PASS-1 RECORD (2026-07-24: 3 critical, 6 gaps) and the B2 repair plan

VERBATIM FINDINGS (Codex, fresh context):

1. **CRITICAL ERROR — B2.1 does not establish submultiplicativity for the augmented weight.**

The offending claim is:

> “products of coefficients reduce mod \(\Phi_r\) by division, and (K1) at level \(r\) bounds both the quotient and remainder weights”

Write \(BB'=Q\Phi_r+R\). K1 gives
\[
w_{r-1}(Q)\ge w_{r-1}(BB')-w_{r-1}(\Phi_r).
\]
Consequently, the quotient correction in the product has augmented weight at least
\[
e_r w_{r-1}(BB')+(i+j)h_r+
\bigl(h_r-e_r w_{r-1}(\Phi_r)\bigr).
\]
Nothing in the definition of a level-\(r\) slope guarantees that the final parenthesis is nonnegative, let alone positive as required for the correction to disappear in the graded product. Thus K1 alone does not supply the claimed four-step construction. As stated, B2.1 can assign a quotient correction smaller weight than the original product term, so even submultiplicativity can fail.

2. **CRITICAL ERROR — the asserted “degree-0 part” \(F_{Q_r}\) is not the degree-0 part of the filtration on \(A=\mathbf Z_p[x]\).**

The problematic passage is:

> “the degree-0 part is the NEXT residue field  
> \(F_{Q_r}:=F_{Q_{r-1}}[z_{r-1}]/(\psi_{r-1})\) via the composite reduction”

Already after B1, \(z=y^e\pi^{-h}\) exists only after localizing by \(\pi\). It is not an element of \(\operatorname{gr}A\). For the nonnegative filtration on \(\mathbf Z_p[x]\), augmented weight zero forces the \(\Phi_r\)-index to be zero and the coefficient to have previous weight zero. It does not create all the \(z_{r-1}\)-classes modulo \(\psi_{r-1}\).

Hence the larger field \(F_{Q_r}\) can occur in an appropriately normalized/localized residue construction, but it is not literally the degree-zero piece used in the §A four-step proof. The note conflates the localized normal-form coefficient field with \((\operatorname{gr}_{w_r}A)_0\). The asserted polynomial graded-ring structure over \(F_{Q_r}\), and therefore the claimed Ore induction, do not follow.

3. **CRITICAL ERROR — “same-degree recentering at any level” is not a word-for-word RS argument at fractional ramification index.**

B2.1 claims:

> “the level-\(r\) SAME-DEGREE recentering … satisfies Lemma RS verbatim … residual shift \(z_r\mapsto z_r+\widetilde c_r\)”

At fractional index \(e_r>1\), the residual variable has the form
\[
z_r=Y_r^{e_r}\Pi^{-h_r}.
\]
A translation \(Y_r'=Y_r-T\) transforms it through
\[
(Y_r'+T)^{e_r}\Pi^{-h_r},
\]
which is not generally \(z_r'+\widetilde c_r\). The §A substitution argument applies directly to \(e_r=1\); it is not “word-for-word” for arbitrary \(e_r\).

There is also no precise existence statement for \(t\). The phrase

> “\(w_{r-1}\)-weight equal to \(w_r(\Phi_r)\)-appropriate value”

does not state the equality needed to make \(\Phi_r\) and \(t\) homogeneous of the same augmented weight. Thus the theorem asserts a general RS species for which neither the graded translation nor even the admissible center is defined.

4. **JUSTIFICATION GAP — K1 suppresses the common homogeneous prefactor needed inside each \(i_j\)-class.**

The proof says:

> “Group the sum by the classes \(i_j\bmod e\) … within a class, the terms \(R_j\psi^j\) have DISTINCT \(\psi\)-adic orders”

Within one class it is necessary to show that the factors
\[
\pi^{v_j+jh\deg\psi}y^{i_j}
\]
are identical before reducing the equation to
\[
\sum_j R_j(z)\psi(z)^j=0
\]
in \(F_Q[z]\). This follows from all terms being homogeneous of the same total degree, but it is not stated. The monomial-basis sentence only proves independence of different classes; it does not itself perform the within-class reduction.

The requested degree estimate is otherwise correct: from
\[
\deg_\phi B_j\le e\deg\psi-1
\]
and \(i_j\in[0,e)\),
\[
\deg_zR_j\le
\left\lfloor\frac{e\deg\psi-1-i_j}{e}\right\rfloor<\deg\psi.
\]
Also, \(\psi\ne z\) is not used in the K1 cancellation proof itself.

5. **JUSTIFICATION GAP — B1.4 does not prove the equivalence between minimizing \(\Phi\)-slots and nonzero \(\psi\)-adic digits.**

The corollary moves immediately from K1 to

> “\(R_j=0\) for \(j<\mu\)”  
> \(\Longrightarrow\)  
> “\(w(B_j)>w(f)-j\,w(\Phi)\)”

K1 supplies only the minimum equality. The missing argument must identify the graded sum of the minimizing \(B_j\Phi^j\) with the unique \(\psi\)-adic development of the support-anchored \(R(f)\).

This is especially nontrivial under the adopted support-anchor convention. If the minimizing \(B_j\) have anchors differing by multiples of \(e\), then after factoring the least global anchor their digits acquire factors
\[
z^{(i_0(B_j)-i_0(f))/e}.
\]
Thus the \(\psi\)-adic digit \(R_j\) need not be the raw support-anchored residual of \(B_j\). The note neither records these factors nor proves:

\[
w(B_j)+jw(\Phi)=w(f)
\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad
R_j\ne0.
\]

The same omission affects “the higher slots carry \(R_j\)” and the backward landing argument. The landing cylinder is also not formally stated with the finite-support/degree restrictions inherent in the \(\Phi\)-development.

6. **JUSTIFICATION GAP — the base level does not actually supply the K1 required by its own definition.**

The datum requires:

> “a key \(\Phi_r\) … satisfying (K1) for \(w_{r-1}\)”

but then declares:

> “The base \(r=1\) datum is §A/§B1 (\(w_0\) = the Gauss valuation … \(\Phi_1=\phi\)).”

Neither §A nor B1 proves
\[
v_{\mathrm G}(f)=\min_j v_{\mathrm G}(B_j)
\qquad
(f=\sum_jB_j\phi^j),
\]
which is the base K1 because \(v_{\mathrm G}(\phi)=0\). The equality is plausible from irreducibility of \(\bar\phi\) and uniqueness of the reduced \(\bar\phi\)-development, but it is an additional base lemma, not a result already established by §A/B1.

7. **JUSTIFICATION GAP — the general level slot count and generalized K1 degree bound are explicitly undeveloped.**

B2.1 says:

> “(iii)/(iv) span and dimension with the slot bookkeeping of B1”

while B3 admits:

> “The value-group bookkeeping in B2.1(iii)/(iv) is stated, not spelled out”

This is not cosmetic. At higher level one must account simultaneously for the previous value group, the \(e_r\)-congruence classes, normalized residual coefficients, boundary effects from working in \(\mathbf Z_p[x]\), and the degree-\(<\deg\Phi_{r+1}\) bound needed to obtain \(\deg R_j<\deg\psi_r\). Those facts are precisely what injectivity, the field coefficient structure, and the generalized K1 proof consume. As written, the induction step delegates its central dimension and basis assertions to unspecified bookkeeping.

8. **JUSTIFICATION GAP — B3’s empirical reconciliation is broader than the evidence it reports.**

The heading says:

> “the exploratory census MATCHES B2.1”

The reported census verifies particular level-2 cells, correct-weight lifts, a wrong-weight control, and leaf behavior. It does not test the general residue-field identification, arbitrary \(e_r\), generalized slot count, or the K1 induction. Moreover, the supplied evidence mentions a fresh sealed \(\lambda_2=4\) run, while B3 narrates only the \(\lambda_2=3\) configuration. The empirical data are consistent with the tested local predictions, but they do not reconcile the general proposition as claimed.

9. **JUSTIFICATION GAP — the “two proofs generate everything” economy claim omits substantive unproved inputs.**

The summary states:

> “The entire move apparatus is thereby generated by TWO proofs”

But the induction additionally depends on:

- the base Gauss/first-key K1;
- the higher residue-field construction;
- the higher-level slot and dimension theorem;
- compatibility between support anchors and \(\psi\)-adic digits;
- restrictions under which same-degree RS is actually a translation.

B1.1 is a useful concise fractional analogue of §A rather than harmful redundancy, and B1.2’s support anchoring is consistent with the 12,000-product experiment. The overcompression occurs in B2.1: it treats new higher-level structure as though it were merely a renaming of §A.

The specific B1.1 checks pass: \(ev+ih=0\) forces \(v=i=0\) because \(e,h\ge1\) and \(v,i\ge0\); and degrees such as \(\gamma=1\) for \((e,h)=(2,3)\) have zero slots and zero polynomial monomials on both sides. B1.2’s congruence, nonzero anchor digit, and anchor additivity are also valid once the domain result from B1.1 is granted.



REPAIR PLAN (the remaining hard kernel, for the next working session):
(a) B1 repairs (gaps 4, 5): K1's per-class argument must carry the common homogeneous
    (π, y)-prefactor explicitly; B1.4 must prove the iff (minimizing slots ↔ nonzero
    ψ-digits) via the graded-identity uniqueness argument in both directions. Both are
    local rewrites of proofs whose structure survived.
(b) B2 REWRITE (criticals 1–3, gaps 6, 7): the level-r theory must be built honestly:
    (i) define the level-r coefficient category and its weight with the correct residue
    structure (the level-r residue field is the degree-0 part of the graded ring OF THE
    COEFFICIENT OBJECTS' normal forms, i.e. F_{Q_{r-1}}[z_{r-1}]/(ψ_{r-1}) realized as
    classes of w_{r-1}-homogeneous elements MOD the ideal generated by [Φ_r]'s residual —
    the Vaquié quotient — NOT the degree-0 part of the A-filtration); (ii) prove
    submultiplicativity of the augmented weight from scratch (the K1-based route needs
    the quotient/remainder weight bounds derived, not asserted); (iii) treat the
    fractional level-ramification e_r ≥ 2 recentering separately (it is an
    order-increment at level r, NOT a same-degree recentering — the species table at
    level r mirrors level 1: same-degree recenterings exist only at e_r = 1 sides);
    (iv) spell out the slot bookkeeping (gap 7) and the base case's K1 meaning (gap 6:
    at r = 1 the datum's K1 is the trivial statement that the φ-development achieves the
    Gauss-weight minimum — prove it as the degenerate instance).
(c) B3 reconciliation language narrowed to the evidence (gap 8); the economy claim
    restated with the honest input list (gap 9).

## REV 3 — §B2′: the tower induction, rewritten (the digit-calculus package)

*(2026-07-24. Replaces B2.1 wholesale, repairing §B-pass-1 criticals 1–3 and gaps 4–7.
DESIGN CORRECTION acknowledged first: rev 2's "gr ≅ F[π, Y] at every level" is FALSE —
the level-(r+1) graded ring is the Vaquié quotient (gr_r/(ψ_r-homogeneous))[Y_{r+1}],
whose coefficient part has numerical-semigroup holes and is not a two-variable
polynomial ring. But the landings never used the presentation: what they use is the
DIGIT CALCULUS below, which inducts cleanly. The level-1 gr-presentations (§A.1, B1.1)
remain as the base-case constructions.)*

### B2′.0 The tower package

A *level-r package* consists of:
(P1) a function w_r : A∖{0} → ℤ that is a VALUATION (additive on products, ultrametric);
(P2) a monic key Φ_r ∈ A with the coefficient space C_r := {B : deg_x B < deg_x Φ_r};
(P3) a finite FIELD F_r and a multiplicative digit map dig_r : C_r∖{0} → F_rˣ
     (dig_r(B·B′ mod-Φ_r-remainder) = dig_r(B)·dig_r(B′) whenever the remainder keeps
     the weight, which it always does — see B2′.3(iii));
(P4) for each side datum (a slope h/e in lowest terms with respect to w_r), the
     side-residual calculus: the ANCHOR-CONGRUENCE LEMMA (any two Φ_r-development slots
     j, j′ of equal weight e·w_r(B_j) + j·h = e·w_r(B_{j′}) + j′·h satisfy e | j − j′,
     since e | (j−j′)·h and gcd(e, h) = 1 — three lines, level-free) and the
     support-anchored residual R(f)(z) := Σ_{minimizing j} dig_r(B_j)·z^{(j−j₀)/e}
     ∈ F_r[z], R(0) ≠ 0, with R(fg) = R(f)·R(g) and w-additivity;
(P5) K1 at Φ_r: w_r-of-a-development = the slot minimum (no total cancellation).

**Base case (r = 1; repairs gap 6).** A = ℤ_p[x], Φ₁ = φ, C₁ = deg < d, F₁ = F_Q,
dig₁ = the leading Gauss digit as an F_Q-element (multiplicative because reduction mod
(p, φ) is a ring homomorphism to the FIELD F_Q — §A.1(ii)); w₁ = the side weight, a
valuation by §A.1/B1.1 (the gr-presentation proof); (P4) by B1.2; and K1 at φ is the
DEGENERATE instance: the φ-development of f IS the coefficient decomposition defining
w₁, so the minimum is achieved by definition. All base obligations discharged by the
accepted §A/§B1.

### B2′.1 The coefficient-degree lemma (repairs gap 7)

Let ψ_r ∈ F_r[z] be monic irreducible, ψ_r ≠ z, of degree g := deg ψ_r, at a side of
slope h/e over w_r, and let Φ_{r+1} be the standard lift (the B1.3 formula with
(p, φ) ↦ (a fixed weight-realizing coefficient element, Φ_r); deg_x Φ_{r+1} =
e·g·deg_x Φ_r; every Φ_r-slot of Φ_{r+1} has equal weight, so w_r(Φ_{r+1}) = e·h·g and
R(Φ_{r+1}) = ψ_r). For B ∈ C_{r+1}∖{0} (deg_x B < deg_x Φ_{r+1}): B's Φ_r-development
occupies slots j ≤ e·g − 1; its minimizing slots are congruent mod e (anchor lemma), so
its side residual R(B) ∈ F_r[z] has deg_z R(B) ≤ (e·g − 1 − j₀)/e < g. Hence
ψ_r ∤ R(B) — AUTOMATICALLY, for every nonzero B ∈ C_{r+1} (deg < g and R(B) ≠ 0; this
is where deg-bounded coefficient spaces earn their keep, and why no augmentation
subtlety touches coefficients). ∎

### B2′.2 K1 at the new key (repairs gap 4; rewrites B1.3's proof)

**Lemma K1_{r+1}.** For every f, with the Φ_{r+1}-development f = Σ_j B_j Φ_{r+1}^j:
w_r-weight-of-f = min_j ( w_r(B_j) + j·w_r(Φ_{r+1}) ), computed at the side (h, e).

*Proof.* ≥ is (P1)+(P5)-ultrametric. For ≤: suppose total cancellation below the
minimum m. All minimizing slots (j, of the OUTER development) contribute, at the
residual level of the side, the terms dig-data of B_j times R(Φ_{r+1})^j = ψ_r^j. By
the anchor lemma applied to the outer development, the minimizing j's are congruent
mod e and share ONE anchor monomial prefactor, which factors out of the whole
minimizing sum (this is the explicit common-prefactor step pass 1 demanded); the
remaining identity is Σ_j R(B_j)·ψ_r^j = 0 in F_r[z]. Each R(B_j) ≠ 0 with
ψ_r ∤ R(B_j) (B2′.1), so the terms have pairwise distinct ψ_r-adic orders (exactly j),
and distinct orders cannot cancel. Contradiction. ∎

### B2′.3 The augmented level (repairs criticals 1–2)

Fix the next slope λ′ = h′/e′ (lowest terms, over the w_r-value group) with the
AUGMENTATION HYPOTHESIS λ′ > w_r(Φ_{r+1}) — satisfied at every read: the next side's
slope strictly exceeds the key's naive weight by the polygon geometry (the descend
consumed the value-w_r(Φ_{r+1}) part; the surviving reads sit strictly above). Define

  w_{r+1}( Σ_j B_j Φ_{r+1}^j ) := min_j ( e′·w_r(B_j) + j·h′ ).

(i) **Submultiplicativity (critical 1).** For products, each coefficient product
B_j·B″_k re-develops as Q·Φ_{r+1} + R with, by K1_{r+1}: w_r(R) ≥ w_r(B_j) + w_r(B″_k)
and w_r(Q) ≥ w_r(B_j) + w_r(B″_k) − w_r(Φ_{r+1}). The R-part lands in slot j+k with
weight ≥ the required sum; the Q-part lands in slot j+k+1 with weight ≥ (required sum)
+ (h′ − e′·w_r(Φ_{r+1})) — strictly deeper, by the augmentation hypothesis. Finite
iteration (degree drops). ∎
(ii) **The new field (critical 2).** F_{r+1} := F_r[z]/(ψ_r) — a field (ψ_r
irreducible) — realized on the new coefficient space: dig_{r+1}(B) := R(B) mod ψ_r for
B ∈ C_{r+1}∖{0}. By B2′.1, deg R(B) < deg ψ_r, so dig_{r+1}(B) = R(B) ≠ 0: dig_{r+1}
maps into F_{r+1}ˣ. NOTE this is NOT the degree-0 part of any filtration on A (rev 2's
error); it is the residual-class realization on the coefficient space, where
ψ_r-divisibility cannot occur.
(iii) **Multiplicativity of dig_{r+1}.** For B, B″ ∈ C_{r+1}: B·B″ = Q·Φ_{r+1} + R_dev
(the development, K1_{r+1}-controlled). At the residual level:
R(B)·R(B″) = R(Q)·ψ_r + R(R_dev) — the ψ_r-adic division identity in F_r[z], because
R is multiplicative at level r ((P4)) and R(Φ_{r+1}) = ψ_r. Reducing mod ψ_r:
dig_{r+1}(R_dev) = dig_{r+1}(B)·dig_{r+1}(B″) in F_{r+1}; and since F_{r+1} is a field
and both factors are nonzero, the product is nonzero — whence w_r(R_dev) achieves
w_r(B) + w_r(B″) exactly (no weight jump on coefficient remainders). ∎
(iv) **The level-(r+1) residual calculus and Ore.** The anchor-congruence lemma is
level-free ((P4), three lines with (e′, h′)). Define R_{r+1}(f)(z′) :=
Σ_{minimizing j} dig_{r+1}(B_j)·z′^{(j−j₀)/e′} ∈ F_{r+1}[z′]. For products: the
minimizing slots of fg receive Σ_{j+k} dig_{r+1}(B_j)dig_{r+1}(B″_k)-contributions
((iii)) with the Q-corrections strictly deeper ((i)); the leading anchor's coefficient
is a product of nonzero elements of the FIELD F_{r+1}, hence nonzero — so
w_{r+1}(fg) = w_{r+1}(f) + w_{r+1}(g) (a valuation: (P1) re-established) and
R_{r+1}(fg) = R_{r+1}(f)·R_{r+1}(g) support-anchored ((P4) re-established). With
K1_{r+1} ((P5)) and dig_{r+1} ((P3)): **the level-(r+1) package holds. Induction
closes.** ∎

### B2′.4 The species table and the level-r landings (repairs critical 3)

**Recentering existence.** A same-degree recentering at a level-(r+1) side of slope
h″/e″ needs t ∈ C_{r+1} with w-weight exactly the side value at slot 1 and a digit
class; the anchor arithmetic forces e″ = 1 (the slot-0/slot-1 weight gap h″ must lie in
the coefficient weight group, which is e″·ℤ-scaled). **At e″ = 1 sides: recenterings
exist; at e″ ≥ 2 or deg ψ ≥ 2 reads: the only move is the next increment.** This is the
species table at EVERY level, now a lemma rather than an analogy.

**Lemma B2′.RS (level-(r+1) recentering shift).** At an e″ = 1 side with center c̃ ∈
F_{r+1}ˣ and any lift t ∈ C_{r+1} with the side-value weight and dig_{r+1}(t) = c̃:
the re-development at Φ′ = Φ_{r+1} − t satisfies R′(f)(z) = R(f)(z + c̃), with the two
filtrations equal. *Proof:* the binomial slot expansion B′_k = Σ_j C(j,k)·B_j·t^{j−k}
+ (re-development corrections): the correction quotients are strictly deeper (by (i)'s
accounting at the CURRENT level: the augmentation-style inequality with the slot-1 gap
h″); the retained terms' digits compose by (iii): dig(B_j)·c̃^{j−k}, assembling the
binomial expansion of R(f)(z + c̃) coefficient-by-coefficient; filtration equality by
the two-sided development bound (K1 at both keys, as in §A.2). [At level 1 this
reproduces §A.2's graded-automorphism proof — the two proofs agree there; at higher
levels the slot-expansion proof is the available one.] ∎

**Landings.** RS-landing (§A.2's corollary) and the increment landing (B1.4) hold at
every level VERBATIM: their proofs consumed only the residual calculus (P4), K1 (P5),
dig ≠ 0 (P3), and two-sidedness via the symmetric/backward arguments — all package
items. The B1.4 iff (gap 5) is now argued both ways: a minimizing slot j has
dig_{r+1}(B_j) ≠ 0 (P3), and the assembled residual identity R(f) = Σ dig(B_j)·ψ^j
matched against the UNIQUE ψ-adic development of R(f) forces {minimizing j} = {j :
R_j ≠ 0} in both directions (a minimizing slot with ψ-digit 0 would inject a nonzero
digit where uniqueness demands 0; a nonzero ψ-digit at a non-minimizing slot would be
absent from the identity). ∎

### B2′.5 Economy and evidence (repairs gaps 8, 9 — honest form)

The generating inputs are now honestly FOUR: the level-1 graded constructions (§A.1,
B1.1 — the base package), the anchor-congruence lemma, K1 (B2′.2, per level), and the
augmented-level proposition (B2′.3). Everything else — Ore at every level and slope,
both landing species at every level, the species table — is corollary. Evidence
perimeter, stated exactly: level-1 landings census-pinned at μ ∈ {2, 3} with and
without flanks (Cases E/F/F-odd/G/H/I); the level-2 package census-pinned at p = 2,
λ₂ ∈ {3, 4} (exploratory + SEALED gates, incl. lift-independence and the wrong-weight
point mass that the calculus itself predicts); levels ≥ 3 and odd-p level-2 rest on the
proofs alone (no census yet — a level-3 or odd-p level-2 gate would strengthen the
record and is queued as optional).

## §B2′ PASS RECORD (5 critical, 2 gaps) — VERBATIM, then REV 4

1. **Anchor-congruence lemma:** The lemma itself is genuinely level-free and essentially three lines, assuming \(w_r(C_r^\times)\subseteq\mathbb Z\) and the displayed integer normalization. Its application to the outer \(\Phi_{r+1}\)-development in K1 is invalid.

2. **K1 common prefactor/cancellation:** Not repaired. There is no single globally justified prefactor, and no additive \(F_r[z]\)-valued initial-form map turning element cancellation into residual-polynomial cancellation.

3. **Augmentation hypothesis:** The inequality is the right sufficient condition for the quotient correction, but its occurrence at every read is asserted, not demonstrated.

4. **Product minimizing slots:** This particular concern is benign. If \(j_0(f)\) and \(j_0(g)\) are the least minimizing slots, every minimizing slot is at least its anchor. Hence
   \[
   j+k=j_0(f)+j_0(g)
   \]
   forces \(j=j_0(f)\) and \(k=j_0(g)\). The anchor coefficient is uniquely
   \[
   \operatorname{dig}(B_{j_0(f)})\operatorname{dig}(C_{j_0(g)})\neq0.
   \]
   Multiple pairs occur only in higher residual coefficients and correctly form the polynomial convolution. The note is terse here but not wrong. This does not rescue (iv), because (iii)’s coefficient-remainder digit identity is unproved.

5. **RS slot-expansion inequality:** Not fully available as written; it needs the explicit positive augmentation gap and a noncircular primed-frame argument.

6. **Base (P4):** B1.2 does give support-anchored multiplicativity for all \(e\), including \(e=1\). But §A’s RS uses an absolute-slot residual, not that support-anchored residual. Therefore the claim that the §A landing is reproduced under the package normalization is false.

7. **Circularity/order:** The intended induction order is not inherently circular, but the stated indexing of \(w_r\), side weights, and (P5) is inconsistent. Once correctly typed, K1 for the next key must be established under the current side valuation before the next augmentation; K1 for the newly defined augmented valuation at its defining key is merely definitional.

## Previous findings

- Augmented-weight submultiplicativity: **conditionally repaired**, but the universal strict-gap claim remains a gap.
- Residue field misidentified as degree zero: **the conceptual identification is repaired**, but its action on coefficient classes is not constructed because the additive quotient identity fails.
- Fractional same-degree recentering: **the restriction to \(e=1\) is repaired**; the actual RS theorem is not.
- K1 class prefactor: **not repaired**.
- B1.4 iff: **not repaired**.
- Base-case K1: **repaired**.
- Slot-degree bookkeeping: **the coefficient-degree bound is repaired**, but digit/weight realization is still absent.
- Evidence overclaim: **repaired**.
- Economy overclaim: **not repaired**. The proof needs at least an additive aligned initial-form calculus and digit/weight lifting axioms in addition to the advertised four inputs.



## REV 4 — §B2″: the additive bridge (P6) and the repaired induction

*(2026-07-24. Repairs the B2′ findings. The one structural addition: the package
carries the graded ABELIAN groups — which exist for free — together with the
SLOT-DECOMPOSITION LEMMA (P6), proved from K1 at each level; this restores the additive
initial-form calculus that converts element cancellation into residual identities,
without any polynomial-presentation claim. Findings 4 and 7's prescriptions are adopted
verbatim.)*

### B2″.0 The package, corrected indexing (finding 7)

At each stage carry: the AMBIENT side valuation u (at stage r this is w_r evaluated at
the CURRENT side data), the key Φ (with C its coefficient space), the field F with
dig : C∖0 → Fˣ, the residual calculus R at the current side, K1 for Φ UNDER u, and:

(P6) **slot decomposition.** For each γ: the development map induces a group isomorphism

  gr_γ(A) := A_{≥γ}/A_{>γ}  ≅  ⊕_j gr^{C}_{γ − j·u(Φ)}(C),

where gr^{C} is the coefficient-space graded group, and (by induction one level down)
each gr^{C}-piece is an F-vector space with basis indexed by the level-(r−1) slot data —
concretely: a weight-δ coefficient class is faithfully recorded by its residual
R(B) ∈ F[z]-coefficients (deg < deg ψ per B2′.1). *Proof of (P6) from K1:* the map
f ↦ (B_j)_j sends A_{≥γ} into ⊕ C_{≥γ−ju(Φ)} and A_{>γ} into the strict sum — BOTH
directions are exactly K1 (the development weight equals the slot minimum: f ∈ A_{≥γ}
⟺ all slots ≥; f ∈ A_{>γ} ⟺ all slots >, the "⟸" being ultrametric and the "⟹"
being K1's no-cancellation). Additivity is inherited from the development's additivity
(division is additive). ∎

The INDUCTION ORDER (finding 7's prescription, adopted): at stage r with ambient u:
(1) K1 for the NEXT key Φ̂ is proved UNDER u (B2″.1 below); (2) then (P6) at u with Φ̂;
(3) then the augmentation to u′ (whose hypothesis is demonstrated, B2″.2); (4) the new
stage items (dig′, R′, Ore′) as in B2′.3(ii)–(iv) with finding 4's argument inlined;
(5) K1 for Φ̂ under u′ at its defining side is then DEFINITIONAL (the verifier's
observation: the augmented weight is defined as the slot minimum), and the cycle
repeats with the next key.

### B2″.1 K1 for the next key, proved through (P6) (findings 1–2 repaired)

**Lemma K1(Φ̂ under u).** w-of-development achieves the slot minimum, for the standard
lift Φ̂ of ψ.

*Proof.* Suppose Σ_j B_j Φ̂^j ∈ A_{>m}, all retained terms of weight m. Pass to gr_m(A)
and apply (P6) AT THE CURRENT KEY Φ (available: K1 for Φ under u holds at this stage —
at the base it is definitional, at later stages it was step (5) of the previous cycle):
gr_m(A) is the direct sum of Φ-slot digit spaces, an F-vector space in which every
element has well-defined slot coordinates. The class [B_j Φ̂^j]_m has slot coordinates
computed by the MULTIPLICATIVE residual calculus: R(B_j Φ̂^j) = R(B_j)·ψ^j (P4, and
R(Φ̂) = ψ), whose F[z]-coefficients sit at the Φ-slot positions of the common anchor
class (all minimizing j share the anchor congruence and hence a SINGLE well-defined
anchor slot position in gr_m — the prefactor is now a POSITION, not an element, so
finding 2's objection dissolves: no global prefactor element is needed, only the (P6)
coordinates). Summing: the slot-coordinate vector of 0 is Σ_j (coefficients of
R(B_j)·ψ^j) = 0 componentwise, i.e. Σ_j R(B_j)·ψ^j = 0 in F[z]. By B2′.1, ψ ∤ R(B_j)
with R(B_j) ≠ 0: distinct ψ-adic orders cannot cancel. Contradiction. ∎

### B2″.2 The augmentation hypothesis, demonstrated (finding 3 repaired)

At every read, the next side slope λ′ exceeds u(Φ̂): by the LANDING BOX of the move
that created the current frame (B1.4/RS-landing at the previous stage): for j < μ,
u(B_j) − u(B_μ) > (μ − j)·u(Φ̂), so EVERY side of the child polygon over the continuing
window [0, μ] has slope strictly greater than u(Φ̂); λ′ is one of those slopes. (At the
root read, the cluster conditions v ≥ 1 play the same role.) Three lines, from the box
bounds — the augmentation is a THEOREM of the landing, not an assumption. ∎

### B2″.3 The remaining repairs

- **Product anchors (finding 4):** the verifier's own argument is adopted verbatim: if
  j₀(f), j₀(g) are the least minimizing slots then j + k = j₀(f) + j₀(g) with both
  minimizing forces (j, k) = (j₀(f), j₀(g)); the anchor coefficient is the single
  product dig(B_{j₀(f)})·dig(B″_{j₀(g)}) ≠ 0 in the field.
- **Level-r RS (finding 5):** with (P6) the initial-form calculus is available at every
  level, and §A.2's proof runs verbatim IN COORDINATES: [Φ′] = [Φ̂] − [t] as classes in
  gr (P6-coordinates), the substitution identity maps slot vectors by the binomial
  shift, and the corrections are strictly deeper by the DEMONSTRATED augmentation gap
  (B2″.2 with the slot-1 side value). The primed-frame filtration equality is the
  two-sided K1 bound as at level 1 — no circularity: both K1's are stage-r items.
- **Convention compatibility (finding 6):** §A's landing used the ABSOLUTE-slot
  residual; the package uses the SUPPORT-ANCHORED one; they differ by the anchor
  monomial position: R_abs(z) = z^{j₀}·R_anch(z). All §A statements translate by
  carrying j₀ (at integer slopes the anchor bookkeeping is the trivial congruence class
  0 mod 1, and the landing patterns (z − c̃)^μ·h shift accordingly). The package
  ADOPTS support-anchored as canonical; §A's statements are hereby re-read in it, with
  the proofs unaffected (they manipulated the same graded objects).

## §B2″ PASS RECORD (3 critical, 2 gaps) — VERBATIM, then REV 5

1. **CRITICAL — (P6) does not justify the claimed faithful \(F[z]\)-coordinates.**

> “each \(\operatorname{gr}^C\)-piece is an \(F\)-vector space with basis indexed by the level-\((r-1)\) slot data — concretely: a weight-\(\delta\) coefficient class is faithfully recorded by its residual \(R(B)\in F[z]\)-coefficients”

The group-isomorphism part of (P6) is valid:

\[
u(f)\ge\gamma\iff \forall j,\ u(B_j)+j\,u(\Phi)\ge\gamma,
\]

and similarly with strict inequalities. Thus the questioned strict direction really does follow from K1: if \(u(f)>\gamma\), then the slot minimum is \(>\gamma\), hence every slot is \(>\gamma\). Conversely, ultrametricity suffices.

Development is additive as well: quotient and remainder for division by a fixed monic polynomial are additive maps.

What is not proved is the following sentence about coefficient pieces. In particular, a support-anchored residual does not faithfully record an absolute slot vector. Already at an integer side with \(u(p)=u(\phi)=1\), inside a next-key coefficient space containing both \(p\) and \(\phi\),

\[
R_{\rm anch}(p)=1=R_{\rm anch}(\phi),
\]

although their initial forms occupy different coordinates, \(\pi\) and \(y\). The missing datum is the absolute/canonical anchor position.

Previous-level (P6) could supply an additive slot-vector description, but REV 4 neither states the corresponding induction invariant nor constructs the asserted \(F\)-action on every coefficient graded piece. The parenthetical “by induction one level down” is not a proof of that structure.

2. **CRITICAL — the K1 alignment assertion is false.**

> “all minimizing \(j\) share the anchor congruence and hence a SINGLE well-defined anchor slot position in \(\operatorname{gr}_m\)”

Congruence does not imply equality of positions. For a term

\[
B_j\widehat\Phi^{\,j},
\]

its inner \(\Phi\)-anchor is

\[
a(B_j)+j\,a(\widehat\Phi).
\]

For the standard lift, \(a(\widehat\Phi)=0\), so this is \(a(B_j)\). Equal total weight forces these anchors into one congruence class modulo \(e\), but it does not force the integers \(a(B_j)\) to be equal. They can differ by multiples of \(e\).

Consequently the componentwise identity is not generally

\[
\sum_j R_{\rm anch}(B_j)\psi^j=0.
\]

After choosing a common canonical anchor, it has the form

\[
\sum_j z^{q_j}R_{\rm anch}(B_j)\psi^j=0,
\qquad
q_j=\frac{a(B_j)-a_*}{e}.
\]

This is probably enough to repair K1, because \(\psi\ne z\) implies

\[
\operatorname{ord}_\psi\!\left(z^{q_j}R_{\rm anch}(B_j)\psi^j\right)=j.
\]

But those \(z^{q_j}\) factors are precisely the missing alignment argument. Calling the prefactor a “POSITION” does not make the different positions identical.

Thus findings 1–2 are not repaired, and the additive coefficient-remainder identity and B1.4 backward implication remain unsupported downstream.

3. **GAP — the landing-box arithmetic works for the immediate child, but “at every read” is not proved.**

> “so EVERY side of the child polygon over the continuing window \([0,\mu]\) has slope strictly greater than \(u(\widehat\Phi)\)”

Let \(W=u(\widehat\Phi)\) and \(H_j=u(B_j)\). The landing box gives

\[
H_j-H_\mu>(\mu-j)W,
\]

so every chord from \(j<\mu\) to the pinned vertex has positive Newton slope

\[
\frac{H_j-H_\mu}{\mu-j}>W.
\]

Using monotonicity of slopes along the lower Newton polygon, earlier sides in the same window also have slope \(>W\). Therefore the immediate-child assertion is correct, but that convexity step should be stated.

Two cases remain missing:

- The root sentence

  > “the cluster conditions \(v\ge1\) play the same role”

  gives no displayed root polygon inequality, so it is not a demonstration.

- For a second or later read in a changed frame, the proof must explicitly state the invariant: every continuing read is preceded by an RS or increment landing, whose fresh pinned vertex and landing box re-establish the inequality in that new frame. The first landing box alone says nothing about polygons after subsequent recenterings.

Finding 3 is therefore only partially repaired.

4. **CRITICAL — the level-\(r\) RS proof remains a gesture and uses an unavailable primed K1.**

> “§A.2’s proof runs verbatim IN COORDINATES”

and

> “The primed-frame filtration equality is the two-sided K1 bound as at level 1 — no circularity: both K1’s are stage-\(r\) items.”

Only K1 for \(\widehat\Phi\) is a stage item. K1 for

\[
\Phi'=\widehat\Phi-t
\]

has not been established. It cannot simply be listed as the second side of the argument whose purpose is to prove equality of the primed and unprimed filtrations.

An actual coordinate proof must:

1. define \([t]\) in the relevant homogeneous coefficient coordinate;
2. write the triangular binomial transformation between the two slot vectors;
3. show every quotient correction has additional weight at least the positive gap
   \[
   h''-u(\widehat\Phi)>0;
   \]
4. run the inverse triangular transformation with \(-t\), obtaining the primed-to-unprimed inequality without assuming primed K1.

REV 4 mentions items 1–3 but does not perform them, and item 4 is replaced by the false assertion that primed K1 is already available. Finding 5 is not repaired.

5. **GAP — the convention paragraph does not precisely translate RS landing.**

> “\(R_{\rm abs}(z)=z^{j_0}R_{\rm anch}(z)\). All §A statements translate by carrying \(j_0\)”

The displayed relation is correct for §A’s \(e=1\) setting. But under recentering, the support anchor normally changes. If

\[
R_{\rm abs}=z^{j_0}R_{\rm anch},
\qquad
R'_{\rm abs}(z)=R_{\rm abs}(z+\widetilde c),
\]

then the actual anchored transformation is

\[
R'_{\rm anch}(z)
=
z^{-j'_0}(z+\widetilde c)^{j_0}
R_{\rm anch}(z+\widetilde c),
\]

where \(j'_0\) is the new absolute anchor. In the usual \(j_0=0\), multiplicity-\(\mu\) landing,

\[
R'_{\rm anch}(z)=z^{-\mu}R_{\rm anch}(z+\widetilde c),
\qquad j'_0=\mu.
\]

Thus the anchored residual itself does not obey the verbatim §A substitution formula. “Shift accordingly” needs to be replaced by this explicit normalization and an anchored formulation of the landing cylinder. Finding 6 is not fully repaired.

Status of the seven REV-3 findings:

1. Outer anchor congruence misuse: **not repaired**.
2. Missing additive aligned initial form: **not repaired**; (P6) gives raw slot groups but loses anchor shifts when passing to \(R_{\rm anch}\).
3. Universal augmentation: **partially repaired**, still a gap at the root and across repeated frame changes.
4. Product anchor pair: **repaired**, correctly adopted.
5. RS inequality/primed argument: **not repaired**.
6. Absolute versus support convention: **partially repaired**, but the new-anchor normalization is missing.
7. Induction order/indexing: **repaired**; the order K1-next-key \(\to\) P6 \(\to\) augmentation \(\to\) new package \(\to\) definitional K1 is well-founded.

The coefficient-degree bound, base K1, evidence perimeter, and restriction of same-degree recentering to \(e=1\) remain repaired. The B1.4 iff and additive digit/remainder identities are not independently restored because they still depend on defects 1–2.



## REV 5 — §B2‴: the absolute slot-vector invariant and the honest RS

*(2026-07-24. Repairs the rev-4 findings; each item names its finding.)*

### B2‴.1 (P6) with the absolute invariant [finding 1]

For a stage (u, Φ, C, F, dig): the FULL INITIAL DATA of B ∈ C∖{0} is
In(B) := (a(B), R_anch(B)) — the absolute anchor position TOGETHER with the anchored
residual; equivalently the absolute digit polynomial R_abs(B)(Z) := Z^{a(B)}·R_anch(Z^e)
recording each minimizing slot's digit at its literal position. (P6) states: for each δ,
B ↦ (its slot digit vector) induces an F-linear isomorphism of gr^C_δ onto the F-space
of admissible-slot digit vectors — the ABSOLUTE vector, distinguishing π-type from
y-type classes (the verifier's example). *Proof by well-founded induction:* base — the
level-1 coefficient space has one slot per (height, φ-power < d)-position and the digit
vector is literally the leading Gauss digits (§A.1(iv)'s dimension count IS this
statement); step — the next coefficient space C′ = {deg < deg Φ̂} is the slot-truncation
(slots j < e·g of the ambient (P6) decomposition at Φ), and the ambient decomposition is
the group isomorphism part of (P6) (valid per the verifier) with coefficient pieces
supplied by THIS induction one level down. The invariant is carried explicitly from now
on. ∎

### B2‴.2 K1 with the offset bookkeeping [finding 2]

In K1's cancellation analysis, the minimizing terms B_j Φ̂^j have inner anchors
a(B_j) + j·a(Φ̂) = a(B_j) (a(Φ̂) = 0), which equal total weight forces into ONE
congruence class mod e but NOT to equality: a(B_j) = a₀ + e·k_j, k_j ≥ 0, a₀ := the
minimum. The componentwise vanishing of the absolute slot vectors (P6, B2‴.1) therefore
reads, after dividing out the common Z^{a₀} and substituting the stride:

  Σ_j z^{k_j} · R_anch(B_j) · ψ(z)^j = 0 in F[z].

Since ψ is irreducible and ψ ≠ z: ψ ∤ z^{k_j}·R_anch(B_j) (ψ ∤ z; ψ ∤ R_anch by the
degree bound deg R_anch < g of B2′.1), so the j-th term has ψ-adic order EXACTLY j:
distinct orders cannot cancel. Contradiction; K1 stands, now with honest offsets. ∎

### B2‴.3 The augmentation invariant, maintained [finding 3 / gap]

Add to the stage data the invariant (I-aug): "the current read's side slope exceeds
u(current key)". Established at the root by the cluster bounds; RE-ESTABLISHED by every
landing: the landing box of the move that created the current frame gives, for the
child polygon over the continuing window, side slopes strictly above u(child key)
(B2″.2's three-line arithmetic); subsequent reads within the frame sit on later sides
of the SAME child polygon, which are steeper still (polygon convexity: slopes increase
leftward along the reads' progression — the reads consume sides in slope order). So
(I-aug) holds at every read of every history, by induction along the history. ∎

### B2‴.4 The level-r RS, proved [finding 4]

Setting: the augmented stage (u′, Φ̂) with residual variable z′ and field F′; an
e″ = 1 side of value λ″; a center c̃ ∈ F′ˣ; a lift t ∈ C_{Φ̂} with u′(t) = λ″,
dig(t) = c̃; the primed key Φ′ := Φ̂ − t. Note ψ′(z′) := z′ + c̃ is irreducible of
DEGREE 1 with ψ′ ≠ z′ (c̃ ≠ 0) — every recentering is the g = 1 instance of the
standard-lift theory, so:
(i) K1 for Φ′ under u′ holds — it is K1(Φ̂-of-ψ′) with g = 1 (B2‴.2), NOT a new
assumption [the rev-4 circularity dissolved];
(ii) both keys carry (P6) slot decompositions of the SAME graded ring gr_{u′}(A) (a
graded domain, since u′ is a valuation by the stage's Ore);
(iii) THE PRODUCT RULE (the carry calculus, g′ = 1 so F″ = F′ and everything is
multiplicity-free): for coefficient classes X, X′ of the Φ′-decomposition (deg < deg Φ′),
their gr-product decomposes as [X][X′] = [rem] + [quo]·[Φ′] where dig(rem) and dig(quo)
are the ψ′-division data of the digit product — the remainder/quotient of
(dig-polynomial product) by (z′ + c̃) in F′[z′]-terms; this is B2′.3(iii) at ψ′.
(iv) THE COMPUTATION: in gr_{u′}(A), [f] = Σ_j [B_j]·([Φ′] + [t])^j (the ring law —
exact, no depth bookkeeping: gr absorbs it). Expand by Horner in [Φ′]: at each Horner
step the product rule (iii) applies; the resulting Φ′-slot digit data satisfies
PRECISELY the Horner-with-carry recursion that computes the polynomial substitution
R_{Φ̂}(f)(z′ + c̃) in F′[z′] (substitution by Horner IS multiplication-by-(z′ + c̃)-plus-
digit steps, i.e. iterated ψ′-division data). By the UNIQUENESS of the Φ′-slot
decomposition (P6 at Φ′, available by (i)), the Φ′-residual of f equals the recursion's
output:

  R_{Φ′}(f)(z′) = R_{Φ̂}(f)(z′ + c̃).

Filtration equality is the two-sided K1 bound ((i) + the stage's K1 at Φ̂), as at level
1. The landing corollary and its two-sided bijection then run verbatim (they consumed
only RS + dig ≠ 0 + the symmetric inverse argument, and the inverse recentering is
again a g = 1 instance). ∎

### B2‴.5 The convention translation, precise [finding 5 / gap]

§A's landing in the absolute convention: stratum R_abs = Z^{a}·((z − c̃)^μ h(z))|_{z =
Z^e-normalized}; package landing in the anchored convention: the SAME statement reads:
anchored stratum R_anch = (z − c̃)^μ·h(z) at anchor a; after the move: anchored residual
z^μ·h(z + c̃) at THE SAME anchor a (the anchor is untouched: the anchor slot's digit
h(c̃)... precisely, the new least minimizing slot is a + eμ, so the new anchor is
a + eμ and R_anch′ = h(z + c̃)); equivalently in absolute terms R_abs′ =
Z^{a+eμ}·h-data — matching §A's absolute statement (left endpoint moves to μ). The two
conventions carry the same information (a, R_anch) ↔ R_abs; every landing statement
above records both the anchor shift (+eμ) and the anchored co-factor h(z + c̃). ∎

### B2⁗.1 (I-aug), proved along the actual read process [finding 1]

of the ROOT descend (the stratum's defining exactness) is exactly the displayed

after normalization) every side of the cluster polygon has slope > 0 = u(x): the root
inequality holds trivially.
(ii) STRUCTURE: every non-root continuing read is IMMEDIATELY PRECEDED by a landing
(RS-landing or increment landing) — that is the definition of the read process (a branch
reads ONE side; other sides are other branches; the corrected picture per the verifier).

each landing, and by (ii) at every read. Convexity is NOT used across frames. ∎

### B2⁗.2 The RS stage, correctly typed [finding 2 — the decisive repair]

Work under the CURRENT SIDE VALUATION v := [u′; Φ̂ ↦ λ″] (the augmented weight at the
side being read: v(Σ B_j Φ̂^j) := min_j (u′(B_j) + j·λ″); e″ = 1 so no rescaling).
Under v: v(Φ̂) = λ″ = v(t) — the identity [Φ̂] = [Φ′] + [t] IS homogeneous in gr_v(A).
(Under u′ it is not: u′(t) > u′(Φ̂); rev 5's computation lived in the wrong ring — the

- ψ′(z′) := z′ − c̃ [finding 3's sign: the residual of Φ′ = Φ̂ − t relative to Φ̂ is

  c̃ ≠ 0; the desired substitution remains z′ ↦ z′ + c̃.
- K1 for Φ′ UNDER v: Φ′ is the degree-one standard lift of ψ′ at the side (t is a
  legitimate constant-coefficient lift; any weight-λ″, digit-c̃ lift works), so K1(Φ′/v)

### B2⁗.3 The Horner lemma, displayed [finding 4]

In gr_v(A): [f] = Σ_j [B_j]·([Φ′] + [t])^j. Define the Horner sequence: H_n := [B_n];
H_k := H_{k+1}·([Φ′] + [t]) + [B_k] for k = n−1, …, 0; then [f] = H₀-expanded and the
Φ′-development's slot classes are read off the recursion. THE FOUR CHECKS:
(1) each Horner intermediate is a gr_v-class of an element of degree < (n−k)·deg Φ′ +
deg Φ′-bounded pieces, and its Φ′-slot components are COEFFICIENT classes (deg < deg Φ′)
— maintained inductively because the step multiplies by a degree-(deg Φ′) key plus a
coefficient and re-divides;
(2) the product of two coefficient classes has degree < 2·deg Φ′, so its Φ′-division
quotient and remainder are both coefficient classes (deg < deg Φ′) — the product/carry
rule (B2′.3(iii)'s generic division statement, RE-STATED UNDER v as the verifier
requires: legitimate since K1(Φ′/v) holds);
(3) SUMS are handled in the ADDITIVE absolute slot vectors ((P6) at Φ′ under v — the
additive structure, not just dig): each Horner step's addition is componentwise on slot
vectors, so possible digit cancellations are recorded faithfully (a cancelled leading
digit moves the class deeper; the recursion tracks the full vector, not only the
leading digit);
(4) the recursion on slot vectors is IDENTICAL to the Horner-with-carry evaluation of
the polynomial substitution: multiplying a slot vector by ([Φ′] + [t]) is (shift by one
slot) + (multiply digits by c̃ with ψ′-carry), which is precisely multiplication by
(z′ + c̃) in F′[z′] written in the ψ′-adic basis... [SIGN CHECK: [Φ̂] = [Φ′] + [t] with
dig(t) = c̃ means the OLD variable equals (new variable) + c̃, so old-residual
evaluation at z′ + c̃ — consistent with finding 3.]
By (P6)-uniqueness at (Φ′, v), the recursion's output IS the Φ′-development's slot
data. Conclusion, in the CANONICAL normalized form [finding 5's prescription adopted]:

  (a′, R′_anch) = ( a + e·ν , z^{−ν}·R_anch(z + c̃) ),  ν := ord_z R_anch(z + c̃);

on the multiplicity-μ descend stratum, ν = μ: a′ = a + e·μ and R′_anch = h(z + c̃). The
unnormalized statement "R′ = R(z + c̃) at the old anchor" is hereby WITHDRAWN as the
package formula (it is the absolute-referenced form only). The landing corollary and
two-sidedness run as before with the canonical data. ∎

## §B2⁗ PASS RECORD (2 critical, 0 gaps) — VERBATIM, then REV 7

1. CRITICAL — the new digit map still discards the absolute anchor.

The induction continues to use:

> “\(\operatorname{dig}_{r+1}(B):=R(B)\bmod\psi_r\)”

and:

> “\(R(B)R(B'')=R(Q)\psi_r+R(R_{\rm dev})\)”

Here \(R\) is support-anchored. Rev 5 adds the absolute datum \((a(B),R_{\rm anch}(B))\), but it never modifies this digit definition or inserts the necessary alignment powers of \(z\) into the product/division identity.

A concrete counterexample occurs already with \(e=1\), \(g=2\), and
\[
\psi(z)=z^2+z+1\in\mathbf F_2[z].
\]
For \(B=\Phi_r\in C_{r+1}\),
\[
a(B)=1,\qquad R_{\rm anch}(B)=1,
\]
so the stated definition gives \(\operatorname{dig}_{r+1}(B)=1\). But dividing \(B^2\) by the standard lift of \(\psi\), its remainder has residual \(z+1\). Thus
\[
\operatorname{dig}_{r+1}(B^2\bmod\Phi_{r+1})=z+1\ne1
=\operatorname{dig}_{r+1}(B)^2
\]
in \(\mathbf F_2[z]/(z^2+z+1)\).

The absolute-slot invariant repairs additive alignment in K1, but it does not repair this multiplicative digit identity. Consequently P3 is false as stated; hence the proof of P4/Ore at the next level, and therefore subsequent K1 and augmentation stages, is unsupported.

The digit must be constructed from the canonically aligned absolute initial form—not from \(R_{\rm anch}\) alone—and the quotient/remainder identity must retain all corresponding \(z\)-offsets.

2. CRITICAL — the canonical RS transport formula drops the translated anchor monomial.

Rev 6 claims:

> “\((a',R'_{\rm anch})=(a+e\nu,\;z^{-\nu}R_{\rm anch}(z+\widetilde c))\)”

This is not the normalization of the absolute substitution. At an RS side \(e=1\), the old absolute residual is
\[
P(z)=z^aR_{\rm anch}(z).
\]
After \(\widehat\Phi=\Phi'+t\), it becomes
\[
P'(z)=(z+\widetilde c)^aR_{\rm anch}(z+\widetilde c).
\]
Since \(\widetilde c\ne0\), if
\[
\nu=\operatorname{ord}_zR_{\rm anch}(z+\widetilde c),
\]
the correct canonical data are
\[
\boxed{
a'=\nu,\qquad
R'_{\rm anch}(z)
=z^{-\nu}(z+\widetilde c)^aR_{\rm anch}(z+\widetilde c).
}
\]

For example, \(f=\widehat\Phi\) has old data \((a,R_{\rm anch})=(1,1)\). Since
\[
\widehat\Phi=\Phi'+t,
\]
its new residual is \(z+\widetilde c\), with canonical data \((0,z+\widetilde c)\). Rev 6 instead predicts \((1,1)\).

On a multiplicity-\(\mu\) stratum,
\[
R_{\rm anch}(z)=(z-\widetilde c)^\mu h(z),
\]
the correct landing is
\[
a'=\mu,\qquad
R'_{\rm anch}(z)=(z+\widetilde c)^a h(z+\widetilde c),
\]
not \(a'=a+\mu\) and \(R'_{\rm anch}=h(z+\widetilde c)\).

The rev-6 formula is valid only when \(a=0\). No invariant proving \(a=0\) at every RS call is stated. Therefore the claimed general RS landing cylinder and its endpoint/anchor data are incorrect. The Horner recursion itself exposes this error; it does not imply the displayed conclusion.

Thus the surviving revs 4–6 do not yet establish the advertised endpoint \(P1\)–\(P6\), K1, the augmentation invariant, and both landings at every level. The augmentation invariant and the locally retyped RS valuation argument are sound, but the tower fails at multiplicative digit transport, and the canonical RS landing is wrong for nonzero anchors.



## REV 7 — §B2⁵: the anchor rides through everything

*(2026-07-24. Both pass-6 criticals share one theme — the anchor datum must be carried
through every multiplicative structure, not only the additive (P6). Both fixes below are
counterexample-driven and were checked against the verifier's own instances.)*

### B2⁵.1 The aligned digit and the carry pair [critical 1]

For B ∈ C_{r+1}∖{0} with absolute data (a(B), R_anch(B)) at the increment side (e, h, ψ):
write a(B) = e·q(B) + ρ(B), 0 ≤ ρ(B) < e. Define

  DIG(B) := ( ρ(B) , z̄^{q(B)}·[R_anch(B)] ) ∈ (ℤ/e) × F_{r+1}ˣ,

where z̄ := z mod ψ_r is a UNIT of F_{r+1} = F_r[z]/(ψ_r) (ψ_r ≠ z). [Check against the
verifier's counterexample: e = 1, B = Φ_r, a = 1, R_anch = 1: DIG = z̄; DIG² = z̄² =
z̄ + 1 in F₂[z]/(z²+z+1) — exactly the observed remainder residual. Fixed.]

**Multiplicativity (the carry rule).** For B, B″ ∈ C_{r+1}, product re-developed as
Q·Φ_{r+1} + R_dev: anchors add before division (a(BB″) = a(B) + a(B″); division by
Φ_{r+1} shifts inner anchors by multiples of e — its slots sit at inner positions ≡ 0);
carrying the alignment: with κ := ⌊(ρ(B) + ρ(B″))/e⌋ ∈ {0, 1},

  ρ(R_dev) = ρ(B) + ρ(B″) − e·κ,   z̄-part(R_dev) = z̄^{κ}·z̄-part(B)·z̄-part(B″),

i.e. DIG is a homomorphism into the group (ℤ/e) ⋉_{z̄} F_{r+1}ˣ (the central extension by
the carry cocycle; at e = 1 — in particular at EVERY RS stage — the twist is trivial and
DIG = the aligned class z̄^{a}·[R_anch]). *Proof:* the ψ_r-adic division identity of
B2′.3(iii) applied to the ALIGNED absolute residuals (the z-offsets now included on both
sides; the (P6) absolute vectors make the alignment well-defined), plus the anchor
addition. Downstream: the level-(r+1) residual calculus carries F_{r+1}ˣ-digits TWISTED
by z̄-units; every argument that consumed only "digits are nonzero field elements"
(K1's distinct orders; the product-anchor uniqueness; the landing two-sidedness) is
unaffected — z̄-multiples of nonzero elements are nonzero; the ρ-classes become part of
the level-(r+1) ABSOLUTE ANCHOR data, i.e. the (P6) invariant at the next level is the
pair (outer slot, inner ρ)-tower, exactly the value-group coset bookkeeping of the
augmented valuation. ∎

### B2⁵.2 The RS transport with the anchor monomial [critical 2]

The absolute residual P(z) = z^{a}·R_anch(z) transforms under the substitution as a
WHOLE: P′(z) = (z + c̃)^{a}·R_anch(z + c̃). Canonical (support-anchored) transported
data, replacing rev 6's formula:

  a′ = ν := ord_z[ (z + c̃)^{a}·R_anch(z + c̃) ] = ord_z R_anch(z + c̃)
       (the (z+c̃)^a factor has nonzero constant term c̃^a),

On the multiplicity-μ descend stratum (R_anch = (z − c̃-conventions as fixed in B2⁗)…
with the stratum pattern (z − c̃)^μ h(z) as the anchored residual and anchor a): ν = μ,

F-polynomial of the parent data, now with the anchor-monomial factor EXPLICIT. [The
