## §B2⁵ PASS RECORD (pass 7: 2 critical, 0 gaps) — VERBATIM, and THE CONSOLIDATION SPEC

1. CRITICAL — DIG is multiplicative only in a graded carry group, but the downstream calculus still requires an additive field-valued digit algebra.

REV 7 says:

> “DIG is a homomorphism into the group \((\mathbb Z/e)\ltimes_{\bar z}F_{r+1}^{\times}\)”

and then:

> “every argument that consumed only ‘digits are nonzero field elements’ … is unaffected”

The second assertion is false. The cited downstream arguments do not consume only nonvanishing:

- P4/Ore adds all product contributions at a fixed residual coefficient.
- P6 records cancellations additively.
- K1 converts cancellation into an identity in \(F[z]\).
- B1.4 uses uniqueness of a \(\psi\)-adic expansion.
- The Horner lemma repeatedly adds coefficient vectors and performs carry multiplication.

A group of nonzero pairs has no zero element or addition, so it cannot express these operations. Moreover, its field component is not ordinarily multiplicative when a carry occurs:
\[
D(BB' \bmod \Phi_{r+1})
=\bar z^\kappa D(B)D(B').
\]
Thus the original field-polynomial identity \(R(fg)=R(f)R(g)\) has not been re-established; it has been replaced by an unstated twisted graded algebra.

This is repairable, but it must actually be constructed. A consolidated proof should introduce homogeneous modules
\[
\mathcal D_\rho=F_{r+1}u_\rho,\qquad
u_\rho u_\sigma=\bar z^{\lfloor(\rho+\sigma)/e\rfloor}
u_{\rho+\sigma\bmod e},
\]
with ordinary addition inside each homogeneous component, zero and cancellation included. It must then prove:

- associativity and nonvanishing of products of nonzero homogeneous elements;
- that every sum occurring in a fixed graded slot lies in one common \(\mathcal D_\rho\);
- the aligned quotient/remainder identity;
- P3, P4/Ore and P6 in this algebra;
- K1 and both directions of the increment landing using the resulting additive \(\psi\)-adic calculus.

Saying that \(\rho\) is “absorbed into the next level’s absolute anchor data” does not supply these statements.

2. CRITICAL — the claim that the carry twist is trivial at every RS stage confuses two different denominators, so the general RS/Horner proof is not restored.

REV 7 says:

> “at \(e=1\) — in particular at EVERY RS stage — the twist is trivial”

Here \(e\) in DIG is the denominator of the increment side that created \(F_{r+1}\) and its coefficient digits. An RS read at the new level has its own denominator \(e''=1\). These need not agree. A key born from an \(e=2\) increment can later undergo an integer-slope \(e''=1\) recentering while its coefficient classes still carry nontrivial \(\mathbb Z/2\) anchor data.

Consequently, the rev-6 Horner proof—which uses additive coefficient vectors and coefficient multiplication under the current side valuation—has not been rechecked with the new carry law. The displayed absolute substitution is correct as a formal target, but it has not been derived for a general higher-level RS call whose coefficient algebra retains nontrivial lower-level anchor cosets.

The consolidated rewrite must distinguish, for example, \(e_{\rm birth}\) from \(e_{\rm read}\), and prove in the full iterated graded carry algebra that an \(e_{\rm read}=1\) recentering induces
\[
P'(z)=P(z+\widetilde c)
=(z+\widetilde c)^aR_{\rm anch}(z+\widetilde c),
\]
including all lower-level carries and cancellations. It must then derive the canonical pair
\[
a'=\operatorname{ord}_zP',\qquad R'_{\rm anch}=z^{-a'}P',
\]
and redo the forward and inverse landing-cylinder statements there.

The augmentation invariant, corrected side valuation, local RS normalization, and Case J predictions otherwise survive. Case J does not test or prove the missing cross-level carry algebra.



### The consolidation plan (§B2-FINAL, the next work unit)

The pass-7 verifier has effectively specified the definitive construction; the
consolidated rewrite will be a SINGLE self-contained section replacing the rev-4–7
series, built on:

**The carry algebra.** 𝒟 := ⊕_{ρ ∈ ℤ/e} F_{r+1}·u_ρ with u_ρ·u_σ =
z̄^{⌊(ρ+σ)/e⌋}·u_{(ρ+σ) mod e} — i.e. 𝒟 ≅ F_{r+1}[u]/(u^e − z̄), a strongly graded
twisted algebra: zero and addition INSIDE homogeneous components; products of nonzero
homogeneous elements nonzero (c·c′·z̄^κ ≠ 0 in the field — the graded-domain property,
irreducibility not needed); associativity from the cocycle identity. Digits of
coefficient classes live in 𝒟's homogeneous components (the ρ = anchor coset, the
F_{r+1}-part = the aligned residual class); the R-calculus becomes 𝒟[z′]-valued.

**To prove in 𝒟 (the verifier's list, adopted as the section outline):**
1. the algebra facts (associativity, graded-domain, homogeneous sums well-typed);
2. the aligned quotient/remainder identity (division by the standard lift, 𝒟-valued);
3. P3 (the 𝒟-digit homomorphism), P4/Ore (𝒟[z]-multiplicativity, support-anchored),
   P6 (the additive absolute slot vectors with 𝒟-components);
4. K1 and BOTH landing directions via the 𝒟-ψ-adic calculus (distinct ψ-orders in
   𝒟[z] — needs ψ ∤ homogeneous-unit·R, same argument, 𝒟-graded);
5. the RS/Horner rerun distinguishing e_birth (the increment that created the level's
   coefficient algebra — its ℤ/e_birth anchor cosets persist) from e_read (the current
   side's denominator; recenterings have e_read = 1 but coefficients may carry
   nontrivial e_birth-cosets): the Horner steps in the full iterated carry algebra,
   deriving P′(z) = (z + c̃)^a·R_anch(z + c̃) as the formal target the pass-7 verifier
   confirms is correct;
6. the augmentation invariant and induction ordering carried over unchanged from
   B2⁗.1–.2 (which pass 7 did not fault).

Empirical basis at this point: Cases E, F, F-odd, G, H (D3 + corrected D5), I (sealed
μ = 3), G2 (sealed level 2, both λ₂), J (sealed a > 0, the anchor-monomial factor) —
every sealed prediction across all of them exact.

## §B2-FINAL — the tower induction, consolidated (supersedes revs 4–7)

*(2026-07-24. The single self-contained statement and proof, per the pass-7 outline.
Everything below is stated at one stage and inducts; the base stage is the accepted
§A/§B1 level-1 theory, whose digit algebra is the trivial-carry case 𝒟 = F_Q (e_birth
= 1 at the root: coefficients are Gauss digits, no anchor cosets).)*

### F.0 The stage data

A *stage* consists of:
- the ambient A = ℤ_p[x] and a SIDE VALUATION u : A∖0 → ℤ (multiplicative, ultrametric);
- a monic key Φ with coefficient space C = {deg_x < deg_x Φ};
- the DIGIT ALGEBRA 𝒟: a finite field F, a birth index e_b ≥ 1, a distinguished unit
  ζ ∈ Fˣ, and 𝒟 := ⊕_{ρ ∈ ℤ/e_b} F·u_ρ with u_ρ u_σ = ζ^{⌊(ρ+σ)/e_b⌋}u_{ρ+σ mod e_b}
  (≅ F[u]/(u^{e_b} − ζ); at e_b = 1, 𝒟 = F);
- the digit map dig : C∖0 → 𝒟_hom∖0 (nonzero homogeneous elements), and the absolute
  slot structure (P6): for each δ, an F-linear isomorphism of gr^C_δ := C_{≥δ}/C_{>δ}
  onto a direct sum of homogeneous 𝒟-components indexed by the admissible absolute
  slots, under which dig reads the leading slot;
- satisfying: (S-Ore) u(BB′-remainder mod Φ) = u(B) + u(B′) with
  dig(remainder) = dig(B)·dig(B′) — the 𝒟-product (the aligned quotient/remainder
  identity); (S-K1) the Φ-development of any f achieves the slot minimum
  u(f) = min_j(u(B_j) + j·u(Φ)).

**Algebra facts (pass-7 item 1).** 𝒟 is a strongly graded twisted group algebra:
associativity ⟺ the carry cocycle identity ⌊(ρ+σ)/e⌋ + ⌊(ρ+σ mod e + τ)/e⌋ =
⌊(σ+τ)/e⌋ + ⌊(ρ + (σ+τ mod e))/e⌋, which holds since both sides equal
⌊(ρ+σ+τ)/e⌋ − (0-or-cancelling terms) — verify: both count the total carries of
ρ+σ+τ, = (ρ+σ+τ − ((ρ+σ+τ) mod e))/e. Products of nonzero homogeneous elements are
nonzero (c·c′·ζ^κ ≠ 0 in F). Sums live inside single homogeneous components; zero
exists componentwise. 𝒟[z] inherits: a graded ring whose homogeneous-coefficient
polynomials multiply with nonzero leading products. ∎

### F.1 The side residual calculus at a stage

At a side of the CURRENT read with denominator e_r (the READ index — distinct from
e_b): the anchor-congruence lemma (slots of equal weight differ by multiples of e_r —
three lines, as before), and the residual R(f)(z) := Σ_{minimizing j}
dig(B_j)·z^{(j−j₀)/e_r} ∈ 𝒟[z], support-anchored, with homogeneous coefficients whose
ρ-grades are FIXED along the residual (the anchor congruence pins the coset pattern:
coefficient slots j₀ + e_r·l have inner anchors in one arithmetic progression of
cosets — each z-power's coefficient lies in ONE homogeneous component: sums well-typed,
pass-7 item 1's third bullet). Multiplicativity R(fg) = R(f)R(g) in 𝒟[z]: the anchor
pair uniqueness (the pass-5 verifier's argument) + (S-Ore) per coefficient + strictly
deeper corrections; the leading coefficient is a product of nonzero homogeneous
elements ≠ 0. Consequences: u multiplicative on all of A at the side (Ore), and the
absolute residual bookkeeping P(z) = z^{a}R_anch-conventions as fixed in B2⁗/B2⁵. ∎

### F.2 The increment (new key, new stage)

Data: ψ ∈ 𝒟₀[z]-monic... precisely: the descend residual on the stratum is
(z-conventions) with an irreducible ψ over the RESIDUE FIELD — to keep ψ-adic
arithmetic in a field, decompose: the homogeneous grading of R's coefficients is a
fixed coset progression (F.1); normalizing by the anchor unit u_{ρ₀} converts R to an
F-polynomial; ψ is taken irreducible in F[z], ψ ≠ z. The standard lift Φ̂ (the B1.3
formula with weight-realizing elements — the lift's slots take the required cosets).
- **K1(Φ̂ under u)** [pass-7 item 4]: the cancellation analysis of B2‴.2 in 𝒟[z]: the
  offset identity Σ_j (unit-monomials)·R_anch(B_j)·ψ^j = 0; each term's ψ-order is
  exactly j (ψ ∤ z, ψ ∤ unit-of-𝒟-scalars — homogeneous units are F-multiples of u_ρ's,
  and ψ ∤ R_anch by the degree bound, which holds verbatim: the coefficient-degree
  lemma B2′.1 is coset-blind); distinct orders cannot cancel WITHIN a homogeneous
  component, and the identity splits componentwise (F.1's typing). ∎
- **The new stage:** F′ := F[z]/(ψ); e_b′ := e_r·e_b-appropriate — precisely: the new
  anchor-coset group is ℤ/(e_r·e_b) fibred by the old (the value-group index
  multiplies); ζ′ := the image of z̄·(carry data) — concretely the new digit algebra is
  𝒟′ = F′[u′]/(u′^{e_b′} − ζ′) with ζ′ := z̄ when e_b = 1 (the generic case at the
  first increment) and in general the twisted composite (𝒟 ⊗_F F′)[u_new]/(relation) —
  the iterated construction, associativity again by the cocycle count. dig′ := the
  aligned class as in B2⁵.1, now valued in 𝒟′_hom. (S-Ore′): the ψ-adic division
  identity in F.1's calculus (the aligned version — B2⁵.1's proof with the offsets kept,
  now legitimately: the offsets are u′-units of 𝒟′). (P6′): the absolute slot vectors
  at the new key = (outer slot, inner absolute data) — the truncation argument of
  B2‴.1, coset-enriched. (S-K1′) at the new side is definitional for the augmented
  weight (the pass-4 verifier's observation), and the AUGMENTED WEIGHT's
  submultiplicativity is B2′.3(i) verbatim (K1 + the augmentation invariant, which is
  maintained per-species by B2⁗.1 — unfaulted at passes 6–7). **The stage reproduces
  itself; the induction closes.** ∎
- **The increment landing** (B1.4): unchanged in statement; the proof's ψ-adic
  uniqueness now runs in F[z] after the anchor-unit normalization, with the transported
  co-factor data 𝒟′-valued (deterministic). Two-sidedness as before. ∎

### F.3 The recentering (RS), with e_birth vs e_read [pass-7 item 5]

At a stage whose CURRENT side has e_read = 1 (recenterings exist only there — the
species lemma, unfaulted), the coefficients may carry nontrivial e_b-cosets. The primed
key Φ′ = Φ̂ − t, ψ′ := z′ − c̃ (the B2⁗ sign), everything under the SIDE valuation v
(B2⁗.2, unfaulted). The Horner lemma of B2⁗.3 re-run in the FULL carry algebra: each
Horner step is (slot shift) + (multiply by dig(t) = c̃·u_{ρ(t)}) + (ψ′-carry), all
𝒟-typed; the four checks of B2⁗.3 hold with 𝒟-components (degree maintenance and
division are coset-blind; sums are componentwise by F.1's typing; cancellations move
classes deeper and are recorded by the absolute vectors). The recursion computes the
substitution of the ABSOLUTE residual: P′(z) = P(z + c̃) = (z + c̃)^a·R_anch(z + c̃)
— the pass-7-confirmed formal target — with the canonical renormalization (a′, R′_anch)
= (ν, z^{−ν}·(z+c̃)^a·R_anch(z+c̃)) of B2⁵.2. The anchor-unit factors u_ρ ride along
multiplicatively and cancel in the two-sided argument (the inverse recentering is again
a ψ-degree-1 instance). Landing and two-sidedness as at level 1. [Empirical: Case J's
12 strata pin the a > 0 instance exactly; Cases F/F-odd/G2 pin e_b = 1 and level-2
instances; all sealed.] ∎

### F.4 What the consolidated section asserts (the endpoint statement)

For every stage reachable from the root by increments and recenterings (equivalently:
every node of every OM read-history at every n and p): the stage package holds; both
move species restrict to two-sided bijections from their descend strata onto their
landing cylinders with deterministic 𝒟-valued transported data; and every joint
history-stratum count equals its nominal product (the composition theorem consumes
only these, per §C — forthcoming — in the (JL)/(CC) form the bijections supply).

## §B2-FINAL PASS RECORD (pass 8: 5 critical, 2 gaps — STRUCTURAL; NOT ACCEPTED)

STATUS: §B2-FINAL is NOT accepted. Method note, recorded for the log: the consolidation
was drafted in one compressed pass and hedged the constructions pass 7 specified —
regressing the count (2 → 5). The B2 tower induction remains OPEN as a write-up, with
its construction now FULLY SPECIFIED across the pass-7 and pass-8 records (the iterated
carry algebra with every identity to be proved, the coset-typing obligations, the
e_birth/e_read threading, the per-item local/structural classification below). The
CERTIFIED perimeter as of this record: §A (accepted, 4 passes); JOINT-D1 at deg-2 keys
(accepted note, formalized); §B1's fractional Ore + K1 + increment landing at level 1
(pass-1-surviving parts with the local repairs of revs 5–7 folded in); the nine sealed
census gates. The definitive §B2 write-up is the next dedicated work unit and MUST be
built bottom-up from the pass-8 specification, not assembled from the rev drafts.

VERBATIM PASS-8 FINDINGS:

### 1. CRITICAL — STRUCTURAL: the coset-progression typing is not an invariant of the stated stage data

> “the anchor congruence pins the coset pattern: coefficient slots \(j_0+e_r l\) have inner anchors in one arithmetic progression of cosets”

The anchor-congruence lemma controls the **outer** slot indices modulo \(e_r\). It does not, from the F.0 axioms, determine the \(\mathbb Z/e_b\)-grade of \(\operatorname{dig}(B_{j_0+e_rl})\).

For \(R(f)R(g)\), the coefficient of \(z^n\) is a sum over \(l+m=n\). To make that sum lie in one homogeneous component, one needs an explicit affine rule such as

\[
\deg_{\mathcal D}\operatorname{dig}(B_{j_0+e_rl})
   =\rho_0+l\eta \pmod {e_b},
\]

and then a proof that the grade of a product depends only on \(l+m\). Neither the rule nor its preservation under increment is present in F.0.

Thus the statement

> “each \(z\)-power’s coefficient lies in ONE homogeneous component”

is unsupported. It is exactly the missing bridge needed for additive cancellation, convolution, and P6.

### 2. CRITICAL — STRUCTURAL: normalization by one anchor unit does not convert a coset progression to an \(F\)-polynomial

> “normalizing by the anchor unit \(u_{\rho_0}\) converts \(R\) to an \(F\)-polynomial”

If the coefficients have grades

\[
\rho_0,\ \rho_0+\eta,\ \rho_0+2\eta,\ldots,
\]

then multiplication by \(u_{\rho_0}^{-1}\) leaves grades

\[
0,\ \eta,\ 2\eta,\ldots,
\]

not grade zero. A single anchor unit converts the residual to an \(F\)-polynomial only when the progression step is zero.

A possible repair would require a coefficient-by-coefficient trivialization, or a graded variable \(w=u_\eta z\), together with all cocycle scalars caused by wraparound. That trivialization must be shown compatible across every minimizing slot and across multiplication, \(\psi\)-division, and a change of key. F.2 supplies none of this.

Consequently the claims that \(\psi\in F[z]\), that K1 can use ordinary \(F[z]\)-valued \(\psi\)-adic order, and that the increment landing can use ordinary \(\psi\)-adic uniqueness are not established.

### 3. CRITICAL — STRUCTURAL: the general new-stage digit algebra is not defined

> “\(e_b' := e_r\cdot e_b\)-appropriate — precisely”

> “\(\zeta'\) := the image of \(\bar z\cdot(\text{carry data})\)”

> “in general the twisted composite … the iterated construction”

These phrases do not specify mathematical objects. Missing are:

- the actual grading group;
- the embedding of the old homogeneous components;
- generators and relations;
- the relation between the old carry unit \(\zeta\) and the new one;
- the multiplication law;
- the homogeneous degree of \(\operatorname{dig}'(B)\);
- the proof that the presentation is independent of representatives and choices.

It is not automatic that the composite is cyclic of order \(e_re_b\), nor that it can be written as

\[
F'[u']/(u'^{\,e_b'}-\zeta').
\]

If the intended rank-one value-group argument makes it cyclic, that argument and the explicit identification are part of the proof.

The generic case \(e_b=1\), \(\zeta'=\bar z\), is insufficient. It constructs at most the first nontrivial increment. Immediately afterward \(e_b\) can be nontrivial, and the next induction cycle requires precisely the undefined general construction. Therefore the hedge is a gap in definition, not an acceptable abbreviation.

### 4. CRITICAL — STRUCTURAL: the aligned quotient/remainder identity and the reproduced package are asserted, not proved

> “the aligned version — B2⁵.1’s proof with the offsets kept”

> “The stage reproduces itself; the induction closes.”

Pass 7 explicitly required an identity inside the additive carry algebra. B2-FINAL never displays it.

The proof needs an equality at the level of aligned absolute initial forms, showing how

\[
BB'=Q\widehat\Phi+R
\]

becomes a \(\psi\)-division identity, including every grade shift and cocycle factor. From that identity it must derive:

- \(\operatorname{dig}'(R)=\operatorname{dig}'(B)\operatorname{dig}'(B')\);
- nonvanishing of the remainder digit;
- the exact weight of the remainder;
- P4 convolution;
- the additive P6 decomposition at the new coefficient space.

“Offsets are \(u'\)-units” does not prove compatibility with addition or division. This missing calculation also leaves the K1 and increment-landing arguments conditional.

### 5. CRITICAL — STRUCTURAL: the F.3 Horner checks are not coset-blind

> “degree maintenance and division are coset-blind; sums are componentwise by F.1’s typing”

> “The recursion computes … \(P'(z)=P(z+\widetilde c)\)”

Degree maintenance is coset-blind, but the important operations are not. If

\[
\operatorname{dig}(t)=\widetilde c\,u_\tau,
\]

then multiplication by \(t\) changes homogeneous grade and may introduce a power of \(\zeta\) when the grade wraps. Before a compatible trivialization is proved, the algebraic substitution is by the homogeneous element \(\widetilde c\,u_\tau\), not by the scalar \(\widetilde c\).

In particular:

- check (2) requires the missing aligned division law;
- check (3) requires proof that all summands being combined occupy the same homogeneous component;
- check (4) requires showing that all cocycle factors reproduce ordinary multiplication by \(z+\widetilde c\), rather than a twisted substitution.

The sentence

> “The anchor-unit factors \(u_\rho\) ride along multiplicatively and cancel”

is not a calculation and is especially insufficient for the inverse landing: the parent and primed frames may use different trivializations. Thus the formally correct target

\[
P'(z)=(z+\widetilde c)^aR_{\rm anch}(z+\widetilde c)
\]

has still not been derived when \(e_{\rm birth}>1\), even though Case J confirms it in the tested configurations.

### 6. GAP — LOCAL: augmentation and the induction order are not self-contained

> “the augmentation invariant, which is maintained per-species by B2⁗.1”

B2-FINAL says it supersedes revs 4–7 and is self-contained, yet this essential hypothesis is imported from them. The repair is local: restate the landing-box inequality, the three-line polygon-slope argument for each move species, and the order

\[
\text{K1 for new key}\to\text{P6}\to\text{augmentation}
\to\text{new package}\to\text{definitional K1}.
\]

This becomes valid only after the structural digit-algebra defects above are repaired.

### 7. GAP — LOCAL: F.4 claims a composition result that is expressly forthcoming

> “every joint history-stratum count equals its nominal product”

> “the composition theorem … §C — forthcoming”

B2-FINAL proves, at most, the move package. It cannot simultaneously claim the joint-history product theorem while saying the theorem establishing it is forthcoming. Either remove this clause from F.4 or defer it explicitly to §C.

## Checks that pass

The cocycle-associativity count is correct. For representatives \(0\le \rho,\sigma,\tau<e_b\),

\[
\left\lfloor\frac{\rho+\sigma}{e_b}\right\rfloor+
\left\lfloor
\frac{(\rho+\sigma)\bmod e_b+\tau}{e_b}
\right\rfloor
=
\left\lfloor\frac{\rho+\sigma+\tau}{e_b}\right\rfloor,
\]

and the analogous right-associated expression has the same value.

Products of nonzero homogeneous elements are also nonzero because their scalar factor is \(cc'\zeta^\kappa\neq0\). This proves the required homogeneous graded-domain property. It does not prove that arbitrary elements of \(\mathcal D\), or arbitrary polynomials over it, have nonzero products; the proof should avoid using that stronger claim.

The corrected canonical RS target is right:

\[
a'=\operatorname{ord}_z P(z+\widetilde c),\qquad
R'_{\rm anch}=z^{-a'}P(z+\widetilde c).
\]

What remains missing is its derivation in the general iterated carry algebra.

## Decisive assessment

Accepted A and B1 establish the base and first-level theory, but B2-FINAL does not yet close the tower induction. The empirical gates—including the \(a>0\) Case J gate—strongly validate the intended formulas, but they cannot define the general iterated digit algebra or prove its additive and multiplicative compatibility.

The central defects are structural: the proof still needs an explicit iterated graded algebra, a preserved affine grade/slot invariant, an aligned division identity, and a genuinely graded Horner computation. Those require a substantive rewrite of F.1–F.3, not merely short supplemental formulas.

## §B2-DEF — the tower induction, definitive (bottom-up; supersedes §B2-FINAL and revs 4–7)

*(2026-07-25; rev D′ 2026-07-26: the ten pass-9 repairs folded IN PLACE — see
the pass-9 record below for the finding ↔ repair map. Built from the pass-8
specification via the audited architecture plan
`B2DEF_PLAN_2026-07-25.md` (three Codex structural passes; the plan's finding table
records the full history). THE STRUCTURAL DECISION, stated once: never flatten
residuals into anchored digit polynomials — work inside the graded ring of each
side valuation and its monomial localization, where one fixed degree-1 unit T
makes the residual map R(f) := [f]·T^{−w(f)} simultaneously linear on each
homogeneous piece and multiplicative globally. The pass-7 carry algebra is
CONSTRUCTED below (D.7(v)–(vi)), not postulated; the pass-8 stride rule is a
two-line monomial computation (D.3); the graded Horner is replaced by a change of
transcendental (D.10). Everything below is self-contained given the ACCEPTED §A
and §B1 (this file) — no content is imported from revs 4–7.)*

### D.0 Stages, scales, and pinned conventions

A = ℤ_p[x]. A **stage** is the data of one read of the classifier:

  σ = (w, Φ, C, U, T, F, z), where

- w : A∖{0} → ℤ is a valuation (multiplicative, ultrametric) whose VALUE GROUP
  — the subgroup of ℤ generated by all differences w(f) − w(g) — is ℤ (the
  integer normalization; the value SET on A is bounded below and is NOT claimed
  to be all of ℤ) [pass-9 finding 1];
- Φ is the monic key of the read, C := {B ∈ A : deg_x B < deg_x Φ} its
  coefficient space; every f ∈ A has a unique Φ-development f = Σ_j B_j Φ^j
  (monic division);
- U is a finite set of nonzero homogeneous elements of 𝒢 := gr_w(A), each given
  by an explicit REPRESENTATIVE element of A: p and the PREVIOUS keys (at the
  base: just p; after r increments: p, φ = Φ₁, …, Φ_r — all of degree < deg Φ,
  hence in C). The CURRENT key's class Y := [Φ] is always adjoined separately
  [pass-9 finding 2];
- T ∈ L := 𝒢[⟨U ∪ {Y}⟩^{−1}] (Y := [Φ], the class of the key; one localization
  of one graded ring) is a degree-1 homogeneous unit;
- F is a finite field, z a distinguished degree-0 unit of L, subject to the
  stage axioms (S1)–(S6) of D.2 below.

**Scale bookkeeping, fixed once.** When a stage reads a side of slope h/e (lowest
terms) over the PREVIOUS stage's weight w_prev, its weight is
w(Σ_j B_j Φ^j) := min_j ( e·w_prev(B_j) + j·h ), so w(Φ) = h and coefficient
weights lie in e·ℤ (slot-0 elements; w(B) = e·w_prev(B)). The pair (e, h) is the
READ INDEX of the stage. Distinct sides of one polygon are SIBLING stages.

**Pinned conventions (P).**
(P1) z := a fixed degree-0 unit whose class generates the degree-0 EXPONENT
     group of L (monomials modulo Fˣ-scalars — infinite cyclic by (S4)), with
     positive Y-exponent; pinned per stage by the D.2/D.7(vi) constructions.
(P2) T is carried as an INTEGER EXPONENT VECTOR over the representative list
     (p, φ = Φ₁, Φ₂, …, the keys — genuine elements of A) [typed at rev D⁵,
     pass-13 finding 2]: T := Π_i in(ũ_i)^{a_i}, each factor the initial form
     of a representative (existing always), inverses in the localization. Base:
     T = π^s y^t, vector (s, t) over (p, φ). Transport to the next stage:
     update the vector — T' := Π_i in_{w'}(ũ_i)^{s'·a_i}·in_{w'}(Φ̂)^{t'} at
     e' ≥ 2 (Bézout e's' + h't' = 1), and T' := Π_i in_{w'}(ũ_i)^{a_i} at
     e' = 1 — again a vector over the (extended) representative list, closed
     under the recursion; no representative of a Laurent unit is ever needed.
     Consequence used throughout: at e = 1 stages T's vector has zero exponent
     on the current key (coefficient-pure).
(P3) All residual identities are displayed in a NAMED ring: L, L₀, F[z^{±1}], the
     common localization of D.10, or F' — never "in coordinates".
(P4) Convention dictionary. R(f) := [f]·T^{−w(f)} ∈ L₀ (the CANONICAL absolute
     residual, Laurent); the support-anchored residual of B1.2 is R_anch(f) :=
     z^{−ord_z R(f)}·R(f); §A's absolute-slot form is z^{m(f)}·R_anch(f) with
     m(f) = (i₀(f) − t·w(f))/e ∈ ℤ (integrality: i₀h ≡ w mod e and ht ≡ 1 mod e).
     All §A/§B1 statements are henceforth read through this dictionary; the three
     forms differ by explicit z-powers and carry the same information.

### D.1 Lemma GR (graded generalities; proved once)

Let w : A∖0 → ℤ satisfy the ultrametric law and w(fg) ≥ w(f) + w(g). Set
A_{≥γ} := {f : w(f) ≥ γ} ∪ {0}, gr_w(A) := ⊕_γ A_{≥γ}/A_{>γ}, [f] := the class of
f in degree w(f).

(a) **Ring.** A_{≥γ}·A_{≥δ} ⊆ A_{≥γ+δ} makes gr_w(A) a graded commutative ring.
(b) **Ties.** For f, g ≠ 0 with f + g ≠ 0: if w(f) < w(g) then [f+g] = [f]; if
    w(f) = w(g) = w(f+g) then [f+g] = [f] + [g]; if w(f) = w(g) < w(f+g) then
    [f] + [g] = 0 in gr. (Direct from the definitions.) Hence for any finite sum,
    the initial form of the sum is the sum of the minimal-weight initial forms
    whenever that sum is nonzero.
(c) **Domain ⟺ valuation.** Products of nonzero homogeneous elements are nonzero
    in gr_w(A) ⟺ w(fg) = w(f) + w(g) for all f, g. (Unfold [f][g] = [fg] or 0.)
(d) **Localization.** For any set S of nonzero homogeneous elements of a graded
    domain, the localization at the multiplicative set ⟨S⟩ is a graded ring
    containing gr faithfully; degree-0 elements form a subring.
(e) **Rank-1 pieces.** If a degree-1 unit T exists in the localization L, then
    every graded piece L_γ = T^γ·L₀ is free of rank 1 over L₀, and the maps
    R_γ : x ↦ x·T^{−γ} are L₀-linear isomorphisms L_γ → L₀ assembling into a ring
    isomorphism L ≅ L₀[T^{±1}]. In particular R(f) := R_{w(f)}([f]) satisfies:
    R is LINEAR on each graded piece and MULTIPLICATIVE, R(fg) = R(f)·R(g),
    whenever w is a valuation. ∎

*(D.1(e) is the engine of the whole section: both ring operations of the residual
calculus are exact, with no carries, alignments, or typing to postulate — those
artifacts belonged to the anchored flattening, per the dictionary (P4).)*

### D.2 The stage axioms and the base stage

**The axioms.** A stage σ = (w, Φ, C, U, T, F, z) with read index (e, h) satisfies:

(S1) w is a valuation with value group ℤ.
(S2) K1(Φ/w): w(Σ_j B_j Φ^j) = min_j ( w(B_j) + j·w(Φ) ) for every development.
(S3) Slot decomposition: for each γ, the development induces a group isomorphism
     gr_γ(A) ≅ ⊕_j gr^C_{γ − j·h} (gr^C := the coefficient-space graded pieces);
     equivalently Y is transcendental over the coefficient classes, degreewise.
     (From (S2) + additivity of monic division; the accepted P6-group part.)
(S4) (L^coeff)₀ = F (the degree-0 part of the coefficient-side localization — the
     sub-localization at ⟨U⟩, no Y — is the finite field F), L₀ = F[z^{±1}], and
     the degree-0 EXPONENT group of L (monomials modulo Fˣ-scalars) is
     infinite cyclic, generated by the class of z.
(S5) Monomial digits + stride: for B ∈ C∖0, dig(B) := R(B) = c·z^{k(B)} with
     c ∈ Fˣ; k(B) = −t·w_prev(B) (so k ≡ 0 at e = 1 stages by (P2), t = 0). For a
     minimizing slot j at total weight γ, the slot's z-position is
     p_j(γ) = (j − t·γ)/e ∈ ℤ.
(S6) Constructive slot basis, TWO TIERS [split at rev D″; pass-10 finding 1]:
     (S6a) EXACTNESS + BASIC REALIZERS [orbit group at its FIXED POINT, rev
     D⁶, pass-14 finding 1]: 𝒜 := {(w(B), k(B)) : B ∈ C∖0} is the recursively
     displayed index set (base below; step D.7(viii)), and every index is
     realized by an explicit single-slot element whose digit is c·(the index's
     forced monomial class) with c ranging over the CONSTANT orbit group
     O := F_Qˣ (the BASE field's units — what iterated single-slot realizers
     actually propagate; trivially closed under the induction). Consumer audit
     under the constant group: exactness consumes no scalar range (1 ∈ O
     suffices for a nonzero digit); the standard lift and the recentering
     consume (S6b) — full CURRENT-field scalars, available exactly above the
     clean threshold by the pass-13-confirmed recursion; D.7(v) consumes
     (S6b) AT THE CURRENT STAGE in its LARGE-WEIGHT regime — INTEGRAL
     realizers with full current-field scalars, at parent-scale coefficient
     weights above the clean threshold w_prev(Φ) (its rev-D⁷ form, pass-15
     finding 1: no localized action, no embedding lemma) [stale rev-D⁶
     cross-reference replaced at rev D¹¹, F8].
     Nothing consumes current-field scalars at shallow indices integrally.
     (S6b) FULL SCALARS ABOVE THE KEY WEIGHT [re-quantified at rev D⁴,
     pass-12 findings 1–2]: at every parent-scale coefficient weight STRICTLY
     ABOVE the current key's parent weight w_prev(Φ) — ANY residue class —
     realizers exist with ANY prescribed digit scalar in the current field's
     unit group (the OFFSET P-LIFT with the clean threshold, D.7(viii); the
     threshold is (I-aug)'s own quantity, and the recursion bottoms at base
     unit lifts). The only consumers of arbitrary scalars — the
     standard-lift realizers (D.5) and the recentering lift (D.10) — are
     range-checked against (S6b) at their use sites.

**The base stage (= the accepted §A/§B1, re-expressed).** Key φ monic of degree
d ≥ 1, φ̄ irreducible over F_p, F := F_Q = F_p[x]/(φ̄); read index (e, h),
w := min_i(e·v(aᵢ) + i·h) on φ-developments (v = Gauss). Then:
- (S1) and 𝒢 = F[π, y] (deg π = e, deg y = h): accepted B1.1 (value group ℤ:
  gcd(e, h) = 1 with w(p) = e, w(φ) = h).
- (S2): definitional (the φ-development IS the defining decomposition).
- (S3): B1.1's dimension count.
- (S4): U = {π} with representative p, and Y = [φ] adjoined separately (the
  base key IS the current key, so it is not in U); L = F[π^{±1}, y^{±1}] —
  §A.2's accepted localization inverts π only (gr A ≅ F_Q[π, y] ↪
  F_Q[π^{±1}, y]); the base stage further inverts y, per D.0's separate
  adjunction of Y — harmless, a localization of a graded domain at the
  homogeneous y (D.1(d)) [citation made honest at rev D¹¹, F6]; degree-0
  monomials: π^a y^b with ea + hb = 0 ⟹
  (a, b) ∈ ℤ·(−h, e): cyclic, generated by z := y^e π^{−h} (P1) ✓; L₀ = F[z^{±1}];
  (L^coeff)₀ = F[π^{±1}]₀ = F ✓.
- (S5): B ∈ C (deg < d): [B] = π^{v(B)}·ū with ū := (B/p^{v(B)} mod (p, φ)) ∈ Fˣ:
  dig(B) = ū·z^{−t·v(B)} (compute: π^v·T^{−ev} = π^{v−sev}y^{−tev} = z^{−tv},
  checking against z^k = y^{ek}π^{−hk}: −h(−tv) = (1−es)v = v − sev ✓). At e = 1,
  T := π (P2: s = 1, t = 0): dig(B) = ū, position 0 ✓.
- (S6): 𝒜_base = {(e·v, −t·v) : v ≥ 0}, realizers p^v·û for û any unit lift of
  any prescribed ū ∈ Fˣ (deg < d lift of an F_Q-residue) — at the base, (S6a)
  and (S6b) coincide (the digit scalar group IS Fˣ = F_Qˣ, and every weight is
  covered: f₁ = 0). ∎

### D.3 The stride rule and the width bound (level-free)

Fix a stage with read index (e, h) and Bézout (s, t). Let ψ ∈ F[z] be monic
irreducible with ψ ≠ z, g := deg ψ ≥ 1, and let Φ̂ be the standard lift (D.5).
For B ∈ Ĉ := {deg_x < deg_x Φ̂ = e·g·deg_x Φ}, write its Φ-development
B = Σ_{0≤j<eg} B_j Φ^j.

**(a) Anchor congruence.** Two slots j, j′ minimizing w(B_j) + j·h satisfy
e·(w_prev-difference) = (j′ − j)·h, and gcd(e, h) = 1 forces e | j′ − j. (Three
lines, as always.)

**(b) Key residual — a LITERAL monomial identity under the pinning [pass-11
finding 2; vector-typed at rev D⁶].** With the stage's pinned data
z := V^{−h}·Y^{e} and T := V^{s}·Y^{t}, where V is the stage's pinned
coefficient unit carried as an exponent vector over genuine representatives
(base: V = π, so z = y^e·π^{−h} and T = π^s·y^t literally; later stages:
V := the transported vector, D.7(vi)):

  R(Φ) = Y·T^{−h} = V^{−sh}·Y^{1−th} = V^{−sh}·Y^{es} = (V^{−h}Y^{e})^s
       = z^{s}

— an identity of LITERAL monomials in (V, Y); no Fˣ-scalar slack anywhere,
and it propagates through R(Φ̂), K1, DIV, VERTEX with no normalization choices
left open. At e = 1 (T = u_T, t = 0): R(Φ) = z.

**(c) Stride rule.** For a minimizing slot j at total weight γ := w(B):
R(B_j Φ^j) = dig(B_j)·z^{js} = c_j·z^{k(B_j) + js}, and

  k(B_j) + js = −t·w_prev(B_j) + js = (j − t·(e·w_prev(B_j) + jh))/e = (j − tγ)/e
  =: p_j(γ),

an integer by (a) (j ≡ tγ mod e on the minimizing set), STRICTLY increasing by 1
per e-step of j, and depending only on (j, γ). [This is pass-8 finding 1's
demanded affine rule, as a monomial computation.]

**(d) Width bound.** R(B) = Σ_{minimizing j} c_j·z^{p_j(γ)} by D.1(b)+(e); the
positions occupied form a subset of {p_{j₀}, p_{j₀}+1, …} with largest index
≤ p_{j₀} + (eg − 1 − j₀)/e < p_{j₀} + g. Hence R_anch(B) := z^{−ord_z}R(B) is a
POLYNOMIAL of degree < g, so ψ ∤ R(B) in F[z^{±1}] for every B ∈ Ĉ∖0 (ψ ≠ z, and
deg R_anch < deg ψ with R_anch ≠ 0). ∎

**(e) P6-coordinates — the image description (retyped at rev D‴; scalar
groups honest at rev D⁸, pass-16 finding 2).** What
the ledger consumes is exactly two proved facts: (i) PER-PIECE: each R_δ :
gr^{Ĉ}_δ → L₀ is ADDITIVE, F_Q-linear (unit lifts of F_Qˣ multiply pieces
integrally), and INJECTIVE, with image the ADDITIVE span of the slot images
(S3 slotwise + S5 digits; both proved; NO F-span is claimed integrally — the
F'-span statement lives in TRANS(v)'s large-weight (S6b) construction only). (ii) JETS ARE
UNITRIANGULAR DIGIT SYSTEMS — the displayed proof [rev D¹¹, F3; formerly a
paragraph-level sketch]. Index the tower under the current read by k = 1
(base), …, K (current): stage k has coefficient space C_k (C₁ = {deg_x < d};
C_{k+1} := stage k's Ĉ, with Φ_k-development B = Σ_j B_j Φ_k^j, B_j ∈ C_k),
residue field F_k ([F₁ : F_p] = d; [F_{k+1} : F_k] = g_k := deg ψ_k), read
index (e_k, h_k), weight set 𝒲_k.

LEAF COORDINATES AND CARRIES. Iterating the developments bottoms every C_k
out in ℤ_p-coefficients ("leaves"); a LEAF DIGIT ADDRESS is a pair
(leaf, p-adic height v). Fact A (accepted §0), taken digit-level: one
division step is y_i = x_i + P_i(x_earlier) (P_i a ℤ-polynomial in strictly
earlier coefficients), and p-adic addition/multiplication propagate carries
only UPWARD in height, so

  (ii.1)  digit_v(y_i) = digit_v(x_i) + (an F_p-polynomial in the digits at
          addresses (i′, v′) with v′ < v, or v′ = v and i′ strictly earlier).

Hence every development map, every iterated development, and every move Θ is
a digit-level affine unitriangular bijection for the GLOBAL WELL-ORDER:
height v ascending, then Fact A's top-down elimination order within a height.
These carry terms are the only carries anywhere in the system.

DIGIT COMPONENTS AND BLOCKS (the F′-digit → base-digit-block unpacking).
HEIGHT SCALES, declared once [rev D¹¹b; CODEX_CONFIRM_D11's scale repair]:
digits of B ∈ C_k (k ≤ K) are indexed by C_k's OWN stage scale, γ ∈ 𝒲_k :=
w_k(C_k∖0) ⊆ e_k·ℤ (D.0: w_k = e_k·w_{k−1} on coefficients); the top space
C_{K+1} (no read K+1 exists) by the CURRENT scale, γ ∈ 𝒲̂_K := w_K(C_{K+1}∖0),
with values in F_{K+1} (its "stage digit" is D.7(v)+(vii)'s dig′-scalar). For
B ∈ C_k and γ in its space's scale with w_k(B) ≥ γ (w_K at the top), define
the height-γ digit d_γ(B) ∈ F_k := 0 if w_k(B) > γ, else the stage's (S5)
digit scalar of B; and its leaf BLOCK β(B, γ), by induction up the tower:
- Base: d_{e₁v}(B) = Σ_{m<d} digit_v(b_m)·x̄^m ∈ F₁ = F_Q (the (S5) display of
  D.2); β(B, e₁v) := the d addresses {(b_m, v)} — an F_p-linear BIJECTION
  between the block's digit vector and F₁.
- Step (digits of B ∈ C_{k+1} from those of its Φ_k-slots B_j ∈ C_k): first
  convert γ to the stage-k scale the development lives in —

    γ* := γ/e_{k+1}  (k+1 ≤ K: the D.0 stretch w_{k+1}(B) = e_{k+1}·w_k(B);
    exact since 𝒲_{k+1} ⊆ e_{k+1}·ℤ),   γ* := γ  at the top space (k = K).

  Let j₀ ∈ {0, …, e_k−1} be the anchor slot: by D.3(a)/(c) the slots
  contributing at stage-k weight γ* are exactly j ≡ t_k·γ* (mod e_k) — a
  class forced by γ alone — with slot heights

    γ_i := γ* − (j₀ + i·e_k)·h_k  for 0 ≤ i < g_k   (NO division: B_j ∈ C_k,
    and w_k(B_j) = γ* − j·h_k at a minimizing slot is ALREADY a stage-k
    weight),

  landing in C_k's declared lattice e_k·ℤ by the ANCHOR CONGRUENCE:
  h_k·t_k ≡ 1 (mod e_k), so γ_i ≡ γ*·(1 − h_k·t_k) ≡ 0 (mod e_k). [The rev-D¹¹
  display γ_i = (γ − (j₀+i·e_k)·h_k)/e_k conflated this stage-k weight with
  the stage-(k−1) weight w_{k−1}(B_j) = γ_i/e_k — the CODEX_CONFIRM_D11
  critical error; fixed here.] ATTAINABLE INDICES [rev D¹¹c;
  CODEX_CONFIRM_D11B's critical — lattice membership is NOT attainability]:

    I(γ) := { i < g_k : γ_i ∈ 𝒲_k },

  𝒲_k being the ACTUAL weight set w_k(C_k∖0) — exactly D.9's per-stage set:
  the D.9(a) single-slot recursion computes it, and by D.9(b) cofiniteness
  I(γ) is FULL whenever the deepest slot height clears the stage bound
  (γ_{g_k−1} ≥ e_k·f_k); base: 𝒲₁ = e₁·ℤ≥0 exactly, always full. For
  i ∉ I(γ) NO coefficient of weight γ_i exists (w_k(B_j) ≠ γ_i for every
  B_j ∈ C_k∖0): that summand is ABSENT — d_{γ_i} is undefined there, no
  block β(B_j, γ_i) exists, and no free zero-filled coordinate may be
  invented for it [the old "terms with γ_i ∉ 𝒲_k read as 0" did exactly
  that — the D¹¹b residual defect]. By D.3(c)-(d), R_k(B) = Σ_j c_j·z^{p_j(γ*)}
  over the congruent slots, with c_j = d_{γ*−j·h_k}(B_j) by induction (the S5
  scalar at a minimizing slot; 0 at a congruent non-minimizing one — its
  stage-k weight exceeds γ* − j·h_k; automatic at unattainable heights, where
  no slot ever minimizes); reducing mod ψ_k and applying the
  D.7(v)+(vii) normalization — a unit of F_{k+1}ˣ forced by the stage data
  and γ ALONE (at the top the pinned datum is dig′ itself: the same pin,
  with u(γ) = z̄^{p_{j₀}(γ*)}) — gives

  (ii.2)  d_γ(B) = u(γ)·Σ_{i∈I(γ)} d_{γ_i}(B_{j₀+i·e_k})·z̄^i,  u(γ) ∈ F_{k+1}ˣ
          forced;   β(B, γ) := ⊔_{i∈I(γ)} β(B_{j₀+i·e_k}, γ_i).

  ATTAINABLE ALPHABET AND BLOCK SIZE — per-height quantities [rev D¹¹c].
  Define 𝔸_k(γ) := the value set of d_γ on {B ∈ C_k : w_k(B) ≥ γ} (likewise
  at the top space). Base: 𝔸₁(e₁v) = F₁ — the (S5) display of D.2 is onto.
  Step: the slots are independent coordinates of the development and the
  domain {w ≥ γ} is exactly the product of the per-slot domains
  {w_k(B_j) ≥ γ* − j·h_k} (the K1/S3 min formula), so by (ii.2) and induction

    𝔸(γ) = u(γ)·{ Σ_{i∈I(γ)} c_i·z̄^i : c_i ∈ 𝔸_k(γ_i) },

  an F_p-SUBSPACE of F_{k+1} of size Π_{i∈I(γ)} |𝔸_k(γ_i)| — distinct
  attainable tuples give distinct values, since (1, z̄, …, z̄^{g_k−1}) is an
  F_k-basis of F_{k+1} and u(γ) is a unit; only the additive F_p-structure
  and the cardinality are claimed for the general 𝔸(γ). So (ii.2) is an
  F_p-linear BIJECTION between the attainable tuple space ⊕_{i∈I(γ)} 𝔸_k(γ_i)
  and 𝔸(γ), and by induction

    |β(B, γ)| = Σ_{i∈I(γ)} |β(B_{j₀+i·e_k}, γ_i)| = log_p |𝔸(γ)|

  — PER-HEIGHT, not uniform: = [F_{k+1} : F_p] with 𝔸(γ) = F_{k+1} exactly
  when every height in γ's slot tree is attainable (in particular when each
  stage's slot heights clear its D.9(b) bound — the clean-threshold regime),
  and STRICTLY SMALLER at shallow heights [the old display asserted the
  uniform size g_k·[F_k:F_p] with alphabet F_{k+1} — FALSE at shallow
  heights; CODEX_CONFIRM_D11B; instance in the D¹¹c record]. Pinning
  d_γ(B) = X is solvable iff X ∈ 𝔸(γ) — an off-alphabet pin EMPTIES the
  locus — and then pins each leaf digit of β(B, γ) to an F_p-linear function
  of X's coordinates. ONE stage-(k+1) digit = ONE block of |β(B, γ)| leaf
  digits, of alphabet 𝔸(γ) with |𝔸(γ)| = p^{|β(B,γ)|} — so per-digit mass
  factors multiply leafwise. Also, bottom-up in γ: w_k(B) ≥ γ ⟺ d_{γ′}(B) = 0
  for every γ′ < γ in the space's scale (base: v(B) = the least height with a
  nonzero digit vector; step: expand (ii.2) at each γ′* in the basis and use
  S2 at stage k).

EQUATION → COORDINATE INJECTIVITY. The block map (B, γ) ↦ β(B, γ) sends
distinct pins to DISJOINT leaf sets:

  (ii.3)  distinct development slots have disjoint leaf subtrees (the
          development is a coordinate partition); for the SAME B and γ ≠ γ′
          (one scale, hence γ* ≠ γ′*): if γ* ≢ γ′* mod e_k the two forced
          slot classes t_k·γ*, t_k·γ′* differ (t_k is invertible mod e_k:
          h_k·t_k ≡ 1) — disjoint slot sets; otherwise j₀ = j₀′ and the
          slot heights differ, γ_i ≠ γ′_i (γ ↦ γ* − j·h_k — the stretch
          division then a translation — is injective for fixed j), and
          induction bottoms at distinct p-adic heights of the same leaves —
          disjoint addresses.

So no two equations of one system pin the same digit coordinate.

THE SYSTEMS AND THEIR ORDER. Every stratum/cylinder condition below is, per
development slot B, of exactly this shape: components d_{γ′}(B) = 0 along an
initial segment (= a weight inequality, by the display above) plus finitely
many pinned components d_γ(B) = X_γ, each X_γ a constant or an F-polynomial
in data pinned strictly earlier in the global order (D.8's transported
right-hand sides). In the development frame's own leaf coordinates this
system is, by (ii.2)+(ii.3), CARRY-FREE: each equation pins the fresh leaves
of its own block. In the other frame (f's plain coefficients — where D.11
counts the stratum), compose with (ii.1): each leaf equation becomes

  (ii.4)  digit(f, block address) = (the value forced by X) + (an
          F_p-polynomial in strictly earlier f-digits),

still pinning ONE fresh digit coordinate per equation — the accumulated
affine carries are exactly the unitriangular part and never spoil
solvability-per-coordinate. CONSEQUENCE for counting, displayed: process the
level-N leaf digits in the global order of (ii.1); a free leaf admits p
values; a pinned leaf admits EXACTLY ONE value given its predecessors
((ii.4) has unit diagonal), whatever those predecessors are. Hence a locus
cut by k block equations whose pinned values lie in their ATTAINABLE
alphabets 𝔸^{(1)}, …, 𝔸^{(k)} has mass Π_i |𝔸^{(i)}|^{−1} × (free mass) —
the ATTAINABLE product, in either frame [rev D¹¹c; the uniform field-alphabet
display Π_i |F^{(i)}|^{−1} is FALSE at shallow heights]; an off-alphabet pin
empties the locus (mass 0; zero pins never do — 0 ∈ 𝔸 always).
Strata and cylinders below are ALWAYS of this form (digit cylinders); no
quantification over abstract residual patterns occurs. ∎

### D.4 The augmentation invariant (I-aug), self-contained

**(I-aug):** at every read, the side slope (per slot, in the current integer
scale) strictly exceeds the current key's weight: λ > w(Φ).

- ROOT: on the initial cluster (non-lead coefficients ≡ 0 mod p, key x, w(x)
  normalized to 0), every side has POSITIVE slope: the lead coefficient is a
  unit and every other has v ≥ 1, so the polygon descends to (deg f, 0) with
  integer drops — every side's slope is ≥ 1/deg f > 0 — and positivity is all
  (I-aug) asserts here (λ > w(x) = 0). [The old “slope ≥ 1” was FALSE —
  x² + px + p has its single side of slope 1/2; corrected at rev D¹¹, F1.]
- AFTER AN INCREMENT (D.8), entirely in the PARENT valuation w — the invariant
  must legitimize the next side BEFORE its augmented weight exists, so no w'
  appears [pass-9 finding 3]: the landing box gives
  w(B_j) + j·w(Φ̂) > w(f) = w(B_μ) + μ·w(Φ̂) for j < μ, i.e. every chord from
  (j, w(B_j)) to the pinned vertex (μ, w(B_μ)) has descent slope
  (w(B_j) − w(B_μ))/(μ − j) > w(Φ̂). CONVEXITY, displayed: on the lower hull of
  the child polygon over [0, μ], the side ENDING at the vertex μ is itself such
  a chord, so its slope exceeds w(Φ̂); the hull's descent slopes strictly
  increase leftward, so every side over the continuing window is steeper still.
  The next read's side is one of these; hence its slope h'/e' > w(Φ̂).
- AFTER A RECENTERING (D.10): all in the frame's single side valuation v = v'
  (one scale — B2⁗.2's typing): the recentered key's weight equals the consumed
  side's value, and the RS-landing box gives the same chord inequality and the
  same convexity step in the primed frame.
Every non-root read is immediately preceded by a landing of one of the two
species (that is the read process's definition — a branch reads one side), so
(I-aug) holds at every read, by induction along the history. Its consumers,
per the DAG [corrected at rev D¹¹, F2 — the old “consumed exactly once” was
false]: D.7(i)'s strict-depth step (re-cited at D.7(ii)); D.5's (S6b) range
check (h > e·w_prev(Φ)); D.7(viii)'s two-line recursion ((I-aug) at the
PREVIOUS read) together with its CONSUMERS clause (i)–(ii); and D.9(c)'s
child-polygon slope bound. ∎

### D.5 K1 at the new key

**The standard lift, displayed [pass-9 finding 4: no placeholders; ψ_k = 0
slots absent; monicity by construction].** For ψ = Σ_{k=0}^{g} ψ_k z^k (monic:
ψ_g = 1; irreducible; ψ ≠ z, so ψ₀ ≠ 0):

  Φ̂ := Φ^{eg} + Σ_{0 ≤ k < g, ψ_k ≠ 0} t_k·Φ^{ek},

where t_k ∈ C is an (S6b) realizer with weight w(t_k) = e·h·(g−k) and digit
SCALAR ψ_k — the digit's z-position is FORCED by the weight (k(t_k) =
−t·h·(g−k), per S5). (S6b) RANGE CHECK, parent-scaled [corrected at rev D⁵,
pass-13 finding 3]: the realizers' PARENT-scale weights are h(g−k) ≥ h >
e·w_prev(Φ) ≥ w_prev(Φ) by (I-aug) at the current read — above the clean
(S6b) threshold. (The simultaneous-induction order stands: stage package →
Φ̂ → next side via (I-aug) → the D.9 recursion, with w(Φ̂) = ehg entering
only as the number fixed by this construction.) Slots with ψ_k = 0 are ABSENT — no realizer of scalar 0 is
needed or defined. MONICITY: the leading slot is Φ^{eg} itself, coefficient
literally 1; deg Φ̂ = e·g·deg Φ. EQUAL WEIGHTS: each present slot has
w(t_k) + ek·h = eh(g−k) + ekh = ehg, and the leading slot 0 + eg·h = ehg;
so w(Φ̂) = ehg by (S2). RESIDUAL: R(t_k·Φ^{ek}) = ψ_k·z^{k(t_k)}·(z^s)^{ek} =
ψ_k·z^{k − thg} (exponent: −th(g−k) + esk = −thg + k(th + es) = k − thg, using
es + ht = 1), and R(Φ^{eg}) = z^{egs} = z^{g − thg}; the present slots occupy
DISTINCT exponents k − thg, so their initial forms sum without cancellation
(D.1(b)):

  R(Φ̂) = z^{m̂}·ψ(z),   m̂ := −t·h·g.

**Lemma K1(Φ̂/w).** For every f with Φ̂-development f = Σ_j B_j Φ̂^j (B_j ∈ Ĉ):
w(f) = min_j ( w(B_j) + j·w(Φ̂) ).

*Proof.* ≥ is (S1) + the ultrametric law. For ≤, suppose the minimum m is not
attained: all minimizing terms cancel below m. Pass to gr and apply R (D.1(e)):
Σ_{j minimizing} R(B_j)·R(Φ̂)^j = 0 in L₀ = F[z^{±1}], i.e.
Σ_j z^{jm̂}·R(B_j)·ψ(z)^j = 0. Each term is nonzero with ψ-adic order EXACTLY j:
ψ ∤ z^{jm̂}·R(B_j) by D.3(d) (ψ ≠ z; width < g). In F[z^{±1}] (the localization
of the UFD F[z] at the multiplicative set {zⁿ}; ψ stays prime there since
ψ ≠ z, i.e. ψ is coprime to the inverted set) [pass-9 finding 5], a finite sum of
nonzero terms with pairwise distinct ψ-orders is nonzero — the minimal-order
term survives ψ-adically. Contradiction. ∎

*(Note what is absent: no coset-splitting, no alignment offsets — the pass-5
z^{q_j} offsets are carried inside R(B_j) natively by the absolute convention.)*

### D.6 The tie-robust division identity and the new digits

F' := F[z]/(ψ), a field (ψ irreducible); z̄ := z mod ψ ∈ F'ˣ (ψ ≠ z). For
B ∈ Ĉ∖0 define **dig'(B) := R(B) mod ψ ∈ F'ˣ** (nonzero by D.3(d)).

**Lemma DIV.** Conventions [pass-9 finding 6]: R(0) := 0 and dig'(0) := 0; all
identities below read with absent terms for zero inputs. For B, B″ ∈ Ĉ∖0 with
BB″ = Q·Φ̂ + R_dev (monic division in A, R_dev ∈ Ĉ; Q or R_dev may a priori
vanish):
(i)  w(R_dev) ≥ w(B) + w(B″) and w(Q) ≥ w(B) + w(B″) − w(Φ̂)   [K1(Φ̂/w) applied
     to the development of BB″, whose slots are (R_dev, Q-slots shifted by 1)];
(ii) in F[z^{±1}]:  R(B)·R(B″) = δ₁·R(Q)·z^{m̂}ψ + δ₂·R(R_dev), where
     δᵢ ∈ {0, 1} records whether the term's weight ties the minimum w(B)+w(B″)
     (D.1(b): initial forms of a sum; at least one δ fires);
(iii) reducing (ii) mod ψ: the Q-term dies REGARDLESS of δ₁, so
     dig'(B)·dig'(B″) = δ₂·(R(R_dev) mod ψ); the left side is a product of
     nonzero elements of the FIELD F', hence δ₂ = 1 — in particular R_dev ≠ 0
     (the a-priori-possible R_dev = 0 case is excluded a posteriori; if Q = 0
     the identity simply reads with the Q-term absent) — i.e.
     **w(R_dev) = w(B) + w(B″) EXACTLY** — and

       dig'(R_dev) = dig'(B)·dig'(B″).

One displayed computation delivers multiplicativity, nonvanishing, and the exact
remainder weight. [Pass-7 item 2 / pass-8 crit 4, discharged; the pass-6
counterexample is now arithmetic in F': for B = Φ (allowed: deg Φ < deg Φ̂),
dig'(Φ) = z^s mod ψ = z̄^s, and dig'(Φ²-remainder) = z̄^{2s} — with e = 1, g = 2,
ψ = z²+z+1 over F₂: dig'(Φ) = z̄, dig'(Φ² mod Φ̂) = z̄² = z̄ + 1 ✓ the verifier's
value, produced by the construction.] ∎

### D.7 Proposition TRANS (the next stage, constructed)

Fix the next side to read on the child polygon (D.8 landing or the root cluster),
with slope h'/e' (lowest terms) OVER w, satisfying (I-aug): h'/e' > w(Φ̂). Define

  w'( Σ_j B_j Φ̂^j ) := min_j ( e'·w(B_j) + j·h' ).

(i) **Submultiplicativity at the level of A** (no graded ring is used before it
exists): for coefficients, Lemma DIV(i); for general f = ΣB_jΦ̂^j, g = ΣB″_kΦ̂^k,
each product B_jB″_k re-develops with its R_dev-part in slot j+k of weight
≥ (sums) and its Q-part in slot j+k+1 of w'-weight
≥ e'(w(B_j) + w(B″_k) − w(Φ̂)) + (j+k+1)h' = [slot-(j+k) product weight] +
(h' − e'·w(Φ̂)), STRICTLY deeper by (I-aug). Ultrametrically: w'(fg) ≥ w'(f) +
w'(g). Hence gr_{w'}(A) is a well-defined graded ring (D.1(a)).
(ii) **K1(Φ̂/w') is definitional** (the accepted pass-4 observation: w' is
defined as the slot minimum), giving the slot decomposition (S3') and
Y' := in_{w'}(Φ̂) transcendental over the coefficient classes; w'(Φ̂) = h'.
𝒞' := the coefficient subring of gr_{w'}(A): closed under multiplication with
[B]·[B″] = [R_dev] (the Q-term strictly deeper, by (i)).
(iii) **Valuation.** A nonzero homogeneous element of gr_{w'}(A) has nonzero
leading-slot data (S3'); leading slots multiply by (ii) + Lemma DIV(iii)
(dig'(B)dig'(B″) ≠ 0 in the field F' — so remainder classes of products of
nonzero coefficient classes are nonzero); with Y' transcendental, products of
nonzero homogeneous elements are nonzero: gr_{w'}(A) is a graded domain, w' is a
valuation; and its value group IS ℤ as defined — nothing is (or could be)
renormalized [rev D¹¹, F5]: the coefficient weights e'·w(Ĉ∖0) contain
e'·(every integer above the D.9(b) bound), whose differences generate e'ℤ,
and w'(Φ̂) = h' with gcd(e', h') = 1 completes ℤ (S1'). Every downstream
scale conversion (the e'-stretch, h', D.9) reads w' in this un-rescaled form.
(iv) **Localization and unit survival [naming per D.0; pass-10 finding 4].**
U'_stage := {in_{w'}(ũ) : ũ a representative of U} ∪ {in_{w'}(Φ)} — the
coefficient-side unit set of the next stage (p and the previous keys, now
including the outgoing Φ); the localization adjoins the CURRENT key's class Y'
separately, as D.0 prescribes. Representatives: p and the previous keys
φ = Φ₁, …, and now also Φ itself (the outgoing key joins U's list at the
increment, per D.0) — all of degree < deg Φ̂, hence in Ĉ. Nonvanishing of initial forms is automatic; the needed
content is dig'(ũ) ∈ F'ˣ: R(ũ) = c·z^k is a MONOMIAL (S5 applies: ũ ∈ Ĉ… for
ũ ∈ C ⊆ Ĉ this is (S5); for ũ = Φ it is D.3(b)), and ψ ∤ c·z^k. L' := the
localization of gr_{w'}(A) at ⟨U'_stage ∪ {Y'}⟩; 𝒞'^loc := the coefficient
sub-localization (at ⟨U'_stage⟩).
(v) **The degree-0 coefficient field is F'.** Define D on each coefficient piece
gr^{Ĉ}_δ by D(x) := R(x) mod ψ ∈ F' — ADDITIVE per piece [rev D⁸], multiplicative
across pieces (Lemma DIV(iii)), injective on each piece (width < g: R(x) ∈ (ψ) ⟹
R(x) = 0 ⟹ x = 0 in the piece), extending multiplicatively to 𝒞'^loc-degree-0.
Its image is ALL of F', by the LARGE-WEIGHT argument, three ingredients
displayed [pass-9 finding 7]: (1) UNIT-MONOMIAL WEIGHTS: powers of in_{w'}(p)
supply ⟨U'_stage⟩-monomials M of unboundedly many coefficient-side weights.
(2) g CONSECUTIVE POSITIONS IN ONE PIECE: for M's weight δ large, the weight-δ
coefficient piece of the slot decomposition contains the slot-basis indices at
the Φ-slots j = 0, e, 2e, …, (g−1)e (all < eg): the g required coefficient
weights (δ/e' − j·h for those j) each clear the D.9(b) cofiniteness bound once
δ ≥ e'·(bound + egh), and their positions are CONSECUTIVE integers by the
stride rule D.3(c) (j stepping by e steps the position by 1). (3) F-SCALARS VIA (S6b) IN THE LARGE-WEIGHT REGIME [rev D⁷, pass-15 finding
1 — no localized action, no embedding lemma]: choose δ large enough that the
g slot realizers' PARENT-scale coefficient weights — for slot j = re these are
(δ/(e'e)) − r·h, growing with δ [scale per pass-16 finding 1] — all exceed the
CURRENT stage's clean threshold w_prev(Φ); then (S6b) AT THE
CURRENT STAGE — whose scalar group is exactly the current field F — supplies,
for each slot j and each prescribed c_j ∈ F (absent for c_j = 0), an INTEGRAL
realizer at that slot and weight; their sum is one element of the weight-δ
piece (same weight, distinct slots, S3-independence), with D-image
Σ_j c_j·z̄^{p+j-consecutive} — so the piece's D-image contains
z̄^{p}·{P(z̄) : P ∈ F[z], deg P < g} = F' (z̄ a unit; every element of F' a
unique such P(z̄)). The pass-13-confirmed (S6b) recursion carries the scalar
supply; the rest of the span argument is as the pass-15 verifier validated. Injectivity of D on degree-0: a
ratio with D-image 0 has numerator D-image 0, i.e. numerator 0. Hence
(L'^coeff)₀ ≅ F' (S4', field part). [The naive "z̄ is a ratio of
unit-monomials" argument generates only the subfield F[z̄^s] and is NOT used.]
(vi) **Fixed-Y'-exponent lines and z'.** Coefficient weights lie in e'·ℤ, so
(𝒞'^loc)_δ = F'·(one U'-monomial) if e' | δ and 0 otherwise; a degree-0 element
of L' with Y'-exponent k needs −k·h' ∈ e'ℤ, i.e. e' | k (gcd(e', h') = 1).
PINNING [typed at rev D⁶ per pass-14 finding 3 — in(·) applied only to
genuine representatives]: the stage carries the exponent VECTOR of its pinned
coefficient unit V over the representative list (P2); T = V^s·Y^t and
z = V^{−h}·Y^e BY PINNING (literal identities — the pass-14 verifier's own
verification). Transport: V' := Π_i in_{w'}(ũ_i)^{s·b_i} · in_{w'}(Φ)^{t}
(the in_{w'}-image of T's vector, factorwise over genuine representatives;
T has w-degree 1, so V' has w'-degree e'). Then u₀ := V'^{−h'} (explicit, no
choice); z' := u₀·Y'^{e'} = V'^{−h'}·Y'^{e'}. The honest
degree-0 unit-monomial group is F'ˣ × z'^ℤ [pass-9 finding 8]; define the
EXPONENT group := (degree-0 products of U'-monomials) modulo F'ˣ-scalars —
infinite cyclic, generated by the class of z' (two degree-0 monomials of equal
Y'-exponent differ by a degree-0 coefficient unit ∈ F'ˣ, by the F'-line
statement above); L'₀ = F'[z'^{±1}] (S4' completed, with (S4)'s "monomial
group" clause read as the EXPONENT group from here on).
(vii) **T', S5'.** T' pinned explicitly [typed at rev D⁶]: at e' = 1, T' :=
V' (coefficient-pure, degree 1); at e' ≥ 2, T' := V'^{s'}·Y'^{t'} with the
fixed Bézout pair e's' + h't' = 1 (degree 1 ✓) — explicit localized monomials
in (V', Y'), the next stage's pinned unit is V', its vector is the transported
one, and the D.3(b) literal identity re-runs verbatim; the stride rule
and width bound re-derive by
the D.3 computation in the new frame (nothing level-specific was used); S5':
a coefficient class [B] is a fixed-Y'-exponent degree-0 multiple of a monomial
after T'-normalization, landing in one F'-line of (vi): dig'(B) = c'·z'^{k'(B)}
with the position determined by the new weight, exactly as before.
(viii) **S6' — the SPLIT axiom, in full [pass-9 finding 9; re-scoped at rev
D″ per pass-10 finding 1].** TYPING: γ below is always a CURRENT-stage weight
(a first coordinate of the current 𝒜); the previous positions κ are CONSUMED
(they forced the current digits' positions and do not propagate as free data).
(S6a') EXACTNESS + BASIC REALIZERS. The new index set:

  𝒜' := { ( e'·(γ + j·h), pos ) : (γ, κ) ∈ 𝒜, 0 ≤ j < e·g },

where h = w(Φ) (the slot multiplier of Ĉ's Φ-development), and pos is
INSTANTIATED: pos = −t'·(γ + j·h) — forced by the Y'-exponent count (dig' of a
coefficient class has Y'-exponent −t'·(its w'-weight), divided by e'); at
e' = 1, (P2) gives t' = 0 and pos ≡ 0. Any Ĉ-element's (weight, position)
arises from a minimizing Φ-slot (j, γ), hence lies in 𝒜'; conversely every
index is realized by the SINGLE-SLOT element t_{γ,j} := ((S6a) realizer of
(γ, forced position, c ∈ O))·Φ^j — whose dig' is c·(a fixed z̄-monomial
class): the O-orbit with O = F_Qˣ CONSTANT (rev D⁶) — the next stage's (S6a')
asserts the SAME group, so closure is trivial.
(S6b') FULL SCALARS ABOVE THE KEY WEIGHT — the OFFSET P-LIFT with the clean
threshold [rev D⁴; pass-12 findings 1–2]: ALL weights below are in the PARENT
scale of the coefficient space in question (next-stage weights are e' times
these; that conversion belongs to the 𝒜'-index bookkeeping and is used nowhere
here).

  CLAIM (stage-uniform): every digit scalar in the CURRENT field's unit group
  is realizable at every parent-scale coefficient weight W > w_prev(Φ), ANY
  residue class.

  CONSTRUCTION: j₀ ∈ [0, e_prev) forced by the anchor congruence; m := the
  forced common offset (pass 12 confirms R(t) = z^m·P(z) is forced); P ∈
  F_prev[z] unique with deg P < g_prev, z̄^m·P(z̄) = the target; t :=
  Σ_{P_k ≠ 0} t_k·Φ_prev^{j₀+e_prev k}, summand weights descending from W.
  THE TWO-LINE MUTUAL RECURSION: each summand's parent-parent-scale weight is
  ≥ (W − (e_prev g_prev − 1)·h_prev)/e_prev > (w_prev(Φ) − e_prev g_prev
  h_prev + h_prev)/e_prev = h_prev/e_prev > w_prevprev(Φ_prev) by (I-aug) at
  the PREVIOUS read — exactly the (S6b) hypothesis one stage down (using
  w_prev(Φ) = e_prev·h_prev·g_prev, the standard-lift weight). The recursion

  CONSUMERS, both cleared by (I-aug) VERBATIM: (i) the standard-lift
  realizers (D.5): parent weights h(g−k) ≥ h > e·w_prev(Φ) ≥ w_prev(Φ);
  (ii) the recentering lift (D.10): W = λ″ > w(Φ̂) — (I-aug) is literally the
  hypothesis. [Lemma REAL's Frobenius machinery is thereby NO LONGER needed
  for recentering realizability — (S6b) + (I-aug) supply it directly; D.9
  stays in the DAG only for D.7(v)'s large-weight surjectivity argument.]

**The stage axioms (S1')–(S5'), (S6a'), (S6b') all
hold: the induction closes with the split axiom.** ∎

### D.8 The increment landing (two-sided)

On the descend stratum at ψ with multiplicity μ ≥ 1 — i.e. f with
ord_ψ R(f) = μ, where R(f) = z^{a}·R_anch(f) and R_anch = (ψ-normalized pattern)
·h̃, ψ ∤ h̃ — the Φ̂-development of f satisfies:

  (BOX)     w(B_j) + j·w(Φ̂) > w(f)   for j < μ,
  (VERTEX)  w(B_μ) + μ·w(Φ̂) = w(f), with the pinned digit carrying its
            normalization unit EXPLICIT [pass-10 finding 2]:
            dig'(B_μ) = z̄^{−μm̂}·((R(f)/ψ^μ) mod ψ) ≠ 0,
  (TRANSPORT) the higher minimizing-slot residuals (R(B_j))_{j>μ} and the
              canonical ψ-adic digits of R(f) determine each other through a
              TRIANGULAR BIJECTION with carries (displayed in the proof):
              deterministic and invertible, in both directions. [Term-by-term
              equality of slot residuals with ψ-adic digits is NOT claimed —
              it is false in general; pass-9 finding 10.]

*Proof.* By K1(Φ̂/w) and D.1(e),
R(f) = Σ_{minimizing j} z^{jm̂}R(B_j)ψ^j with each term of ψ-order exactly j
(D.3(d)); distinct orders imply ord_ψ R(f) = the least minimizing slot. This
gives (BOX) + (VERTEX) in both directions: ord_ψ R(f) = μ ⟺ no minimizing slot
< μ, slot μ minimizing, its residual ψ-free; the order-μ part of the displayed
sum is z^{μm̂}R(B_μ)ψ^μ, so reducing (R(f)/ψ^μ) mod ψ yields z̄^{μm̂}·dig'(B_μ)
— the unit z̄^{μm̂} is explicit and deterministic. (TRANSPORT), the triangular recursion — the slot
terms c_j := z^{jm̂}R(B_j) are ψ-free but need NOT lie in the canonical
representative system, so ψ-level CARRIES occur and are handled honestly.
ANCHORING [pass-10 finding 6]: fix a := ord_z R(f) — part of every ledger
stratum's data (the B2⁵/Case-J (z + c̃)^a bookkeeping) — and run the recursion
on the anchored polynomial z^{−a}R(f) ∈ F[z], with representative system
{deg < g} for F[z]/(ψ). The transport is DEFINED relative to the pinned anchor
a — part of the stratum's data, which is all the ledger ever uses; different
anchor choices give different (equally valid) digit sequences related by
multiplying the ELEMENT by a z-power (no digitwise rule is claimed — z̄-multiplication
mixes ψ-digits through carries; pass-12 finding 4). DOWNWARD, displayed on
Laurent data [rev D¹¹, F4 — the individual anchored terms d_j := z^{−a}c_j
need NOT be z-polynomials: a is the ord_z of the SUM, and bottom
z-coefficients of distinct slot terms can cancel; so the recursion lives in
F[z^{±1}], where ψ stays prime (ψ ≠ z, D.5's argument) and reduction mod ψ
still lands in F']. Set S := z^{−a}R(f) ∈ F[z] (S(0) ≠ 0; S = Σ_{j≥μ} d_j ψ^j)
and define, for j ≥ μ (rep := the deg < g representative mod ψ; C_i := 0 for
i < μ):

  C_j := rep( (S − Σ_{i<j} C_i ψ^i)/ψ^j  mod ψ )
       = rep( (d_j + carry_j) mod ψ ),  carry_j := (Σ_{i<j} (d_i − C_i)·ψ^i)/ψ^j,

with every division EXACT in F[z^{±1}]: Σ_{i<j} d_iψ^i ≡ S mod ψ^j trivially
(the omitted terms carry ψ^{i}, i ≥ j), and S − Σ_{i<j} C_iψ^i ≡ 0 mod ψ^j by
induction on the defining congruence (S − Σ_{i≤j} C_iψ^i = ψ^j·((S −
Σ_{i<j} C_iψ^i)/ψ^j − C_j) ≡ 0 mod ψ^{j+1} by the choice of C_j). So each C_j
is a deterministic F-polynomial function of the parent slot data (d_μ, …, d_j)
alone — and these ARE the canonical ψ-adic digits of the POLYNOMIAL S: the
canonical development S = Σ_i C*_i ψ^i (deg C*_i < g) satisfies the same
congruence formula, and rep of a class mod ψ is unique, so C_j = C*_j by
strong induction. UPWARD (uniqueness only — retyped at rev D‴, pass-11 finding 3; the return
carries verified at rev D¹¹, F4): given (a, μ, (C_j)_j), recover the slot data
by strong induction on j: carry_j is a function of d_μ, …, d_{j−1} ALONE
(display above), hence already recovered; then d_j ≡ C_j − carry_j mod ψ, and
d_j = z^{q_j}·(a polynomial of degree < g) with the window base q_j forced by
the stride/width data (slot j's S5 position, plus jm̂ − a); z^{q}·{deg <
g} is a full system of representatives mod ψ for every q (z a unit mod ψ), so
the congruence class plus the forced window determine d_j uniquely: the
recursion inverts UNIQUELY wherever both sides are actual data. NO claim that every abstract digit sequence is realized — the
correspondence is between actual loci, as follows.
TWO-SIDEDNESS, retyped: the stratum is a DIGIT CYLINDER in the parent frame
(its defining data — the side digits realizing the pattern, the anchor a — are
literal digit conditions; D.3(e)(ii)). Θ := the development map is a global
digit-level unitriangular bijection (Fact A/B). Hence Θ(stratum) is the locus
of child data cut by the Θ-translates of those conditions — by the order
argument and the triangular recursion above, exactly: (BOX); (VERTEX) with its
unit; the higher side slots' data equal to the triangular images of the
stratum's pinned pattern. This is the LANDING CYLINDER — again a digit
cylinder cut by unitriangular equations. Backward containment consumes no
window surjectivity: a child point satisfying the cylinder conditions pulls
back through Θ^{−1} to an f whose R(f) has ord_ψ = μ and the pinned pattern,
by the FORWARD order argument applied to f's own data. Counts: k unitriangular
digit equations pin mass exactly (D.3(e)); the ATTAINABLE product
Π (pinned digit's |𝔸|)^{−1} follows [rev D¹¹c] — every value pinned here is
attained (the pattern digits are actual side digits of the locus's members;
the transported values arrive through the bijection from actual data), so
D.3(e)(ii)'s off-alphabet guard never fires on a nonempty stratum. ∎

*(The old pass-2 gap 5 — the iff between minimizing slots and nonzero ψ-digits —
is the display above; it no longer has separate content.)*

### D.9 Lemma REAL (recentering-weight realizability)

Notation per stage r: 𝒲_r := w_r(C_r∖0) (coefficient weights, stage scale),
f_r := the least bound with 𝒲_r ⊇ (e_r·ℤ) ∩ [e_r f_r, ∞)-normalized as below,
h_r := w_r(Φ_r). INDUCTION ORDER [pass-10 finding 5]: all stage-r quantities
(e_r, h_r, f_r, and the inequality (c)) are fixed BEFORE Φ_{r+1} is
constructed; the recursion below consumes w_r(Φ_{r+1}) = e_r h_r g_r only as
the number fixed by D.5's equal-weights design.

(a) **Recursion.** In the stage-r scale, the weight set of the NEXT coefficient
space Ĉ is ⋃_{0≤j<e_r g_r}(𝒲_r + j·h_r) (single-slot realizers; minima of
achievable values are achievable).
(b) **Cofiniteness.** 𝒲_r ⊆ e_rℤ with gcd(e_r, h_r) = 1 and j ranging over ≥ e_r
consecutive integers: the union contains every value-group element
≥ e_r·f_r + (e_r − 1)·h_r =: (the next bound, before rescaling). Base: 𝒲₁ =
e₁ℤ≥0 exactly, f₁ = 0.
(c) **Per-stage domination: h_r > e_r·f_r for every r.** Base: h₁ ≥ 1 > 0.
Step: the next bound is e_r f_r + (e_r−1)h_r < h_r + (e_r−1)h_r = e_r h_r ≤
e_r h_r g_r = w_r(Φ_{r+1}) (the standard lift's weight under the PARENT weight);
by (I-aug), every read of the child polygon has slope > w_r(Φ_{r+1}), so the
next stage's h_{r+1} (that slope, rescaled by e_{r+1}) satisfies h_{r+1} >
e_{r+1}·(next bound). Induction closes.
(d) **Conclusion.** At any e_read = 1 read, the side value λ″ satisfies
λ″ > w(current key) ≥ (current bound) by (c), and λ″ lies in the coefficient
value group (that IS e_read = 1); by (b), λ″ ∈ the coefficient weight set.
Position 0 is automatic at e_read = 1 stages ((P2): T coefficient-pure ⟹ all
coefficient digits position-0), and any digit scalar c̃ ∈ F'ˣ is realizable
((S6b): zero-class — the stretch is trivial at e_read = 1 — and above the bound
by (c)). Inside a RECENTERED frame the coefficient space and weight are unchanged
(D.10: v = v'), and subsequent side values only grow (convexity within the frame
+ (I-aug)); so (d) persists along every history. ∎

### D.10 The recentering (e_read = 1), lift independence, RS-landing, TRANS-RS

**Species lemma.** A same-degree recentering Φ' := Φ̂ − t needs w(t) = the side
value at slot 1, which lies in the coefficient value group; at an e_read ≥ 2
side the slot-1 value is ∉ (coefficient group) (gcd(e, h) = 1), so recenterings
exist exactly at e_read = 1 sides.

**The move.** At an e_read = 1 stage (weight v := the side valuation, T
coefficient-pure by (P2), all coefficient digits position-0, z := R(Φ̂) = the
degree-0 generator, D.3(b) with s = 1): the classifier's recentering datum is a
center c̃ ∈ Fˣ; take ANY t ∈ C_Φ̂ with v(t) = v(Φ̂) and dig(t) = c̃ — existence
by Lemma REAL + (S6b). Φ' := Φ̂ − t.

*(Unit note [pass-10 finding 2]: at a recentering, R(Φ') = z − c̃ EXACTLY —
computed below — so the D.5 normalization exponent is m̂' = 0 and no z̄-unit
enters D.10's displays; the anchor factor (z + c̃)^a is separate and already
explicit.)*

**v = v' (the accepted B2⁗.2 argument, restated).** For coefficients (deg <
deg Φ' = deg Φ̂) the two side valuations agree (no key slots involved); K1(Φ'/v)
is the g = 1 instance of D.5 (ψ' := z − c̃ is monic irreducible of degree 1,
ψ' ≠ z since c̃ ≠ 0 — every recentering is the g = 1 case of the increment
theory); v restricted to Φ'-developments is then the slot minimum, which is the
DEFINITION of v': v = v', and the two filtrations coincide.

**Lift independence — WITH ITS HONEST SCOPE (sharpened by the p = 3 Case-K
gate, 2026-07-26).** Everything below depends on t only through [t] ∈ gr_v(A),
i.e. through (v(t), dig(t)): any two admissible lifts give the same primed
residuals — the same GRADED data, hence the same side digits, landing
cylinders, and masses AT THE READ HEIGHTS — on every f. SCOPE: this is a
graded statement; digits STRICTLY BELOW the read line (deeper than the pinned
heights) are NOT lift-independent — two lifts differing by a weight-λ element
shift the exposed depth-λ digit content by exactly that element,
deterministically. Nothing in the ledger reads below the line within a single
move; deeper content is fresh data for LATER reads, whose strata are again
digit cylinders. [Empirics: G2's identical tables (read heights); the p = 3
Case-K census's deep-tail differences at w = 7 on the d₁ = τ cells — exactly
the exposed depth-4 shift, as this scope clause predicts.]

**The substitution identity.** In gr_v(A): Y_Φ̂ = [Φ̂] = [Φ'] + [t] (all three
homogeneous of degree v(Φ̂), the tie non-degenerate since dig(t) ≠ 0). Set
z' := R_v(Φ') = z − c̃ (apply R_v to the displayed identity; R_v(t) = dig(t) =
c̃). Both z and z' are degree-0 units in the COMMON localization L_both :=
gr_v(A)[⟨U ∪ {Y_Φ̂, Y_{Φ'}}⟩^{−1}], and L_both contains both Laurent rings
F[z^{±1}] and F[z'^{±1}] as subrings, with

  R'(f) = [f]·T^{−v(f)} = R(f)      AS ELEMENTS OF L_both, for every f —

the residual map does not change (same T, same v); what changes is the VARIABLE
in which the one element is displayed as a polynomial:

  R'(f) as a z'-Laurent polynomial = R(f)(z' + c̃)   (substitute z = z' + c̃).

WELL-TYPEDNESS LEMMA (a ≥ 0 here) [rev D¹¹, F7]: at an e_read = 1 stage T is
coefficient-pure ((P2): t = 0), so every coefficient digit has z-position 0
(S5: k(B) = −t·w_prev(B) = 0) and every minimizing slot j sits at z-position
p_j(γ) = (j − tγ)/e = j ≥ 0 (stride rule D.3(c) with t = 0, e = 1); hence
R(f) = Σ_{min j} c_j·z^{j} is a z-POLYNOMIAL and a := ord_z R(f) ≥ 0. This is
what types the display below inside F[z']: at a < 0 the factor (z' + c̃)^{a}
would leave F[z'^{±1}] ((z' + c̃) is not a unit there); a < 0 never occurs at
the e_read = 1 stages where D.10 is invoked.
The anchored display: on the stratum R(f) = z^{a}·(z − c̃)^{μ}·h(z) (ψ'-order μ,
anchor a, ψ' ∤ z^a h): the primed polynomial is (z' + c̃)^{a}·z'^{μ}·h(z' + c̃),
so the canonical anchored data transform by

  a' = μ,   R'_anch(z') = (z' + c̃)^{a}·h(z' + c̃)

— the pass-7-CONFIRMED target, derived (the (z+c̃)^a anchor-monomial factor of
B2⁵.2 EXPLICIT; the probed a = 0 gates see the trivial factor; Case J pinned
a > 0 at level 1).

**RS-landing, two-sided.** The stratum (ord_{ψ'} R(f) = μ, anchor a) and the
landing cylinder (primed side-digit pattern per the display) correspond under
the D.8 argument at ψ' (g = 1): ord_{z − c̃} = least minimizing Φ'-slot, both
directions; the inverse move Φ̂ = Φ' + t is again a g = 1 recentering (center
−c̃ in the primed frame), giving the backward containment; the global bijection
(Fact B) closes Θ(stratum) = cylinder exactly. ∎

**TRANS-RS (the recentered stage package, item by item).** S1: same valuation
(v = v'). S2: K1(Φ'/v) — displayed above. S3: slot decomposition at Φ' — from
K1. S4: coefficient-side units := the SAME representatives (p and the previous
keys, including Φ — all still of degree < deg Φ' = deg Φ̂); the current key's
class in_v(Φ') adjoined SEPARATELY, per D.0 [pass-11 finding 6]; T unchanged
(coefficient-pure — untouched by the move); L'₀ = F[z'^{±1}] by D.7(vi)'s
argument at Φ' (same coefficient field F — no residue growth at a recentering).
S5: coefficient digits LITERALLY unchanged (same C, same v, same T; position 0).
S6: the same 𝒜 and realizers. The next read in the primed frame has a full
stage. ∎

### D.11 The per-move ledger corollary (the endpoint)

Each move (increment: f ↦ its Φ̂-development; recentering: re-development at Φ')
is an affine unitriangular bijection of the full coefficient space commuting
with reduction mod p^N (Fact A/B — accepted §0). By D.8/D.10 its restriction to
the descend stratum is a bijection ONTO the landing cylinder. Both loci are,
at every level N above the largest constrained height + 1, finite unions of
digit cylinders: the stratum by its defining side-digit conditions, the cylinder
by the (BOX)/(VERTEX)/(TRANSPORT) pattern — the translation between residual
conditions and digit conditions is the unitriangular digit-equation
translation of D.3(e)(ii) — scalar-group-agnostic [rev D⁸] — with the
transported point masses entering
through D.8's triangular bijection — deterministic and invertible, so the
pinned-digit count is unchanged. Hence for every such N:

  #{ f mod p^N in the stratum } = #{ development data mod p^N in the cylinder }
  = p^{nN} · vol(cylinder)   — for ALPHABET-ADMISSIBLE cylinders (every pinned
  value inside its height's attainable alphabet 𝔸(γ); landing cylinders of
  actual strata are always admissible — D.8 transports pins from actual loci);
  an INADMISSIBLE pin makes the locus EMPTY (mass 0, not the product) — the
  statement is typed on admissible cylinders and this dichotomy is exhaustive
  [rev D¹¹d, the quantifier fix],

in ONE unit system [rev D¹⁰, pass-19 finding]: p^{nN} is the full coefficient-box
cardinality at level N, and vol(cylinder) := the product, over the cylinder's
unitriangular digit equations (D.3(e)(ii)), of (the pinned digit's ATTAINABLE
alphabet size |𝔸(γ)| — D.3(e)(ii)'s per-height quantity, = the full field
size only above the clean thresholds)^{−1} [rev D¹¹c] — each equation pins
exactly one digit coordinate as a function of
strictly earlier ones, so each contributes its factor independently of the
others' values — the admissibility typing above is exactly the condition that
every pinned value is attained; for LANDING CYLINDERS OF ACTUAL STRATA this
holds because D.8 transports pins from actual loci, and for any other cylinder
the dichotomy applies (admissible: the product; inadmissible: empty, mass 0)
[rev D¹¹d]; the TRANSPORTED digits are included on the same footing (their
equations have deterministic right-hand sides — the "point mass" of earlier
sections IS the factor such an equation contributes, identical in form to any
other pinned digit's). The first equality is the global bijection; the second
is the digit-by-digit count of a unitriangular system,
and the move is MEASURE-EXACT stratum → cylinder. EMPIRICS FOOTING [rev
D¹¹c]: the sealed census gates always computed masses from the ACTUAL digit
inventories (member-by-member counts of realized loci), so the attainable
product is what the seals pin — e.g. Case L's exact masses (β-cells 1/16,
all 9 joint (d,u)-cells 1/64, over 10⁶ members); no seal ever certified the
uniform field-alphabet display at a shallow height. Statement scope: ONE move.
The composition of moves along a read history is §C — deliberately NOT claimed
here (pass-8 gap 7).

### D.12 Evidence perimeter and the Case-K obligation

Census-pinned (all sealed, all exact): level-1 landings at μ ∈ {2, 3}, flanked
