## §C REV 2 — the composition theorem, incremental form (supersedes rev 1 wholesale)

*(2026-07-26. Rebuilt per the pass-1 findings: lift-carrying histories; the
global coordinate order + the unitriangular transport lemma C.0.5; the
incremental decomposition C.1 with the conditional hinge; the cylinder-valued
induction with the cumulative state cylinder [rev 9; formerly the pin-set];
typed Z and N; the uniformity claim
deferred to the assembly. Consumes only accepted machinery.)*

### C.0 Coordinates, order, digit systems, histories

**The global order.** The coefficient box's BASE coordinates are pairs
c = (ℓ, i): ℓ = the p-adic level, i = the coefficient index. Order:
(ℓ, i) ≺ (ℓ', i') iff ℓ < ℓ', or ℓ = ℓ' and i > i' (within a level, from the
TOP coefficient down — Fact A's elimination order).

**Digit systems.** A digit system E is a finite set of equations, each of the
form x_c = φ(x_{c'} : c' ≺ c) with the LEADING coordinates c pairwise distinct;
pins(E) := the set of leading coordinates; vol(E) := ∏_{c ∈ pins(E)}
(alphabet of c)^{−1} — presentation data of the EXPLICIT system E; §C's
definitions below consume pin data only through locus statistics (pin COUNT
= codimension, free/determined status — PIN-WELLDEF, C.0.5) [rev 9]. Its
solution set is a "cylinder". All pattern data below
are FIXED tuples (with nonzero entries where the polygon conventions require),
so strata are pure equation systems — no inequations occur. BLOCK CONVENTION
[C-pass-2 finding 7]: a digit equation over a residue field F of degree d
(over F_p) is ALWAYS read as its base equations under the fixed per-position
residue bases of D.3(e) — pins are base coordinates throughout, and each
F-digit contributes the factor |𝔸|⁻¹ of its ATTAINABLE alphabet 𝔸 at its
height (= p^{−d} above the clean threshold, smaller sub-blocks at shallow
heights, per D.3(e)(ii)'s rev-D¹¹c attainable accounting — which is what the
sealed censuses always measured). ROOT NODE [finding 5]: every history
begins with ν₀ whose system E(ν₀) := (the initial cluster conditions) ∪ (the
root read's side pattern) — D.11 applies to the root read by D.4's ROOT case,
and E(ν₀)'s volume factors are included in the product. EMPTY HISTORY
[finding 8]: T(∅, Z) := Z.

**Histories carry lifts [pass-1 finding 1]; uniform root indexing [C-pass-3
finding 3].** A read history H = (ν₀, ν₁, …, ν_k), where ν₀ is the ROOT READ
(its system E(ν₀) := the initial cluster conditions ∪ the root side pattern;
D.4's ROOT case supplies its (I-aug); D.11 applies to it as the first move)
and each subsequent ν_i:
each ν_i carries its species and its NODE DATUM, displayed [rev 12 —
fresh-Fable Finding 2: the former pointer "as in rev 1" dangled at the
excised body; fields named as in §B2-DEF — the fields L11/§D4-R consume]:
  ν_i = ( SPECIES: increment (e_i·g_i > 1) or recentering (e_i = g_i = 1);
          SHAPE DATA: the stage tuple (e_i, h_i, g_i, μ_i) — stride, rise,
            residual degree, multiplicity; the read side's per-slot SLOPE
            is −h_i/e_i, (I-aug)-steep per D.4 — and the anchor a_i
            (D.8's ANCHORING: recorded stratum data, = ord_z R(f) on the
            locus);
          SIDE DATA: the endpoints s₀'_i and j*_i := s₀'_i + w'_i (raw
            width w'_i), the stride positions p_j (D.3(c)) — hence the
            digit count — and the SIDE HEIGHT u*_i := the right-endpoint
            height (on-lattice; at an ADJACENT read, u*_i = the standing
            window-vertex height — the side hangs from the pinned
            vertex). These determine the read-side LINE new_i on the
            standing window's slots (root: the initial polygon): slope
            −h_i/e_i per slot through (j*_i, u*_i);
          DIGITS: the FIXED side-pattern digit tuple (d_j) (values in
            D.3(e)'s alphabets, nonzero where the polygon conventions
            require — the lead in particular);
          ψ_i: the residual factor descended at (monic irreducible over
            F_i, deg ψ_i = g_i, ord_{ψ_i} R_anch(ν_i) = μ_i) — the
            branch choice, not recoverable from the digits alone;
          for RECENTERINGS, the LIFT t_i itself (canonical choice: the
            (S6b) offset-P-lift realizer; any admissible lift is allowed
            and recorded) ).
DERIVED from the tuple (functions of node data, no further fields):
R_anch(ν_i) and vtx(ν_i) (below); the read's TOTAL SIDE WEIGHT
(augmented scale) γ_i := e_i·u*_i + j*_i·h_i (= e_i·new_i(j) + j·h_i at
every side slot j — the argument of D.3(c)'s stride positions p_j(γ_i)
in R_anch(ν_i)) and the NORMALIZATION EXPONENT m̂_i := −t^B_i·h_i·g_i
(D.5: R(Φ̂_i) = z^{m̂_i}·ψ_i, with (s^B_i, t^B_i) the read's Bézout pair
e_i·s^B_i + h_i·t^B_i = 1 — this is §B2-DEF D.5's Bézout pair (s, t)
(cf. lines ~2026/2472), superscripted ᴮ here to DISAMBIGUATE from the
recentering LIFT field t_i of the tuple above [rev 14 — pass-15 F1: the
former (s_i, t_i) collided symbol-for-symbol with the lift t_i]; a
function of (e_i, h_i) only. At a RECENTERING e_i = g_i = 1, so the
Bézout convention e_i·s^B_i + h_i·t^B_i = 1 forces s^B_i = 1, t^B_i = 0
(exactly D.5's e = 1 base case P2), whence m̂_i = 0 — this vanishing is
BY the e = g = 1 convention, NOT because the lift t_i is zero (t_i is an
admissible recentering lift, generically nonzero) — so vtx(ν_i)'s unit
exponent z̄^{−μ_i·m̂_i} reads off m̂_i unambiguously) [rev 13 — pass-14
F2: both consumed below, formerly unlisted];
F_{i+1} = F_i[z]/(ψ_i), the frame data
(D_{i+1}, stretches/STR, κ), the line staircases and the floor F_i of
C.1/C.1.5. Then every Θ_i, Ψ_H := Θ_k∘…∘Θ₀ (the ROOT
MOVE INCLUDED — C-pass-5 finding 1, fixed in the live definition), and
S(H, Z) are determined. LIFT-TRANSLATION REMARK (scope-corrected at rev 3, C-pass-2 finding 6): two
admissible lifts for one node differ by an element of weight > the side value;
the conjugating translation is a digit-level unitriangular bijection carrying
the one joint stratum onto the joint stratum of the history whose DOWNSTREAM
node data (centers, patterns, lifts, terminal system) are correspondingly
transported — equal masses for the TRANSPORTED pair. With downstream data held
literally fixed, no correspondence is claimed (the deeper digits genuinely
differ, per D.10's scope clause); Theorem C applies to each lift-carrying
history separately.

**REALIZABLE histories [rev 12, (SAE) added as the THIRD data condition —
fresh-Fable Finding 1, FABLE_VERIFY_C_2026-07-26.md; rev 8, (HV) as a
predicate on node data, per the derivation S7; rev 7, C-pass-6 finding 4].** Define, as a component of
node ν_i's data, its ANCHORED RESIDUAL POLYNOMIAL R_anch(ν_i) :=
Σ_j d_j·z^{p_j(γ_i)−a_i} ∈ F_i[z] (d_j = the FIXED side-pattern digits,
p_j = D.3(c)'s stride positions, a_i = the anchor) — pattern data only —
and its TRANSPORTED VERTEX VALUE
  vtx(ν_i) := z̄^{−μ_i·m̂_i}·z̄^{a_i}·((R_anch(ν_i)/ψ_i^{μ_i}) mod ψ_i)
              ∈ F_{i+1}ˣ.
[Why well-defined: the descend stratum fixes the FULL side-digit tuple
(d_j), which determines R_anch(ν_i) completely; D.8's split R_anch =
(ψ-normalized pattern)·h̃ is a factorization OF this fixed polynomial, so
(R_anch/ψ^μ) mod ψ — hence vtx — is pattern data, not f-data.]
A lift-carrying history is REALIZABLE iff at every read ν_{i+1} (i ≥ 0)
its data satisfy the THREE side conditions below ((NA)/(HV) are the two
that C.1(ii) isolates; (SAE) is independent of both — rev 12). All are
equations/inequalities of NO E — they constrain the history's DATA,
never f. Notation, all node data: old := ν_i's read-side line new_i
extended to the window slots [0, μ_i] (the standing frame's box line —
at i = 0 the root read's line, D.4's ROOT case); new := ν_{i+1}'s side
line new_{i+1} extended likewise (slope −h_{i+1}/e_{i+1} per slot
through the recorded side height).
  (NA) at a NON-ADJACENT read (right endpoint j* := s₀'+w' < μ_i), the
       pinned old vertex lies strictly above the extended new line:
       old(μ_i) > new(μ_i);
  (HV) at an ADJACENT read ν_{i+1}: (pattern lead of ν_{i+1}) = vtx(ν_i);
  (SAE) SPAN-ENTRY INEQUALITY [rev 12 — the third condition]: the read
       side lies strictly above the old box line at every span slot
       below the window vertex, and matches it at the vertex when the
       span reaches it:
         new(j) > old(j)    for every span slot j ∈ [s₀', j*], j < μ_i;
         new(μ_i) = old(μ_i)    when j* = μ_i (adjacent).
       Since new − old is affine with strictly negative slope ((I-aug),
       data), the condition reduces to a single endpoint check: at an
       ADJACENT read it is automatic given the vertex-anchored side
       height (strict decrease from the μ_i-tie); at a NON-ADJACENT read
       it is exactly new(j*) > old(j*). Checkable from the history's
       node data alone — both lines are node data; no stratum enters.
Both sides of (HV) are explicit functions of node data, as are (NA)'s
and (SAE)'s two lines: realizability is a predicate on the history
alone. [Exercised live in the derivation's S5.1/S5.2 census: vtx(ν₀) = 1
forced the vertex digit on all 600k members, 0 violations.] A history
failing any of the three conditions has EMPTY joint stratum (the
classifier never emits it: it reads actual hull sides, which satisfy all
three on a nonempty locus by D.8); Theorem C's quantifier ranges over
REALIZABLE histories only.
TRANSITION-ADMISSIBLE (definition) [rev 13 — pass-14 F1]: a read ν_{i+1}
appended to a prefix (ν₀, …, ν_i) is TRANSITION-ADMISSIBLE iff the
extended prefix through ν_{i+1} satisfies the three data conditions
(NA)+(HV)+(SAE) at the appended read ν_{i+1} — so appending a
transition-admissible read to a realizable prefix yields a realizable
extended prefix. The per-step statements (C.1, C.1.0(b)(iii), C.1.5)
HYPOTHESIZE this of their appended reads; without the hypothesis their
conclusions are FALSE on the F-EMPTY data of the remark below.
REMARK (why (SAE) is independent — the certified empty-stratum instance)
[rev 12; FABLE_VERIFY_C_2026-07-26.md secs. 1–2, config F-EMPTY]. n = 4,
p = 2: root recentering with old = (3, 2, 1, 0) on μ₀ = 3; read side
[0, 2], (e₁, h₁) = (1, 3), new = (7, 4, 1, −2). (I-aug): 3 > 1 OK; (NA):
new(3) = −2 < 0 = old(3) OK; (HV) vacuous — yet new(2) = 1 = old(2):
(SAE) fails at the endpoint. The endpoint value digit sits AT the floor,
C.1's selection ("heights STRICTLY ABOVE F_i") classes it E_inh, and its
nonzero demand meets a coordinate zero-pinned by (BOX): the joint
stratum is EMPTY while the nominal fresh volume is 2⁻⁶ — Theorem C(b)
would predict 62500 members of the 4·10⁶ census; the actual count is 0
(numerically certified). Without (SAE), C.1(i), C.1.5(1), and Theorem
C(a)/(b) are false as quantified; nothing in (I-aug)+(NA)+(HV) implies
(SAE).

### C.0.5 The unitriangular transport lemma

**Lemma.** Each move map Θ (the Φ̂-development; the Φ'-re-development; their
inverses; and the lift-translations above) satisfies, modulo every p^N:
(Θx)_c = x_c + φ_c(x_{c'} : c' ≺ c) — unitriangular for ≺, in both directions.
*Proof.* Fact A's division accounting: each output coefficient equals the
corresponding input coefficient plus a ℤ[key-coefficients]-combination of
strictly higher-index coefficients (same or lower level via the monic unit,
strictly higher index when the level ties — exactly ≺-earlier); iterated
divisions compose; inverses of unitriangular maps are unitriangular;
reductions commute (Fact A/B). ∎
**Corollary (pin transport).** For a digit system E, the pullback Θ*(E) is a
digit system with THE SAME pins(E) and vol(E): substitute and re-solve each
equation for its leading coordinate — the unitriangular correction φ_c moves
only ≺-earlier terms to the right side. PIN-WELLDEF [rev 6, from the
derivation note S2.3]: a digit-cylinder LOCUS determines its pin set (two
digit systems with the same solution set have equal pin sets — solve in the
≺-order and compare free/determined coordinates level by level), so
pins(landing cylinder) = pins(stratum) exactly, and all freshness bookkeeping
may run stratum-side. SCOPE [rev 9]: §C consumes PIN-WELLDEF as a
CARDINALITY-AND-STATUS tool only — a digit-cylinder locus owns its pin COUNT
(its codimension: mass p^{nN−K} ⟺ K pins) and each coordinate's
free/determined status (solve in ≺-order, as in the proof just given); no
pin ADDRESS is consumed by any definition below. Pins live on the BASE
coordinates and heights are base levels, so these statistics are
frame-independent. [Pass-1 findings 5, 8, 9 groundwork.]

### C.1 The incremental decomposition (freshness, cumulative form)

Fix a history prefix (ν₀, …, ν_i). Its cumulative object is the STATE
CYLINDER Σ_i := Ψ_i(S(prefix_i)) — the prefix locus in frame-(i+1)
coordinates, the (ZC) object of C.1.5 (Σ_{−1} := the full box;
Σ_i = Θ_i(Σ_{i−1} ∩ stratum(ν_i)) by D.8/D.10), carried with its MASS
p^{nN}·∏_{m≤i} vol_nom(E_fresh(ν_m)) ((ZC-c)). No cumulative pin-SET is
defined [rev 9 — supersedes the rev-5 Π-recursion]. Where earlier text needs
"the number of pins", it is the locus's CODIMENSION via PIN-WELLDEF:
|Π_i| := Σ_{m≤i} m*(ν_m) (m* = C.1(ii)'s D.11-nominal fresh digit count) —
notation for a locus statistic (a digit-cylinder locus of mass p^{nN−K} has
exactly K pins), never a set of addresses.

**Lemma C.1.0 (window refinement) [C-pass-2 finding 1].** (a) BLOCKS: monic
division has a deterministic shape — the Φ̂-development's slot-j coefficient
B_j occupies, as base labels, the index block of degrees [j·deg Φ̂,
(j+1)·deg Φ̂) ∩ [0, n); iterating, every frame's (slot, inner-…) coordinate is
a determined base label, and C.0.5's maps match labels identically. (b)
REGIONS AND NESTING, corrected [rev 6 = the Fable derivation
C10B_DERIVATION_2026-07-26.md, machine-checked on two instances incl.
s₀ > 0; DIVERGENCES from revs 4–5 stated there plainly]:
(i) ν_{i+1}'s clause region is the FULL current window Win_i (frame-(i+1)
slots [0, μ_i]; base span [0, (μ_i+1)·D_{i+1}) ∩ [0, n), ANCHORED AT 0 — the
child window carries no side-offset, contrary to rev 5's (b)(ii), which was
FALSE for s₀ > 0): a hull-side read asserts (α) span clauses on its side's
slots [s₀', s₀'+w'], (β) strictly-above-extended-line clauses at slots < s₀',
and (γ) the same at slots in (s₀'+w', μ_i].
(ii) FACTOR spans nest — μ_{i+1}·D_{i+2} ≤ w'·D_{i+1} ≤ μ_i·D_{i+1} (the
width inequality bounds FACTOR spans, not window spans); Win_{i+1} = the
factor span ∪ its vertex block, and only the vertex block can overhang Win_i
(never at recenterings, where D_{i+2} = D_{i+1}).
(iii) RIM (hypothesis [rev 13 — pass-14 F1 sweep]: every later read
TRANSITION-ADMISSIBLE, C.0 — the clause consumes (SAE) via DOM(2)): no
node's fresh clause content ever constrains a coordinate at
base indices ≥ μ_i·D_{i+1} (the blocks carrying ν_i's transport data) —
every later demand there unfolds, per block,
parallel to ν_i's line (slope w(Φ̂_i) per slot) from an entry value ≤ old_i,
hence is implied (I-aug + DOM(2)'s span-entry inequality, sourced from
C.0's (SAE); the derivation's rim lemma).
(c) LEVELS, with the conversion displayed [rev 4, C-pass-3 finding 4]: for a
fixed frame and slot label, the digit at stage height h is the base digit at
level ℓ = (h − off)/str, where str := the product of the history's stretches
e₀·e₁·… down to that frame and off := the slot-determined offset (both
integers, str ≥ 1, fixed by the label — D.3(e)'s literal coordinates give
exactly this affine correspondence). Two heights AT THE SAME LABEL share
(str, off), so h < h' ⟺ ℓ < ℓ': strict stage-scale inequalities transfer to
strict base-level inequalities wherever C.1 compares constrained
coordinates at a common label. ∎

**Scale declaration [rev 8, per the derivation S7 — reconciling C.1.5 with
C.1.0(c)].** All of §C's height comparisons are in absolute v_p-scale; a
base coordinate (b, l) has height l + off(b) with off(b) the inner-tower
offset at CURRENT stage weights (κ_r = h_r/(e_r·STR_r)); C.1.0(c)'s
(h − off)/str is this map in stage scale. A key's κ is UPDATED at its
augmentation — stale-κ arithmetic (a prior frame's κ applied to a later
frame's demands) is the source of the rev-7 'displaced address' artifact:
this declaration DIAGNOSES it (derivation note S5); no collision occurs in
current-frame ht-arithmetic.

**Lemma DOM (interior floor domination) [rev 10 — pass-9 gap 1: the
(INV-a) domination displayed; supersedes the bare citation to the C10B
machine-check, which stands as instance evidence (S6.1/S6.2 floors)].**
For every realizable prefix (ν₀, …, ν_i): at every base index b of the
factor interior [0, μ_i·D_{i+1}), line_i(b) ≥ line_m(b) for all m < i
(line_m := node m's read-side line as an AFFINE function of the base
index, absolute scale); consequently the floor is the current staircase:
F_i(b) = old_i(slot(b)) there.
*Proof — induction on i; each step is three displayed inequalities plus
one identification.* Work at the ν_{i+1} read in frame-(i+1) slots
j ∈ [0, μ_i], all heights in the current stage scale over w (absolute
statements follow by C.1.0(c): divide by the frame's accumulated stretch
str; strictness and equality transfer). Write old(j) := line_i(j) — ν_i's
read-side line extended to [0, μ_i], NODE DATA, C.0's notation [rev 12;
on the stratum this is D.8's box line through the pinned vertex,
old(μ_i) = w(B_{μ_i}), by (PU) one step down — commentary, not consumed
by (1)–(3)] — and
new(j) := ν_{i+1}'s side line extended to [0, μ_i], per-slot drop
h_{i+1}/e_{i+1}; j* := s₀'+w' (the side's right endpoint).
(1) MONOTONE DIFFERENCE [source: D.4's chords]. (new − old)(j) is affine
in j — and in b, by affine interpolation between slot positions — with
slope −(h_{i+1}/e_{i+1} − w(Φ̂_i)) < 0: (I-aug) at the ν_{i+1} read —
D.4's chord convexity on D.8's box (every chord to the pinned vertex
descends more steeply than w(Φ̂_i); hull descent slopes increase
leftward; the read side is such a chord) — gives h_{i+1}/e_{i+1} >
w(Φ̂_i). So new − old strictly decreases.
(2) SPAN-ENTRY INEQUALITY [source: C.0's (SAE) — realizability; rev 12.
The former source "D.8's box" was CIRCULAR: (BOX)/(VERTEX) are
f-statements, valid only on a NONEMPTY stratum, cited inside a lemma
quantified over realizable (data-only) prefixes — fresh-Fable Finding 1;
on (SAE)-violating data the lemma's STATEMENT is false, so the condition
must sit in the quantifier]. (SAE) gives directly: new(j) > old(j) at
every span slot j < μ_i, and new(μ_i) = old(μ_i) when j* = μ_i. Hence
new(j*) − old(j*) ≥ 0, and with (1): new ≥ old at every b ≤
j*·D_{i+1}, strictly left of j*. Beyond the window vertex the gap
reverses: for j > μ_i, old(j) − new(j) = (old − new)(μ_i) +
(j − μ_i)·(h_{i+1}/e_{i+1} − w(Φ̂_i)) > 0, using (old − new)(μ_i) ≥ 0
(adjacent: equality — (SAE)'s vertex anchor; non-adjacent: strict by
(NA) — realizability).
(3) WIDTH CONFINEMENT [source: D.3(d) via C.1.0(b)(ii)'s (W)].
μ_{i+1}·D_{i+2} = μ_{i+1}e_{i+1}g_{i+1}·D_{i+1} ≤ w'·D_{i+1} ≤
j*·D_{i+1} ≤ μ_i·D_{i+1} (ψ_{i+1}^{μ_{i+1}} divides the residual factor,
of degree w'/e_{i+1}): the NEXT factor interior lies inside both the
(2)-dominated range and the current interior; chaining (3) down the
history, every node's clause region contains the current interior.
(PU) PARALLEL UNFOLDING [sources: D.7's w'-formula; D.5's equal-weights
design; D.8 at the landing]. The next frame's box line IS the read line:
old_{i+1} = new as affine base-index functions. INTERCEPT: w'(f) :=
min_j(e_{i+1}·w(B_j) + j·h_{i+1}) (D.7, on the frame-(i+1) development)
= e_{i+1}·new(0), because every development slot lies weakly above the
new line — window slots by the stratum's (α)/(β)/(γ) clauses, transport
slots j > μ_i by w(B_j) ≥ old(j) (K1(Φ̂_i/w)) and old(j) > new(j) ((2))
— with equality attained at the span endpoints; the new stage scale is
e_{i+1}·str, so the absolute intercepts agree. SLOPE: w'(Φ̂_{i+1}) =
e_{i+1}h_{i+1}g_{i+1} (D.5's equal weights at the new stage) per
frame-(i+2) slot of D_{i+2} = e_{i+1}g_{i+1}·D_{i+1} base indices =
h_{i+1}/e_{i+1} per D_{i+1} — the read side's own per-index slope. Equal
intercept, equal slope. The ROOT instance (frame-1 box line = the root
read line) is the same computation at D.4's ROOT case. [At a RECENTERING
ν_{i+1}: e_{i+1} = g_{i+1} = 1, D_{i+2} = D_{i+1}; (1)–(2) run verbatim
against the standing frame's box, (3) is trivial, and (PU) holds with
D.10's RS-landing box (v = v'; the primed key's weight is the consumed
side's value — D.10/D.4), by the same support argument.]
CLOSE. Base i = 0 [completed at rev 11, pass-10 gap 1 — the spaced form of
the sentence had dodged the first guarded edit; root display now inserted at
the live site]: there is no earlier LINE (no m < 0), but Z₀ also carries the
CLUSTER ZEROS (every box coordinate pinned at level 0 — floor height 1). On
the window's interior slots j < μ₀ the root line has height ≥ λ₀·1 ≥ 1 (D.4's
ROOT case: every side slope ≥ 1), so the line dominates the cluster floor at
every box coordinate of the window (the monic lead is not a box coordinate):
F₀(b) = old₀(slot(b)) on the interior, with the cluster zeros absorbed — the
induction starts from the staircase form. Step, at any b in the next interior and any
m ≤ i: line_{i+1}(b) = new(b) [definition] ≥ line_i(b) [(1)+(2), since
b ≤ j*·D_{i+1} by (3); old = line_i by DEFINITION at rev 12, with (PU)
one step down — the root included — identifying it with the frame's box
line on the stratum] ≥ line_m(b) [induction hypothesis; b is in the current
interior by (3)]. FLOOR FORM: (ZC-a)'s F compares STEP functions — node
m's staircase top at b is line_m at the LEFT EDGE of b's frame-(m+1)
block. Blocks nest (D_{m+1} | D_{i+2}), so the coarser edge x ≤ the
finer edge y ≤ b, and line_{i+1}(x) ≥ line_{i+1}(y) [lines descend]
≥ line_m(y) [the line comparison, at y ≤ b in the next interior]: the
current staircase dominates every earlier one per index, and F_{i+1} =
old_{i+1}'s staircase (= line_{i+1}'s, by (PU)) on [0, μ_{i+1}·D_{i+2}). ∎

**Lemma LST (level-set scale-typing) [rev 9 — the pass-7 finding-5 residue,
now displayed; derivation C15 S5/S7].** Let (ν₀, …, ν_i) be any realizable
prefix (increments and recenterings in any interleaving), in frame-(i+1)
tower coordinates. Each inner tower level r carries the weight
κ_r := h_r/(e_r·STR_r) FIXED AT THE READ THAT AUGMENTED that level's key
(the increment read on that key's window; recenterings create no inner
level and change no κ — the CURRENT key never enters off); as declared,
ht(b, l) := l + off(b), off(b) := Σ_r innerslot_r(b)·κ_r. Then:
 (i) TYPING: ht(b, l) is the K1-chain weight of the coordinate's basis
   monomial p^l·∏_r Φ̂_r^{innerslot_r(b)}; hence for every current-window
   slot coefficient B, w(B) = min{ht(b, l) : (b, l) ∈ blk(B), y_{(b,l)} ≠ 0}
   (the iterated slot-minimum), and for every γ ∈ ℚ the weight-γ initial
   form in_γ(B) is a function of exactly the LEVEL SET
   L_γ(B) := {(b, l) ∈ blk(B) : ht(b, l) = γ} (typed in Lemma TYP below:
   it lives in D.3(e)(i)'s graded piece at γ's stage index);
 (ii) FLOOR CONSTANCY ON THE INTERIOR: on the factor interior — where all
   fresh content lives (C.1's rim bullet) — F_i(b) = old_i(slot(b)):
   ht-constant on each current block;
 (iii) SELECTION: for a factor-interior slot coefficient B with
   γ' > old_i(slot(B)), the ENTIRE level set L_{γ'}(B) lies strictly above
   the floor downset {ht ≤ F_i} — after ARBITRARY augmentations, the
   absolute-height formula selects the whole weight-γ' level set.
*Proof — induction on i (the per-key κ update discipline made explicit).*
BASE i = 0: the frame-1 tower coordinate (b, l) has monomial p^l·x^b; the
root read fixes w(x) = h₀/e₀ = κ₀ (STR₀ = 1), and K1(Φ̂₁/w) evaluates w of
any inner coefficient as the slot minimum of l + b·κ₀ over its nonzero
digits — ht IS the chain weight, and the ht-grading is the grading whose
pieces D.3(e)(i) describes: (i) holds. STEP at an INCREMENT ν_{i+1} (stage
data (e_{i+1}, h_{i+1}), e·g > 1): the frame-(i+2) tower adds exactly ONE
inner level — the just-read key Φ̂_{i+1} — at weight κ_{i+1} :=
h_{i+1}/(e_{i+1}·STR_{i+1}), the v_p-slope of the side ν_{i+1} READ; this
is the moment that κ is SET (beforehand only ν_i's landing bound
w(Φ̂_{i+1}) ≥ old_i(μ_i) is known, and computing with THAT value is the
stale-κ artifact, derivation S5). TRANS (D.7) builds the augmented
valuation with the read side value at the new key and touches no inner
weight (keys r ≤ i are never re-read); so off gains the single term
innerslot_{i+1}·κ_{i+1}, and ht = l + off is again the K1-chain weight of
the monomial: (i) persists. STEP at a RECENTERING ν_{i+1} (e_read = 1,
degree unchanged): the key is REPLACED (Φ̂ ↦ Φ̂ + t), the tower depth and
every inner κ_r are unchanged (the replaced key is the CURRENT one, absent
from off); the primed frame is reached by a C.0.5 unitriangular map at
fixed labels (C.1.0(a)) and D.10's frame valuation v = v' carries the
primed development: (i) transfers verbatim. (ii): on the factor interior
the current line dominates every earlier staircase (Lemma DOM's floor
form, displayed above; the C10B machine-check stands as instance
evidence only), so
F_i(b) = old_i(slot(b)) there: one ht-value per current slot, constant per
block. (iii): by (i) every coordinate of L_{γ'}(B) has ht = γ' >
old_i(slot(B)) = F_i on blk(B) (block-constant by (ii)); the floor downset
on the block is {ht ≤ F_i}: disjoint. ∎
[Exercised: S5.1's level sets {(0,5),(1,4)} at γ' = 5 and {(2,3),(3,2)} at
γ' = 11/2 (κ₁ = 5/2 CURRENT — the stale value 2 is the rev-7 artifact);
S5.2's designed p = 3 collision dissolves; S5.3 re-derives every recorded
label verdict. C.1.5's proof consumes (iii) at its strip and value steps.]

**Lemma TYP (the degree-γ' initial form, typed) [rev 10 — pass-9 gap 2].**
Fix a frame with accumulated stretch str, a current-window slot
coefficient B ∈ Ĉ, and γ' ∈ ℚ; set δ' := str·γ' — the stage-scale index
of the absolute height γ' (C.1.0(c)/the scale declaration: the two
scales differ by the factor str at fixed labels; equalities and strict
inequalities transfer).
(a) THE OBJECT. The weight-γ' level-set content of B lives in
gr^{Ĉ}_{δ'} — D.3(e)(i)'s graded piece at δ'; the absolute scale
relabels the piece index, nothing else. Display: B is literally its
tower expansion B = Σ_c y′_c·m_c (D.3(e)(ii)/Fact A: the iterated
development down to ℤ_p-digit coordinates; m_c := the basis monomial
p^l·∏_r Φ̂_r^{innerslot_r(b)} of c = (b, l), y′_c := the unit lift of
the digit y_c), and each nonzero summand has weight ht(c) (LST(i): ht is
the K1-chain weight; unit lifts of F_Qˣ multiply pieces integrally,
D.3(e)(i)). On any locus where blk(B)'s coordinates of ht < γ' vanish
(the literal downset — C.1.5), every surviving summand has weight ≥ γ',
so w(B) ≥ γ' and, taking classes in the piece (the class map is additive
and kills weight > δ' — D.1),
  in_{γ'}(B) = Σ_{c ∈ L_{γ'}(B)} y′_c·in(m_c)  ∈ gr^{Ĉ}_{δ'}:
an ADDITIVE function (per-digit 𝔽_p-linear on the base coordinates after
C.0's block expansion; NO F_Q-module structure on individual base digits is
claimed — the F_Q-structure enters only through the target alphabet, which is
all that TYP(b)'s additive-surjection/kernel-coset count consumes; retyped at
rev 11, pass-10 gap 2) of exactly the level-set
digits (y_c)_{c ∈ L_{γ'}(B)} and of no other coordinate. If γ' is
achievable at no coordinate of the block (off the lattice), L_{γ'}(B) =
∅ and the piece receives nothing — matching C.1's on-lattice inventory.
CONSEQUENCE: gr^{Ĉ}_{δ'} is the F_Q-span of {in(m_c) : c ∈ L_{γ'}(B)}
(any B of weight δ' has, by LST(i)'s min formula, no nonzero digit below
ht γ', so the display applies to it unconditionally).
(b) USE 1 — C.1.5's counting consumes exactly this object. The fresh
value clause at exact valuation γ' is R_{δ'}(in_{γ'}(B)) = v, R_{δ'} :=
D.3(e)(i)'s piece map at δ'. The composite (level-set assignments) → L₀,
(y_c)_c ↦ Σ_c y′_c·R_{δ'}(in(m_c)), is additive with image = the
additive span of the slot images (by (a)'s CONSEQUENCE) = the digit's
ALPHABET (D.3(e)(i); D.11's per-digit convention — same piece, same map;
in particular the emitted pattern value v lies in the image). An
additive surjection onto its image has equal-size fibers (kernel
cosets), so the clause cuts exactly (alphabet)^{−1} among the level-set
assignments; R_{δ'}'s injectivity on the piece (D.3(e)(i)) is what makes
|alphabet| = |piece| the D.11-nominal cardinality. No basis, address, or
leading-coordinate claim enters.
(c) USE 2 — LST's selection consumes exactly this object. LST(iii)
selects the weight-γ' content of a factor-interior B with γ' >
old_i(slot(B)): by (a) that content is a function of exactly L_{γ'}(B),
so selecting it selects the WHOLE level set, which LST(iii) places
strictly above the floor downset — the typed object's coordinates are
free on Σ_i, and it is the object (b) counts. §C consumes "degree-γ'
initial form" in no other way. ∎

**Lemma C.1 (rev 9 — clause-species selection + frame-free implication;
supersedes the rev-3 pin-membership rule, per the pass-8 disposition).**
Let the prefix through ν_i be realizable (C.0) and let ν_{i+1} be any
TRANSITION-ADMISSIBLE read (C.0's definition: the extended prefix
through ν_{i+1} satisfies (NA)+(HV)+(SAE)) of the stage that node ν_i's
landing produces [rev 13 — pass-14 F1: (i)'s proof cites
(SAE)/(NA)/(HV); without the hypothesis, F-EMPTY refutes (i) as
quantified]; type
its stratum conditions as per-slot VALUATION CLAUSES carrying their digit
realizations (as in (i) below). The selection rule is a definition on NODE
DATA + the state cylinder: freshness is the (α)/(β)/(γ) GEOMETRIC INVENTORY
relative to Σ_i's floor F_i ((ZC-a) — itself node data: the pointwise max
of the history's line staircases over their clause regions; on the factor
interior F_i IS ν_i's old staircase, ht-constant per block by LST(ii)):

  E_fresh(ν_{i+1}) := the clause content of E(ν_{i+1}) whose heights lie
    STRICTLY ABOVE F_i — per species: the (α) interior strips and on-lattice
    value digits; the (β) strips; the (γ) strips left of the crossing slot
    j×; the non-adjacent endpoint's strip and value digit;
  E_inh(ν_{i+1}) := the rest (all content at heights ≤ F_i: below-old
    zeros, (γ) at/beyond j×, the adjacent endpoint, every rim demand);
  E_fresh(ν₀) := E(ν₀) (empty floor);
  vol_nom(E_fresh) := the D.11-nominal product of the inventory's per-digit
    alphabet-cardinality factors.

No pin membership enters the selection — "above F_i" is checked from node
data by LST ht-arithmetic, clause by clause. Then:
(i) IMPLICATION via the CROSSING ANALYSIS [rev 7, transferred from the
    derivation C10B_DERIVATION_2026-07-26.md S3.3; supersedes the rev-4
    full-window hull domination, which is FALSE beyond the crossing slot] —
    the stratum's conditions are typed as per-slot VALUATION CLAUSES (each
    "the slot coefficient's weight ≥ the line at this slot" / "= with digit
    value v"), each clause carrying its digit realization (the finite set of
    digit equations of D.3(e)(ii)). CLAIM: on Σ_i, every clause of
    E_inh(ν_{i+1}) — the at-or-below-floor content — is implied. PROOF, per
    clause species against Λ(ν_i) = (BOX)+(VERTEX)+(TRANSPORT), all in frame
    i+1 (old(j) := ν_i's line extended to [0, μ_i]; new(j) := ν_{i+1}'s line
    L' extended likewise; by (I-aug) the new line is strictly STEEPER):
    (α) SPAN slots j ∈ [s₀', s₀'+w']: THE LINE COMPARISON IS (SAE)
    [rev 12 — fresh-Fable Finding 1; supersedes the rev-10 endpoint
    chain, which DERIVED the comparison from "D.8's box" through the
    stratum's own endpoint clauses (w(B_{s₀'}) = new(s₀'), w(B_{j*}) =
    new(j*)) — f-statements presupposing a point of the joint locus:
    CIRCULAR for empty strata]. By C.0's (SAE) (realizability — a DATA
    condition, cited, not derived): new(j) > old(j) at every span slot
    j < μ_i, and new(μ_i) = old(μ_i) in the adjacent case j* = μ_i.
    Equivalently, in the crossing-slot view of
    the hull geometry (= DOM(1)+(2)): new − old strictly decreases
    ((I-aug)) and is ≥ 0 at j*, so the single crossing j× lies weakly
    right of the span. The clause content at levels ≤ old(j) is (BOX)'s
    zeros — implied clause by clause; the forced zeros at off-lattice
    span slots (level new(j) > old(j)) are fresh strip content,
    inventoried in (ii).
    (β) LEFT slots j < s₀': new is steeper and new(s₀') > old(s₀')
    ((SAE); s₀' < μ_i), so
    new(j) > old(j) STRICTLY at every left slot — nothing is implied here;
    the whole strip (old(j), new(j)] is fresh (see (ii)).
    (γ) RIGHT slots j ∈ (s₀'+w', μ_i]: new descends strictly faster than old
    from new(s₀'+w') ≥ old(s₀'+w'), so there is a SINGLE CROSSING slot j×:
    for j < j× the strip (old(j), new(j)] is fresh; for j ≥ j×, new(j) ≤
    old(j) and the clause asserts a weaker-or-equal bound than (BOX) —
    implied. At j = μ_i the clause "strictly above new(μ_i)", read against
    the pinned NONZERO value at old(μ_i), is the arithmetic condition
    old(μ_i) > new(μ_i) on the HISTORY DATA alone (C.0's (NA),
    realizability) — it contributes no f-pin.
    The old-vertex value clause (adjacent case) is Λ(ν_i)'s (VERTEX) clause
    itself. The joint system retains only the FRESH clauses' realizations —
    no coordinate is ever constrained twice, and no equation identity is claimed
    anywhere. NO pointwise domination of the new line over the old on the
    FULL window is claimed (false at (γ) slots ≥ j×); the implication is
    species-by-species.
(ii) E_fresh's realizations constrain only coordinates FREE on Σ_i —
    strictly above the floor at factor-interior indices, hence outside
    Z_i's downset and off DIG_i's rim leads (C.1.5's (ZC); at rev 9
    freshness IS the species inventory, so no disjointness postulate
    remains) — and E_fresh is exactly
    the geometrically new content, per CLAUSE SPECIES [rev 6]: interior-span
    strips/digits and the right-endpoint hinge as before; (β)-strips (slots
    left of the side, the strip (old line, extended new line]) are ALWAYS
    fresh — real fresh digits revs 4–5 omitted; (γ)-strips are fresh iff non-adjacent,
    up to the crossing slot j×, implied at and beyond it; three purely-DATA
    side conditions [rev 12] ((SAE)'s span-entry inequality; old(μ_i) >
    new(μ_i) non-adjacent; hinge value matches) exclude
    unrealizable histories and are equations of NO E. Fresh realizations
    always constrain base indices < μ_i·D_{i+1}, at levels strictly above
    the cumulative floor
    (the derivation's downset invariant); fresh digit COUNTS m* are
    D.11-nominal. No
    base ADDRESS of a digit equation is ever claimed or used [rev 8]:
    C.1.5 counts fresh value digits by the injectivity of the piece map

(No standalone-volume factorization of E(ν_{i+1}) is claimed [rev 4: the
former (iii) dropped, C-pass-3 finding 5]: the theorem consumes only the
fresh volumes.)

*Proof of the case analysis supporting (ii)* [rev 7 — transferred from the

### C.1.5 The per-step conditional exactness lemma (supersedes the
displaced-carry codimension lemma; rev 8 — the derivation
C15_DERIVATION_2026-07-26.md S4–S6, closing C-pass-7 findings 1–3)

**Scale.** All heights are absolute v_p-scale rationals. The current
frame's tower coordinate (b, l) has height ht(b,l) := l + off(b),
off(b) := Σ_r innerslot_r(b)·κ_r over the coordinate's INNER tower levels,
κ_r := h_r/(e_r·STR_r) with the CURRENT stage weights (a key's κ changes
at its augmentation). C.1.0(c)'s conversion is this same map in stage
scale (off_stage = STR·off, division by STR); strictness transfers.

**Downsets are literal.** Since w is defined through the K1 chain as an
iterated slot-minimum, a valuation clause "w(B) ≥ β" is, in the frame's
own tower coordinates, the literal zero system {y_(b,l) = 0 :
ht(b,l) < β, b ∈ blk(B)} (plus ht = β when β is demanded strictly). No
carries occur in valuation content.

**State cylinder.** Σ_i := Ψ_i(S(prefix_i)), the prefix locus in
frame-(i+1) coordinates; Σ_i = Θ_i(Σ_{i−1} ∩ stratum(ν_i)) (D.8/D.10).

**Invariant (ZC), carried along the history.**
 (ZC-a) Σ_i = locus(Z_i ∪ DIG_i), with F_i := the pointwise max of the
   history's line staircases over their clause regions (in ht-terms,
   constant per block: F_i(b) = max_m old_m(slot_m(b))) and Z_i := the
   literal zero downset under F_i with SPECIES-CORRECT TOPS [rev 12 —
   fresh-Fable Finding 5: the former bare "top F_i", read literally,
   zeroed the rim VALUE levels, contradicting DIG_i]: at indices whose
   governing clause is a strict bound ((BOX)-type — every factor-interior
   index) the top is INCLUSIVE, zeros at ht ≤ F_i(b); at the
   value-carrying rim indices (vertex/transport blocks) it is EXCLUSIVE,
   zeros at ht < F_i(b) only — the ht = F_i level set there belongs to
   DIG_i, which pins NONZERO values. (All of §C's Z_i consumers are
   interior-only, where the inclusive top is exactly the (BOX)-strict
   content; the initialization below and both instance scripts use this
   downset.);
 (ZC-b) DIG_i is unitriangular with each equation solving for the level
   set of one rim block (base indices ≥ μ_i·D_{i+1}) — LEADS AT RIM;
   non-lead arguments unrestricted;
 (ZC-c) mass(Σ_i) = p^{nN}·∏_{m≤i} vol_nom(E_fresh(ν_m)) for every
   N ≥ 1 + the largest constrained level.
Initialization: at i = 0, (ZC) is D.8 at the root read (D.4 ROOT case):
Z_0 = cluster zeros + (BOX), DIG_0 = (VERTEX)+(TRANSPORT) (leads = rim
level sets, right sides = history constants), (ZC-c) = D.11, one move.

**Lemma.** Let the prefix through ν_i satisfy (ZC) and be realizable
(C.0's (NA), (HV) — the latter as defined in C.0 via vtx(ν_i) — and
(SAE) [rev 12]). Let
ν_{i+1} be a TRANSITION-ADMISSIBLE read (C.0: the extended prefix
through ν_{i+1} satisfies (NA)+(HV)+(SAE) at ν_{i+1} [rev 13 — pass-14
F1: prefix realizability alone does not constrain the appended read —
appending F-EMPTY to the realizable root refuted (1) as formerly
quantified]) of the frame-(i+1) window with fresh nominal digit
count m* (C.1(ii)'s inventory). Then
 (1) mass(Σ_i ∩ stratum(ν_{i+1})) = mass(Σ_i)·vol_nom(E_fresh(ν_{i+1}));
 (2) (ZC) holds for Σ_{i+1}.
*Proof.* (1): Solve Σ_i's system in ≺-order (graph over free
coordinates). E_inh is implied (C.1(i)). Fresh STRIP clauses are literal
zeros at coordinates with ht strictly above F_i at factor-interior
indices: not in Z_i, not leads of DIG_i ((ZC-b): rim), pairwise distinct —
each cuts exactly p^{−1} on the graph. Fresh VALUE clauses pin the first
residual digit of a slot coefficient B at its exact valuation γ' =
new(slot): on the joint zero locus the digit is R_{δ'}(in_{γ'}(B)) with
in_{γ'}(B) ∈ gr^{Ĉ}_{δ'}, δ' := str·γ' — Lemma TYP(a)'s typed object,
an additive function of exactly the weight-γ' level set (graded content;
carries are (TRANSPORT) = inherited); by TYP(b) — the composite
assignment map is additive onto the digit's alphabet (= D.3(e)(i)'s
piece-map image, D.11's convention), so its fibers are equal-size kernel
cosets — the equation cuts exactly (alphabet)^{−1}; level sets sit strictly above F_i (γ' > old, in
ht-terms uniform across the block — LST(iii)), are interior, and are pairwise
disjoint (one per slot): the factors multiply. The adjacent hinge's lead
clause degenerates to the data identity (HV) — pins nothing (or empties
the stratum: excluded by realizability). (2): mass by (1) + bijectivity;
split by the next interior I' = [0, μ_{i+1}D_{i+2}) ⊆ [0, w'D_{i+1}):
on I' the old zeros are implied by ν_{i+1}'s clauses (new ≥ F_i there —
Lemma DOM's step displays (1)+(2) with its floor form; same frame, same
coefficients, downset containment); off I' the
survivors — old zeros beyond the crossing slot (indices ≥ j×D_{i+1} ≥
w'D_{i+1} ≥ μ_{i+1}D_{i+2}: j× lies weakly right of the span (C.1(i)),
so j× ≥ j* ≥ w', and DOM(3) confines; the chain is WEAK throughout —
equality at the adjacent s₀' = 0 boundary, where j× = μ_i = w' and the
former strict > was false [rev 12, fresh-Fable Finding 3]) and DIG_i
(leads ≥ μ_iD_{i+1}) — have all leads at future-rim
indices, and Θ_{i+1}-pushforward preserves leads (C.0.5). ν_{i+1}'s own
content re-presents as Λ(ν_{i+1}) (D.8/D.10 two-sidedness): (BOX) = the
new literal core on I', (VERTEX)+(TRANSPORT) = new rim equations with
history-constant right sides. ∎
*(What C.2 consumes: (ZC-c)'s mass recursion. Pin-set language: by
PIN-WELLDEF the locus Σ_i has a well-defined pin count, = |Π_i| =
Σ_{m≤i} m*(ν_m) (C.1's codimension statistic); no pin ADDRESS is defined
or needed.)*

### C.2 Theorem C (rev 2 — the joint-history ledger, incremental volumes)

**The transported system.** T(H, Z) := E(ν₀) ∪ Θ₀*( E_fresh(ν₁) ∪ Θ₁*( …
E_fresh(ν_k) ∪ Θ_k*(Z) … ) ), with E_fresh per C.1's species selection
(E_fresh(ν₀) := E(ν₀); the root's volume factors are in the product).
ADMISSIBLE Z [rev 9 — a LOCUS condition, superseding the pin-set typing]:
any digit system each of whose equations SOLVES FOR coordinates that are
FREE on Σ_k's locus — i.e. NOT among its determined coordinates [rev 12 —
fresh-Fable Finding 4: the former "free-coordinate complement" parsed as
the complement OF the free set, the opposite of the intent] (non-lead
arguments unrestricted). Free/determined status is locus-determined (PIN-WELLDEF's
≺-order argument), and the condition is CHECKABLE from node data + (ZC):
Σ_k's determined coordinates are exactly its zero downset with (ZC-a)'s
SPECIES-CORRECT TOPS [rev 13 — pass-14 F3: the former uniform ≤ formula
re-included the rim value levels, contradicting (ZC-a)]:
{(b, l) : ht(b, l) ≤ F_k(b)} at strict-bound/(BOX)-type indices (every
factor-interior index), {(b, l) : ht(b, l) < F_k(b)} at the
value-carrying rim indices — plus DIG_k's lead level sets (the rim
ht = F_k level sets, at the history's read values) — all computed from
node data by LST ht-arithmetic. N(H, Z) := 1 + the largest BASE level occurring among the
coordinates of T(H, Z)'s equations, and N := 1 when T(H, Z) has no
equations [rev 4] —
finite: every node's pattern is finitely many digit equations (D.3(e)(ii))
and Z is finite [finding 8 repaired].

**Theorem C.** For every REALIZABLE lift-carrying history H (C.0) and
admissible Z:
(a) [rev 9 — stated on LOCI] S(H, Z) is exactly the solution locus of
    T(H, Z), and S(H, Z) = Ψ_H^{−1}(Σ_k ∩ locus(Z)); Ψ_H carries it
    BIJECTIVELY onto the image locus Σ_k ∩ locus(Z), with equal mass (the
    move maps are global digit-level bijections, Fact A/B). Pin data enter
    only as CARDINALITIES: by PIN-WELLDEF both loci have pin count
    |Π_k| + |pins(Z)| = Σ_{m≤k} m*(ν_m) + |pins(Z)| (the codimension read
    off the mass). No pushforward pin SET is asserted, and no clause-wise
    landing selection is used or needed [rev 5, C-pass-4 finding 3:
    Λ_fresh deleted — D.11's landing content stays per-move where it is
    proved; the assembly consumes only the count and the free-coordinate
    complement];
(b) for every N ≥ N(H, Z):
    #{ f mod p^N ∈ S(H, Z) } = p^{nN} · ∏_{i=0}^{k} vol(E_fresh(ν_i)) · vol(Z).

[The rev-8 pin-count READING of (a) is superseded: absorbed into (a)'s
rev-9 locus statement and the rev-9 admissibility definition above.]

*Proof.* Induction along the prefix with the state cylinder Σ_i and the
invariant (ZC) of C.1.5, initialized at the root (C.1.5's base = D.8 +
D.11 at ν₀). Step: C.1.5(1) multiplies the mass by exactly
vol_nom(E_fresh(ν_{i+1})); C.1.5(2) re-establishes (ZC); the move maps
are global digit-level bijections (Fact A/B), so S(prefix_{i+1}) has the
same mass as Σ_{i+1}. After ν_k, append Z: its equations solve only for
coordinates free on Σ_k's solved graph (admissibility), so Z cuts exactly
vol(Z). This
gives (b): #{f mod p^N ∈ S(H, Z)} = p^{nN}·∏_{i=0}^{k}
vol(E_fresh(ν_i))·vol(Z) for every N ≥ N(H, Z). For (a): S(H, Z) is the
solution set of T(H, Z) (C.1(i): the inherited clauses are implied
stepwise, so intersecting with each stratum adds exactly the fresh
realizations); its pin count is Σ m* + |pins(Z)| (PIN-WELLDEF + the mass
count: a digit-cylinder locus of mass p^{nN}·p^{−K} has exactly K pins);
Ψ_H maps it bijectively onto the image locus Σ_k ∩ locus(Z) with equal
mass (the move maps are global digit-level bijections; Fact A/B), whence
equal pin count (PIN-WELLDEF + equal mass). ∎

family of histories realizing a factorization type (and any p-uniformity

## §C PASS RECORD (pass 3 on rev 3: 3 critical, 4 gaps) and the rev-4 repairs (in place)

Repairs 6 (lift scope) and 7 (block convention) GENUINE at pass 3; the kernel
still open. Rev-4 repairs, each with its displayed mechanism:
1. CRIT (nesting unproved) — THE WIDTH INEQUALITY, displayed: at an increment
   with parent side width w (Φ-slots) and residual multiplicity μ at ψ of
   degree g on side (e, h): deg R_S = w/e ≥ μ·g ⟹ μ·deg Φ̂ = μ·e·g·deg Φ ≤
   w·deg Φ — the child continuing window's base span sits inside the parent
   side's base span BECAUSE the ψ^μ-factor's degree cannot exceed the
   residual's. Recenterings: degree unchanged and μ' ≤ μ (the descend factor
   divides z'^μ·h'). C.1.0(b) restated with this inequality as its content.
2. CRIT (implication asserted) — THE HULL-CONVEXITY IMPLICATION, displayed:
   Λ(ν_i) places the child polygon's points weakly above the old side line
   (strictly for j < μ); the lower hull of points above a line lies above the
   line (any affine minorant below all points is below the hull); the next
   side is a hull side, so the new side line ≥ the old line pointwise on the
   window. Hence every below-old-line prefix CLAUSE of stratum(ν_{i+1}) — the
   clauses are typed as per-slot VALUATION BOUNDS with digit realizations —
   is a weaker-or-equal bound than Λ(ν_i)'s corresponding clause: implied,
   clause by clause, no equation identity needed. The joint system keeps only
   the fresh clauses' realizations; no pin ever carries two equations.
3. CRIT (root indexing) — uniform indexing: H = (ν₀, …, ν_k), ν₀ the root
   read; Π₀ := pins(E(ν₀)); T starts at E(ν₀); the product runs i = 0 … k;
   Z-admissibility against the full Π_k.
4. GAP (scale conversion) — displayed: per frame and slot, base level ℓ =
   (stage height h − offset(slot))/stretch, stretch = the product of the
   history's e's, offset slot-determined; strict comparisons at the SAME label
   share the same affine map, so strictness transfers.
5. GAP (C.1(iii) false as written) — DROPPED: the theorem consumes only fresh
