### H.1 Theorem XHD-w — the fixed-height monomial weights

**Statement (= V.0 (XHD-w), with the honest quantifier split displayed).**
For every step datum (m, o) with source cell α, target cell β, and height
point h of that step's parameter space (H.0 typing; the step's parameter
space includes the source-anchored gap data its inventory reads — H.1.2),
there is a fixed-height weight monomial
    g^{α,β}_{m,o}(h) = q^{−E(h)},
E an integer affine form in h with positive weights on every coordinate,
equal to the per-assignment C.1.5 fresh-volume ratio at height h. Proved
here in two layers:
  (XHD-w-FIX) — at ANY ONE fixed realizable prefix satisfying (ZC) and
    reaching a representative of α, every fresh assignment realizing
    (m, o, β) at h has conditional mass exactly q^{−E(h)}, with E as
    displayed in H.1.1–H.1.2. STATUS: DERIVED (from C.1.5(1), C.1(ii),
    TYP(b), LST, DOM — the walk below). This strictly contains §V V.0's
    (U-A) and adds the affine-exponent form (M4a's clause).
  (XHD-w-UNIF) — the SAME value at every representative of α under every
    admissible history: this is exactly (XHD-u)(u-R)'s mass face,
    DERIVED at H.4.1 (it is a §C theorem after all, via Lemma DOM's
    floor form — the mass face only; the count face stays fenced).
Deep-regime caveat, displayed (not hidden): ONE affine E per
(m, o, α, β) is asserted on each XHD-d listed component; on the DEEP
components (all step heights above the clean thresholds of §B2-DEF
D.7(viii)/(S6b)) the affine form is the same across components and the
regime split is invisible. At shallow-height boundary components the
attainable-alphabet accounting (D.3(e)(ii), rev D¹¹c) replaces some
alphabet factors by sub-block cardinalities, changing E's constant term
component-by-component: E is ONE affine form PER LISTED COMPONENT. V.0's
display "E affine in h" is consumed by §V only through per-component sums
(XHD-s; V.0's own disjoint-component convention), so the per-component
form is spec-sufficient; the alignment note is recorded at H.6 as a
declared reading, not a weakening.

**H.1.1 The walk: the per-step conditional mass IS the displayed alphabet
product.** Fix a realizable prefix through ν_i satisfying (ZC) and a
transition-admissible read ν_{i+1} of species m with outcome/target data
(o, β) at height point h. C.1.5(1) gives
    mass(Σ_i ∩ stratum(ν_{i+1})) = mass(Σ_i)·vol_nom(E_fresh(ν_{i+1})),
i.e. the conditional mass of ONE fresh assignment is vol_nom(E_fresh) —
by C.1(ii)'s selection, the D.11-nominal product over the fresh
inventory. Walk the inventory per clause species (C.1's (α)/(β)/(γ)
census, all against the floor F_i, which on the factor interior IS ν_i's
old staircase — Lemma DOM's floor form):
  (w-1) STRIP PINS. At each window slot j, the fresh zeros fill the strip
    (old(j), new(j)) at (α)/(β) slots and (old(j), new(j)] left of the
    crossing slot j× at (γ) slots, plus the non-adjacent endpoint strip.
    Each strip level is one literal base-digit zero equation per base
    coordinate of its block (C.0's block convention), cutting exactly
    p^{−1} on Σ_i's solved graph (C.1.5(1)'s strip step — not in Z_i,
    not a DIG_i lead, pairwise distinct). Count: #(strip levels in the
    label's lattice) × (block dimension) — by C.1.0(c)/LST, the level
    count between two lines at a fixed label is the integer affine form
    str·(new(j) − old(j)) up to the fixed lattice offset of the label.
  (w-2) VALUE DIGITS. One on-lattice value digit per span slot (and the
    non-adjacent endpoint): the clause pins R_{δ'}(in_{γ'}(B)) = v via
    TYP(b)'s additive composite; equal-size kernel cosets cut exactly
    |𝔸|^{−1}, 𝔸 = the ATTAINABLE alphabet at that height (D.3(e)(i)
    piece-map image; = p^d above the clean threshold, the attainable
    sub-block below it — D.3(e)(ii) rev D¹¹c).
  (w-3) NOTHING ELSE. E_inh is implied (C.1(i) — the crossing analysis);
    the adjacent hinge degenerates to the data identity (HV), pinning
    nothing; (NA)/(SAE) are equations of NO E; the rim receives no fresh
    pin (C.1's rim bullet). So the fresh-volume ratio is EXACTLY
    ∏(w-1) p^{−1} · ∏(w-2) |𝔸|^{−1} — the displayed alphabet product,
    at q = p (base theory; [2r] REL.1's retype carries the O_δ version
    at q = p^δ, consumed only through [2r], not claimed here). ∎

**H.1.2 The exponent E is affine with positive weights (M4a's clause,
proved for the step form).** Collect exponents from H.1.1:
    E(h) = Σ_{slots j} d_j·[str·(new(j) − old(j))]_lattice + Σ_{digits} a_dig(h),
d_j = the block dimension at slot j (template data), a_dig = log_p|𝔸|.
(i) AFFINE: new(j) − old(j) is affine in the two reads' height
coordinates (H.0's derived-line convention: both lines are affine in h
with template-fixed rational coefficients; str clears denominators —
C.1.0(c) makes the lattice level count exactly this integer form plus a
label-fixed offset that is CONSTANT on each congruence class of h, hence
constant per XHD-d listed component). a_dig is the constant d above the
clean threshold; on a boundary component it is the component-constant
attainable sub-block dimension. Hence E = one integer affine form per
listed component. (ii) POSITIVE WEIGHTS, per coordinate:
  — in the step's own rise h_{i+1}: DOM(1)'s monotone difference gives
    slope −(h_{i+1}/e_{i+1} − w(Φ̂_i)) < 0 for new − old, so raising
    h_{i+1} at fixed earlier data raises new − old at every slot strictly
    left of the anchor and adds ≥ 1 strip level per e_{i+1}·STR steps at
    ≥ 1 slots: the h_{i+1}-coefficient of E is a positive integer
    (a per-slot count of lattice crossings, ≥ 1 by the span being
    nonempty — the side has width ≥ 1);
  — in the non-adjacent û*_{i+1}: raising û* lifts the whole new line at
    fixed slope, growing every span strip by one level per lattice step:
    coefficient ≥ #span slots ≥ 1. Source-side coordinates enter E only
    through old(j) with NEGATIVE sign in new − old; those coordinates
    belong to the PREVIOUS step's weight (in a template product each
    height coordinate is charged at its own read; the one-step parameter
    space of g^{α,β}_{m,o} is the step's own (h-1)/(h-2) coordinates
    PLUS the source-anchored gap coordinates already fixed by α's
    retained stage data — H.4.1's inventory-through-state clause is what
    makes this split well-typed). On each listed component the total
    template exponent Σ_i E_i(h) has positive weight in EVERY coordinate
    of h: each h-coordinate's positive charge at its own read strictly
    dominates the bounded negative transfer to the NEXT read's strip
    count, because new − old telescopes — the strip content between
    consecutive staircases counts each lattice level at most once across
    the template (Lemma DOM: staircases are monotone per index; the
    cumulative floor only rises). DISPLAY DUTY: per template family, the
    positivity margin is displayed with the component list (H.2); the
    H-n3 gate censuses one family's E against direct conditional masses.
STATUS: DERIVED per component, with the per-family positivity display a
standing per-template duty (mechanical, template-computable), exercised
at the gate. This is M4a's "vol-exponents affine with positive weights"
clause in its XHD-w step form.

**H.1.3 AUD-E — the exponent-map audit (the plan's CTS.2 gcd concern,
addressed for the WEIGHT side).** The K1-J2 concern: congruence factors
such as gcd(a, q^g − 1) entering through norm/trace/ratio/collision
exponent maps, unaudited. Audit of H.1.1's factor roster: every factor
of vol_nom is an ADDITIVE cardinality — (w-1) literal zero equations on
base digit coordinates (factor p^{−1} each), (w-2) kernel-coset counts
of TYP(b)'s ADDITIVE surjections onto piece-map images (factor a power
of p, the attainable sub-block dimension; TYP's rev-11 typing: no
F_Q-module structure on individual base digits is claimed, so no
multiplicative structure is available to smuggle an exponent map in).
NO factor is the image size of a multiplicative map: the maps where
gcd(a, q^g − 1) lives — the anchor twist z̄^{a−μm̂}, norms/ratios in
cell-membership predicates, collision strata — occur in this framework
only as CELL-MEMBERSHIP and COUNT data, i.e. in the T-layer ((iv)-POLY's
per-entry closed forms, [1v]'s EXPONENT-MAP AUDIT duty, gate-sealed
there), which XHD-w conditions on through (m, o, α, β) but never weighs.
CONCLUSION: E's coefficients are lattice-level counts × block dimensions
— staircase arithmetic only; no congruence factor gcd(a, q^g − 1) can
enter E. The multiplicative congruences instead surface in XHD-d's
constraint census as the (HV)-hinge congruence family — where they are
displayed and dealt with, not hidden (H.2.1 (C3)). STATUS: DERIVED
(a closure property of the displayed factor roster). ∎

### H.2 Theorem XHD-d — exact semilinear height domains, listed partition

**Statement (= V.0 (XHD-d) verbatim).** For every finite template γ
(length 1 included), the exact realizable height domain
H(γ) ⊆ ∏_i ℕ^{d_i} is semilinear with bases/periods EXPLICITLY LISTED
per template and components PAIRWISE DISJOINT (the rev-3 partition
convention), two-sidedly exact: (no orphan) T_γ(x, h) > 0 for some
representative x ⟹ h ∈ H(γ); (no stray) h ∈ H(γ) ⟹ T_γ(x, h) > 0 for
EVERY representative x of α.

**H.2.1 The constraint census — what cuts H(γ).** By §C C.0, a history is
realizable iff its data satisfy (NA)+(HV)+(SAE) at every read; γ fixes
all node data except h. The h-constraints, per read, are exactly:
  (C1) LATTICE/COPRIMALITY CONGRUENCES: gcd(h_i, e_i) = 1 — a finite
    union of residue classes mod e_i (h-1); û*_i's on-lattice condition
    is absorbed by the (h-2) scaling; the label-lattice offsets of
    H.1.2(i) impose congruences with template-fixed moduli (products of
    e_r·STR_r — LST's κ denominators).
  (C2) AFFINE STRICT INEQUALITIES, integer after clearing (H.0):
    (I-aug) at each read: h_{i+1}/e_{i+1} > w(Φ̂_i) — TRIANGULAR: a
      lower bound on h_{i+1}, affine in earlier coordinates with
      positive coefficients (w(Φ̂_i) = e_i·h_i·g_i, stage scale);
    (SAE) at each non-adjacent read: new(j*) > old(j*) — a LOWER bound
      on û*_{i+1}, affine in earlier coordinates (adjacent: automatic,
      C.0 — the vertex-anchored equality plus (I-aug) strictness);
    (NA) at each non-adjacent read: old(μ_i) > new(μ_i), i.e.
      û*-scaled: u*_{i+1} − (μ_i − j*)·h_{i+1}/e_{i+1} < old(μ_i) — an
      UPPER bound coupling û*_{i+1} to h_{i+1} and earlier coordinates.
  (C3) THE (HV)-HINGE FAMILY, in the TWISTED frame: at an adjacent read,
    (HV) demands pattern lead = vtx(ν_i), and vtx carries the unit
    factor z̄^{−μ_i·m̂_i} with m̂_i = −t^B_i·h_i·g_i — h_i-dependent
    through a finite-order unit (ord(z̄) | q^{g_i} − 1): in the FIXED
    frame this is a p-DEPENDENT congruence on h_i (the K1-J2 family).
    CLAIM (consumed as the M4b-T INPUT, tagged): with cells in the
    twisted VAL.1 frame (anchor-aligned — probe-forced, part of VAL.1's
    statement), the hinge demand is carried by the twist and imposes NO
    constraint on h beyond (C1)-(C2): the required lead value moves WITH
    the twist, and its attainability is height-uniform above the clean
    threshold. This is precisely M4b-T's normal-form conclusion (the
    anchor monomial is the ONLY height-dependent factor at fixed
    template) — H.5.1's kernel; every use below carries the tag
    [GIVEN M4b-T-AUD]. Also t^B_i (Bézout, e_i·s^B + h_i·t^B = 1)
    depends on h_i only through h_i mod e_i — constant per (C1) class,
    so the twist EXPONENT is component-constant: the tag is needed only
    for the audit ("nothing else depends on h"), not for well-typedness.
So: H(γ) = {h ∈ ∏ℕ^{d_i} : (C1) ∧ (C2)} [GIVEN M4b-T-AUD for the claim
that (C3) adds nothing]. Both directions of this set identity are proved
at H.2.3; first the LIST.

**H.2.2 Lemma H-LIST (the explicit listed partition — the offset/slack
staircase construction; discharges M4a-X Route A's affine-semigroup duty
in the listed form).** Let S ⊆ ℕ^D be cut by (a) finitely many
congruences h_j ≡ c mod M_j (unions of classes allowed), and (b) affine
integer inequalities in the TEMPLATE ORDER: for each j, lower bounds
h_j ≥ φ(h_{<j}) + 1 and optionally upper bounds h_j ≤ ψ(h_{<j}, and
later-read lower coordinates per (NA)'s coupling, reordered so every
bound cites only earlier coordinates — the census (C2) admits this
order: order each read's h before its û*, whence (I-aug)/(SAE) are lower
bounds and (NA) is an upper bound on û* citing h of the same read) — φ,
ψ affine with rational template coefficients. Then S is a finite DISJOINT
union of linear sets with computable bases/periods. CONSTRUCTION+PROOF:
(1) Let M := lcm(all M_j, all denominator-clearing factors). Partition
ℕ^D by the residue vector r = h mod M (finitely many cells; distinct
cells disjoint — the partition convention is automatic). (2) On one cell,
each bound's fractional data are constant: the least admissible value of
h_j above its lower bound in its residue class is φ^↑_r(h_{<j}) := an
affine form with the SAME linear part as φ and an r-determined constant.
(3) Coordinates with lower bounds only: substitute h_j = φ^↑_r(h_{<j}) +
M·n_j, n_j ∈ ℕ. The composite map n ↦ h(r, n) is affine (composition of
affine forms at fixed r) and injective (triangular, diagonal M > 0), so
S ∩ cell(r) = L(h(r, 0); {v_j(r)}) — base and periods READ OFF the
substitution (v_j := ∂h/∂n_j, triangular integer vectors). (4) A
coordinate with lower AND upper bound (the (NA)-coupled û*): write
û* = lower^↑_r + M·t with the SLACK t ≥ 0 constrained by t ≤ c_r(h) with
c_r affine, positive weight in the same read's h (DOM(1): the (NA) gap
grows with h_{i+1} — coefficient (μ_i − j*)/e_{i+1} > 0 non-adjacent).
The under-staircase set {(h, t) : 0 ≤ t ≤ c_r(h)} splits by t's residue
mod the h-coefficient of c_r into finitely many linear sets with periods
{(unit_h, 0-shift), (unit_h, full-shift)} — the displayed two-period
normal form (worked instance: {0 ≤ t ≤ 2x} = L((0,0);{(1,0),(1,2)}) ⊔
L((1,1);{(1,0),(1,2)})); compose with (3)'s substitution. (5) The
feasible cells (those whose base point satisfies all strict bounds —
decidable affinely) are the LIST; empty cells are dropped. Disjointness:
(1)'s residue partition refined by (4)'s slack residues. ∎
This is the construction the plan's REV-5 affine-semigroup duty demanded
(finitely many translation orbits, boundary orbits included — here:
finitely many residue-and-slack cells, each ONE orbit of its own period
lattice); composition-compatibility of the translations is M4b-T's
clause (H.5.1), not re-proved here. STATUS: DERIVED (self-contained
integer arithmetic; per-template lists mechanical from the census).

**H.2.3 Two-sided exactness.**
  (NO ORPHAN) — DERIVED. If T_γ(x, h) > 0, some realized branch carries
    the template at h; the classifier reads actual hull sides, and on a
    nonempty locus D.8 gives (NA)+(HV)+(SAE) at every read (§C C.0's
    converse direction), (I-aug) by D.4's induction, the lattice
    conditions by B1.1's value group: h satisfies (C1)+(C2), hence lies
    in the listed set (H-LIST covers {(C1) ∧ (C2)} exactly). ∎
  (NO STRAY), deep components — DERIVED [GIVEN M4b-T-AUD at hinges].
    Let h satisfy (C1)+(C2) with all step heights above the clean
    thresholds, and let x be ANY representative of α reached by an
    admissible prefix satisfying (ZC). Build the witness chain read by
    read: at each step the demanded fresh content is (w-1) strips (zero
    digits — always assignable) plus (w-2) value digits, whose demanded
    values are: free alphabet choices (span digits — nonempty alphabets,
    = full F-blocks above the clean threshold), the NONZERO lead/endpoint
    demands (satisfiable: |𝔸| = p^d ≥ 2 and 𝔸 ∋ nonzero values — (S6b)
    full scalars above the key weight, the OFFSET P-LIFT with the clean
    threshold, §B2-DEF D.7(viii); 1 ∈ O suffices per D¹¹'s consumer
    audit), and the hinge lead (determined, nonzero in F_{i+1}ˣ,
    attainable in the twisted frame [GIVEN M4b-T-AUD]). Realizability of
    the assembled history holds by construction ((C2) IS the data
    predicate), so C.1.5(1) applies stepwise from Σ(x's prefix): each
    step multiplies by a POSITIVE ratio, whence T_γ(x, h) ≥ 1. ∎
  (NO STRAY), boundary components — KERNEL XHD-d-EX(∂), FENCED. At
    components with some height at or below a clean threshold, the
    attainable sub-block 𝔸 may exclude a demanded nonzero value (a
    stray candidate: listed but unrealizable). DUTY: per template
    family, decide each boundary component by the attainable accounting
    (D.3(e)(ii) rev D¹¹c) and DROP the failing components from the list
    (the list is then exact by the same walk with 𝔸 replaced by its
    attainable form). Named kernel with refuter: gate H-n3's two-sided
    census (H.6) — any orphan or stray at a boundary point indicts
    exactly this kernel. STATUS: FENCED (deep part derived; boundary
    decision procedure displayed, per-family execution open).

### H.3 Theorem XHD-s — per-component geometric sums in ℚ(q)

**Statement (= V.0 (XHD-s) verbatim) and proof.** Per listed linear set
L(h₀; P) of H(γ) (H-LIST), with the component's affine exponent
E_c(h) = E_c(h₀) + ⟨w, h − h₀⟩ (H.1.2):
    Σ_{h ∈ L} ∏_i g_i(h_i) = Σ_{n ∈ ℕ^P} q^{−E_c(h₀) − Σ_v n_v·⟨w, v⟩}
                            = q^{−E_c(h₀)} · ∏_{v ∈ P} (1 − q^{−⟨w, v⟩})^{−1},
using (a) H-LIST's parameterization n ↦ h(r, n) is a BIJECTION onto the
component (injectivity was proved there — the sum factorizes over the
free ℕ^P grid, no point counted twice), and (b) ⟨w, v⟩ > 0 for every
period v: periods are triangular with positive diagonal entry M and
nonnegative entries, and E has positive weight in every coordinate on
the component (H.1.2(ii)), so each ratio is q^{−a}, a ≥ 1. Hence the
component sum ∈ ℚ(q) with geometric denominators (1 − q^{−a}),
convergent at every prime-power evaluation; by the partition convention
the finitely many component sums ADD EXACTLY to the H(γ)-sum, no overlap
terms (V.0's rev-3 convention, which H-LIST satisfies by construction).
STATUS: DERIVED, conditional exactly on its inputs' tags (H.1.2's
per-family positivity display; H.2's [GIVEN M4b-T-AUD] and XHD-d-EX(∂)
where the component list touches boundary components). ∎

### H.4 (XHD-u) and (ENT-U) — the uniformity clauses

**H.4.1 (u-R), MASS FACE — DERIVED (the inventory-through-state
theorem).** Claim: the per-assignment C.1.5 ratio at (m, o, β, h) is the
same at EVERY representative x of α under EVERY admissible history
reaching it. Walk: by H.1.1 the ratio equals the D.11-nominal product
over the fresh inventory, and the inventory is determined by (i) the
floor F on the read region and (ii) the read's own line and lattice data.
For (i): fresh content constrains only base indices < μ_i·D_{i+1} (C.1's
rim bullet — no fresh pin at or beyond the rim), and ON THE FACTOR
INTERIOR the floor IS the current staircase (Lemma DOM's floor form:
F_i(b) = old_i(slot(b)) there) — the deeper history's staircases are
DOMINATED and drop out. The current staircase is a function of the
source stage data (frame, stretches, D, e_i, and the source-anchored
line) — all retained in the cell pair's skeleton/state data plus the
step's height point (H.0/H.1.2's gap-coordinate typing). For (ii): strip
level counts by LST arithmetic (function of the lines and lattices),
alphabet sizes by TYP(b)'s piece images (function of height vs clean
threshold + stage data). No other datum of the history enters any
factor. Hence ratio = function of (α, β, m, o, h) alone. ∎
  (This is the promotion of §V V.0's (U-A) from "one fixed history" to
  the full quantifier, for the MASS face — the promotion is exactly
  Lemma DOM's floor collapse, which V.0 could not cite without owning
  [2b] material. XHD-w-UNIF (H.1) is discharged by this clause.)

**H.4.2 (u-R), COUNT FACE — FENCED.** The claim that T_γ(x, h) depends
on x and its history only through (α, β⃗, m⃗, o⃗, h) is a LUMPABILITY
assertion about counts: it needs every observable each species'
evaluation maps consume to be refined by JREF^src(s) — the PER-SPECIES
OBSERVABLE CHECK of the table build ((iv)-REP's proviso, §V (iv)-REP).
Not derivable from §C (which never compares histories at the count
level). FENCED as kernel U-R-COUNT: = the per-species check executed
with (D2) per skeleton; refuters: the JOINT-probe (§V V.5), gate H-n3's
representative layer (two representatives, same cell, censused counts).

**H.4.3 (u-T) HEIGHT RECTANGULARITY — split verdict.**
  PER COMPONENT — DERIVED [GIVEN M4b-T equivariance with π_v = id,
  H.5.1]: each period v of a listed component is a PATH-LENGTH-NEUTRAL
  translation (it changes no step of the template — periods move heights
  only, H-LIST), so M4b-T's translation bijection carries assignment
  chains at h onto assignment chains at h + v, composing targets with
  the anchor twist; with twisted cells and π_v = id the (m, o, β)-data
  are preserved, so T_γ(x, h) = T_γ(x, h + v): the count is constant on
  each listed component, and every chain at one h has its counterpart at
  every other h of the component — (u-T)'s within-component content.
  CROSS COMPONENT — KERNEL XHD-u-X, FENCED: V.0's display asserts ONE
  h-independent value per step across ALL of H(γ); components differ by
  residue data that no neutral translation connects (t^B mod e changes,
  attainable alphabets change at boundary components), so the
  cross-component equality is a genuinely additional claim. If it FAILS,
  the repair is displayed and local: T becomes component-indexed and K_e
  splits per component class — finitely many extra states, the same
  architecture (recorded so RESUM cannot silently assume the merge, in
  the same spirit as M4b-T's π_v re-indexing clause). Refuter: H-n3's
  count census at multiple h across ≥ 2 components of a second family
  (H.6 — the primary family has one component; the limitation is
  recorded there).

**H.4.4 (ENT-U) — DERIVED [GIVEN M4b-T equivariance with π_v = id, on
the shallow layer].** §V's spec is PER LINEAR SET already: over each
listed component c of H(ε̊), I^ent_c is one polynomial constant in h_ent
on c. The entrance objects are shallow §D4-R shape-prefixes with
designated continuing branch and landing cell ([1]'s catalogue); a
component period is a path-length-neutral translation of the ENTRANCE
heights, and M4b-T's bijection (applied to the shallow cascade, root
included — Lemma DOM's (PU) root instance anchors the frame) carries
realized entrance prefixes at h_ent onto realized entrance prefixes at
h_ent + v preserving the landing cell (twisted frame, π_v = id), hence
I^ent_{ε,β₀}(h_ent) = I^ent_{ε,β₀}(h_ent + v): constancy on c. No
cross-component claim is needed — INIT-RAT (§V (iii)) sums per
component. STATUS: DERIVED conditional on H.5.1's kernel; refuter:
H-n3's entrance layer (one shallow family, counts censused at ≥ 3
heights along one component).

### H.5 M4b-T, the discharge shape, and (iv)-REP

**H.5.1 M4b-T (height-translation equivariance) — statement + partial
walk + kernel.** STATEMENT (plan §3, REV 5 form): translation by a
path-length-neutral lattice vector v (a period of a listed component —
it changes no step of the template) induces an explicit BIJECTION of
linked value systems composing each target with the twist by
anchor^{⟨w,v⟩}; it carries the JREF^src stratification at height point P
to the one at P + v, a priori permuting cell labels by some π_v;
EQUIVARIANCE CLAUSE (to prove): with cells in the twisted frame, π_v
acts trivially on type labels. TARGET CONCLUSION of the NORMAL-FORM
AUDIT (proved, never premised): after auditing ALL residual
coefficients, factor collisions, and lift choices, the anchor monomial
z̄^{a−μm̂} (C.0's display) is the ONLY height-dependent factor at fixed
template. WALKED NOW (the twist-exponent computation, per component):
under h_i ↦ h_i + Δ_i along a period (residues fixed, so t^B_i is
constant — H.2.1 (C3)): m̂_i = −t^B_i·h_i·g_i shifts by −t^B_i·Δ_i·g_i,
so vtx(ν_i)'s unit factor twists by z̄^{+μ_i·t^B_i·g_i·Δ_i}; the total
side weight γ_i = e_i·u*_i + j*_i·h_i shifts affinely, moving D.3(c)'s
stride positions p_j(γ_i) rigidly (the stride pattern is γ-translation-
covariant); D.5's residual computation transports initial forms with the
single monomial factor — the exponent ⟨w, v⟩ is explicit per template.
OPEN (kernel M4b-T-AUD): the ALL-quantifier of the audit (every residual
coefficient, every collision stratum, every lift choice — nothing else
h-dependent) and the π_v = id clause. EVIDENCE (not proof): the Case-J
sealed gate PASS (rev-7 anchor-monomial transport at a > 0, 12 strata,
valuation jumps exact) and the value probe's twisted-cell fiber
constancy at all 24 census q (fixed-frame cells provably char-dependent
— what M4b-T looks like with the twist omitted). REFUTERS: H-n3's
constancy layer (H.6); STATE-probe-2 (plan §4). If π_v ≠ id: RS.0/K_e
re-index by π-orbits and height sums run per orbit — displayed here as
in the plan, so RESUM cannot silently assume triviality. STATUS: FENCED
(exponent walk derived; audit + π_v = kernel).
COMPOSITION COMPATIBILITY (Route A's residual duty): translations along
two periods of one component compose additively in the exponent
